### [1] [Semiclassical state on algebra of observables for de Sitter space and time reversal symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14175)
*Muxin Han,Yichen Hu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解决了 de Sitter 静态贴片全息中观测者时钟代数无法复现半经典体关联函数的问题：通过引入“重时钟态”并考虑双向时钟及时间反演对称，构造出能够匹配体关联函数的 type II₁ 因子的推广结构，并最终用 Brown–Kuchař 尘埃场给出观测者哈密顿量的场论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 de Sitter 静态贴片全息中，把体量子场耦合到观测者的动力学时钟会得到规范不变可观测量的 type II₁ von Neumann 代数 M_+，但其唯一的迹态（tracial state）无法复现半经典的体关联函数。作者希望找到一种能正确恢复半经典体关联的态与代数结构，并给出观测者模型的场论来源。

Method: 作者引入一族“重时钟态”，代表具有大惯性的观测者参考系，并证明在重时钟极限下规范不变着装可观测量的 n 点关联函数复现 de Sitter 时空上的体量子场关联函数。随后推广观测者哈密顿量到 H_obs=|π̂|，同时包含正向（π≥0）与逆向（π<0）时钟，得到 type II₁ 因子 M，并证明其同构于 M_+⊗M_2(C)。在该框架中时间反演对称实现为交换两个时钟取向的反酉算子，进而得到规范不变且时间反演不变的 type II₁ 代数 F。作者将重时钟态构造推广到 M 和 F。最后，通过把 Einstein 引力耦合到 Brown–Kuchař 尘埃场，在 G→0 极限下沿观测者世界线做微扰约化，推导出 H_obs=|π̂|。

Result: 在重时钟极限下，规范不变着装可观测量的 n 点关联函数与 de Sitter 体量子场关联函数一致；含双向时钟的代数 M 保持为 type II₁ 因子且同构于 M_+⊗M_2(C)；时间反演实现为交换时钟取向的反酉算子，给出 type II₁ 代数 F；重时钟态下的 n 点关联同样匹配半经典体关联；观测者哈密顿量 H_obs=|π̂| 可从 Einstein 引力与 Brown–Kuchař 尘埃场耦合在 G→0 极限的微扰约化中导出。

Conclusion: 本文通过重时钟态解决了 de Sitter 静态贴片全息中迹态无法复现半经典关联的问题，并把观测者代数推广到含双向时钟与时间反演对称的情形（M≅M_+⊗M_2(C) 及其时间反演不变子代数 F），证明重时钟态可正确恢复半经典体关联函数。通过 Brown–Kuchař 尘埃场在 G→0 极限下的世界线微扰约化，为观测者哈密顿量 H_obs=|π̂| 提供了场论基础，从而为观测者模型建立了更坚实的理论根基。

Abstract: In de Sitter static patch holography, coupling bulk quantum fields to an observer's dynamical clock yields a type II$_1$ von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}_+$ of gauge-invariant observables. However, the unique tracial state on this algebra fails to reproduce semiclassical bulk correlation functions. We resolve this issue by introducing a family of heavy-clock states, representing an observer reference frame with heavy inertia. We prove that in the heavy-clock limit, $n$-point correlation functions of gauge-invariant dressed observables reproduce the bulk quantum field correlation functions on de Sitter spacetime. Furthermore, we investigate a generalized observer Hamiltonian $\widehat{H}_{\rm obs}=|\widehatπ|$ that incorporates both forward-running ($π\geq 0$) and backward-running ($π<0$) clocks. The resulting von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{M}$ of gauge-invariant observables remains a type~II$_1$ factor and is isomorphic to $\mathcal{M}_+\otimes M_2(\mathbb{C})$, where $M_2(\mathbb{C})$ is the algebra of $2\times2$ complex matrices transforming between the two clock orientations. Time-reversal symmetry is implemented in this setting as an anti-unitary operator that exchanges the two clock orientations, yielding a type~II$_1$ von Neumann algebra $\mathcal{F}$ of gauge-invariant, time-reversal-invariant observables. We generalize the heavy-clock state construction to both $\mathcal{M}$ and $\mathcal{F}$, showing that $n$-point correlation functions calculated in heavy-clock states match the corresponding semiclassical bulk correlation functions. Finally, by coupling Einstein gravity to the Brown--Kuchař dust field, we derive the observer Hamiltonian $\widehat{H}_{\rm obs}=|\widehatπ|$ from a perturbative reduction along the observer's worldline in the $G \to 0$ limit. This provides a field-theoretic foundation for the observer model developed in this work.

</details>


### [2] [Defect conformal field theories with free-in-the-bulk fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14176)
*Christopher P. Herzog,Daniel Lloyd-Jones*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维自由无质量费米子体中的二维共形缺陷：体 Dirac 方程强烈约束缺陷 CFT 数据，固定体-缺陷 OPE 中算子维度，并在单一影子配对满足幺正性时对体-缺陷-缺陷三点函数施加双扭曲关系；单极子缺陷的最低模式是例外，两个影子伙伴均可幺正且三点函数由影子变换联系。文中用双线性流、Gross-Neveu 型四费米子耦合等例子阐释，并在微扰混合不动点处给出违反双扭曲条件的具体相互作用例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形缺陷（特别是对其上相互作用的费米子）如何受体动力学约束，揭示体 Dirac 方程对缺陷 CFT 数据（算子维度、三点函数）的结构性限制，并探索双扭曲关系何时被破坏。

Method: 以四维自由无质量费米子为体，在二维缺陷上引入相互作用；利用体 Dirac 方程约束缺陷共形场论数据。分析体-缺陷算子乘积展开与体-缺陷-缺陷三点函数，结合影子(shadow)对称与幺正性约束得到双扭曲关系。通过可精确求解的双线性流、Gross-Neveu 型四费米子耦合，以及微扰重整化群混合不动点进行具体演示。

Result: 体 Dirac 方程固定体-缺陷 OPE 中算子维度；当影子对中仅一个满足幺正性时，体-缺陷-缺陷三点函数满足双扭曲关系。单极子(monodromy)缺陷的最低模式是例外：影子对双方均幺正，三点函数由影子变换联系。在微扰混合不动点处找到显式相互作用例子，双扭曲条件被违反，且两个影子通道组合后给出规则的体-缺陷-缺陷关联函数。

Conclusion: 体的自由 Dirac 方程对二维缺陷 CFT 施加强烈约束，双扭曲关系是普遍的结构性结果，而单极子缺陷与微扰混合不动点揭示了该关系的例外与失效机制；影子通道的联合为构造规则关联函数提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study codimension-two conformal defects for massless fermions that are free in a four-dimensional bulk, but interact on a two-dimensional defect. The bulk Dirac equation strongly constrains defect conformal field theory data: it fixes the dimensions of operators in the bulk-defect operator product expansion and, for bulk-defect-defect three-point functions, enforces a double-twist relation when only one member of a shadow pair is allowed by unitarity. The lowest modes of monodromy defects provide an important exception, since both shadow partners can be unitary and their three-point functions are instead related by a shadow transform.
  We illustrate these structures with defect interactions, including an exactly tractable bilinear flow and Gross-Neveu-type quartic couplings. At a perturbative mixed fixed point, we find an explicit interacting example in which the double-twist condition is violated and the two shadow channels combine to give a regular bulk-defect-defect correlation function.

</details>


### [3] [The dimensional reduction of the spin-3 and spin-4 partially broken Fronsdal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14284)
*D. Dalmazi,A. L. R. dos Santos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对部分破缺的 Fronsdal 模型（自旋 3 和自旋 4）进行从 D+1 维到 D 维的 Kaluza-Klein 维数约化，得到无额外传播模式的有质量高自旋模型，并发现其质量为零极限具有非平凡的正负螺旋度交织行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高自旋（s=3、s=4）有质量模型能否像低自旋（s≤2）情形那样，通过 Kaluza-Klein 维数约化从部分破缺的 Fronsdal 模型得到，并考察所得模型的结构与质量极限行为。

Method: 采用 Kaluza-Klein 维数约化，将 D+1 维部分破缺的自旋 3 和自旋 4 Fronsdal 模型约化到 D 维；对约化模型进行在壳（on-shell）分析，并用规范不变场组合给出协变描述。

Result: 约化后的模型描述了无额外传播模式的有质量自旋 3 和自旋 4 粒子；它们与 Singh-Hagen 模型相差在壳为零的二次项；协变描述自然给出高阶导数模型；对 s=3 和 s=4，质量为零极限的粒子内容虽对应 ±j（j=0,1,…,s）螺旋度，但以非平凡方式交织，无法通过局域场重定义对角化。

Conclusion: 高自旋（s=3、s=4）情形下，通过维数约化得到的有质量模型在质量极限上表现出与低自旋不同的非对角化螺旋度交织结构，且协变描述必然涉及高阶导数，揭示了高自旋有质量理论的特殊性质。

Abstract: Here we perform a Kaluza-Klein dimensional reduction from $D+1$ to $D$ dimensions of a partially broken Fronsdal model for spin-3 and spin-4. The reduced models describe massive spin-3 and spin-4 particles without extra propagating modes. They differ from Singh-Hagen models by quadratic terms which vanish on shell, similarly to their higher dimensional massless parents. A covariant description of the reduced models in terms of gauge invariant field combinations naturally leads to higher derivative models, contrary to lower spin cases ($s\le 2$).
  For both $s=3$ and $s=4$ we have a rather peculiar massless limit. Although their particle content corresponds to the $\pm j$ helicities with $j=0,1, \cdots s$, as expected, they appear intertwined in a non trivial way preventing diagonalization through any local field redefinition.

</details>


### [4] [Three-dimensional vacua of $G_2$ flux compactifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14313)
*Aravind Aikot,Julian Choi,Timm Wrase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维 $\mathcal N=1$ 有效理论（来自 IIA、IIB/I 型及杂化弦在 $G_2$-结构空间上的紧化）的超对称真空，利用超势齐次性与标量度量导出普适真空关系与轴子稳定约束，并给出 AdS$_3$ 标量质量对应的共形维数、各类真空中的具体维数值、Minkowski 真空的精确平坦方向，以及大通量极限下的尺度分离阻碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不指定完整模空间度量的前提下，系统刻画三维 $\mathcal N=1$ 弦论紧化中 AdS 与 Minkowski 超对称真空的结构，确定对偶二维共形场论中算符的标度维数，并厘清大通量极限与尺度分离的可能性。

Method: 利用超势关于标量场的齐次性以及物理标量度量，推导普适真空关系与轴子稳定约束；将 AdS$_3$ 标量质量通过布雷滕洛纳-弗里德曼（BF）型关系换算为对偶 CFT 中算符的标度维数 $\Delta$；针对不同理论分支（大质量 IIA、IIB/I 型、杂化）分别计算具体维数，并结合膜荷（tadpole）约束与轴子认同（axionic identifications）分析大通量物理极限及无挠/有挠分支上的尺度分离阻碍。

Result: 在大质量 IIA 的 AdS$_3$ 真空中得到上界 $\Delta_{\max}\geq(20+4\sqrt{67})/7\approx 7.5$，并在无挠分支上得到 $B_2$ 轴子的精确维数 $\Delta_b=6$；IIB/I 型与杂化 AdS$_3$ 真空包含普适的体积-伸缩子模式 $\Delta_{\varphi s}=4$；在 IIB 单态-挠率分支上得到 $\Delta_{\varphi s}=20/7$ 与 $\Delta_b=16/7$；所有保留的经典闭弦理论中的超对称 Minkowski 真空都具有精确的体积-伸缩子平坦方向；并识别出各向同性尺度分离的挠率阻碍。

Conclusion: 通过对超势齐次性与标量度量的普适分析，可以无需完整模空间度量即确定三维 $\mathcal N=1$ 弦论真空的关键性质，给出各类 AdS$_3$ 真空的共形维数、Minkowski 真空的平坦方向，并揭示挠率对大尺度分离的阻碍。

Abstract: We study supersymmetric vacua of three-dimensional $\mathcal N=1$ effective theories from type IIA, type IIB/type I, and heterotic compactifications on $G_2$-structure spaces. Superpotential homogeneity and the physical scalar metric yield universal vacuum relations and axion stabilization constraints without specifying the full moduli-space metric. We express AdS$_3$ scalar masses through $Δ$, the scaling dimension of the corresponding operator in the dual two-dimensional conformal field theory. In massive type IIA AdS$_3$ vacua, we derive the bound $Δ_{\max}\geq(20+4\sqrt{67})/7\approx 7.5$ and an exact dimension $Δ_b=6$ for $B_2$ axions on the torsion-free branch. Type IIB type I and heterotic AdS$_3$ vacua contain a universal volume-dilaton mode with $Δ_{φs}=4$. On the singlet-torsion type IIB branch, we also obtain the volume-dilaton mode with $Δ_{φs}=20/7$ and $Δ_b=16/7$. Every supersymmetric Minkowski vacuum in the retained classical closed-string theories possesses an exact volume-dilaton flat direction. We characterize physical large-flux limits after tadpole constraints and axionic identifications, and identify torsion obstructions to isotropic scale separation.

</details>


### [5] [One-loop QED effective action at finite temperature and density beyond HTL limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14345)
*Farahmand Hasanov,Nikita Kolganov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在任意偶数维时空、有限温度和化学势下计算标量与旋量QED非局域单圈有效作用的框架，将有效作用形状因子归结为共同基本核，并给出无质量形状因子的闭合表达式及小质量展开系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限温度和化学势条件下，研究QED的非局域单圈有效作用对于理解热介质中的电磁响应（如德拜屏蔽、磁化率）至关重要，但现有方法常受限于软动量近似或无法同时处理质量、温度、化学势与动量依赖。

Method: 基于热核展开至背景场二次阶，将有效作用形状因子化为一个共同的基本核；通过对外部动量展开的重新求和，得到用多重对数函数及其对阶数导数表示的无质量形状因子闭合表达式；并确定小质量展开任意阶系数，公式保留对欧几里得外动量、温度和化学势的完整依赖。

Result: 得到无质量形状因子的显式闭合表达式；导出小质量展开的任意阶系数；计算出相应的光子极化算符及其静态极限，获得德拜屏蔽质量和电磁磁化率。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将有限温度、有限化学势下的QED有效作用问题统一到热核方法和重求和技术下，避免了软动量近似，为热QED中的屏蔽与磁化率等物理量提供了精确且系统的计算手段。

Abstract: We develop a framework for computing the nonlocal one-loop effective action in scalar and spinor QED at finite temperature and chemical potential in arbitrary even spacetime dimensions. Based on the heat kernel expansion to quadratic order in background fields, the framework reduces the effective action form factors to a common fundamental kernel. By resumming the external-momentum expansion, we obtain explicit closed-form expressions for the massless form factors in terms of polylogarithms and their derivatives with respect to the order. The same method determines the coefficients of the small-mass expansion to arbitrary order. The resulting expressions retain the full dependence on external Euclidean momentum, temperature, and chemical potential, without invoking the soft-momentum approximation. We also derive the corresponding photon polarization operators and evaluate their static limits, obtaining the Debye screening masses and electromagnetic susceptibilities.

</details>


### [6] [Characteristic Modes of Non-minimally Coupled Thick Branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14430)
*Jing-Xin Gong,Heng Guo,Yong-Tao Lu,Xin-Yi Pan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非最小耦合体标量场的厚膜的特征模式，发现两种厚膜模型的引力子零模均局域在膜上，并计算了共振KK模式与准正规模式，二者在低阶泛音上吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征模式能揭示引力系统固有性质与动力学响应，但具有非最小耦合标量场的厚膜的特征模式（特别是共振KK模式与准正规模式之间的联系）尚未被系统研究。

Method: 考虑两种代表性厚膜解：单扭结标量构型与具有内部结构的形变扭结构型。通过分析张量扰动方程，先检验引力子零模的局域性，再分别在频率域和时间域独立计算准正规模式，并将共振KK模式的质量与衰变率同准正规频率的实部与虚部幅值进行对比。

Result: 两种膜模型中引力子零模均局域在膜上。单扭结膜在ξ=1/7时未发现明显共振KK模式；形变膜在ξ=0.99时，增大形变参数k会产生更多共振模式且首个共振寿命更长。两种独立方法得到的低阶泛音准正规频率吻合良好；共振KK模式的质量与衰变率分别与对应准正规频率实部和虚部幅值吻合紧密。

Conclusion: 厚膜中非最小耦合标量场不影响引力子零模的局域化；共振KK模式与准正规模式之间存在明确对应，且形变参数可调控共振模式的数目与寿命。

Abstract: Characteristic modes provide important information about the intrinsic properties and dynamical response of gravitational systems. In this work, we investigate characteristic modes of thick branes with a non-minimally coupled bulk scalar field. Two representative thick brane solutions are considered, corresponding to a single-kink scalar configuration and a deformed kink configuration with internal structure, respectively. By analyzing the tensor perturbation equations, we first examine the localization of the graviton zero mode in the two brane models. For both brane models, the graviton zero mode is localized on the brane. No pronounced resonant Kaluza--Klein (KK) modes are found for the single-kink brane at $ξ=1/7$. For the deformed brane, we investigate the resonant KK modes at $ξ=0.99$, finding that increasing the deformation parameter $k$ leads to more resonant modes and a longer-lived first resonance.
  We then calculate the quasinormal modes (QNMs) of both brane models independently in the frequency and time domains. The quasinormal frequencies (QNFs) obtained by the independent methods agree well for the low overtones. The masses and decay rates of the resonant KK modes agree closely with the real parts and the magnitudes of the imaginary parts of the corresponding QNFs, respectively.

</details>


### [7] [Supersymmetric Observables from Surface Defect Anomalies in 4d and 6d SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18086)
*Zi-Xiao Huang,Ma-Ke Yuan,Yang Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维与六维超共形场论（SCFT）中半BPS表面缺陷对超对称Casimir能量及超对称Rényi熵普适对数项的贡献。作者利用缺陷反常多项式的等变积分及其在超对称Rényi背景上的推广，得到以缺陷't Hooft反常表达的闭式公式，并通过手征代数、五维局域化、全息探针膜及局域化手征费米子等多种方法交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面缺陷对超对称可观测量（如Casimir能量与Rényi熵的对数项）的贡献通常难以精确计算。作者旨在建立一套系统框架，将缺陷的贡献直接与缺陷的't Hooft反常联系起来，从而为四维和六维SCFT中的表面缺陷提供可计算的普适公式。

Abstract: We review a many-body formulation of gravitational anomalies as obstructions to realizing the complete low-energy data by a local {\it bosonic} lattice quantum system of the same dimension. An invertible bulk reproduces conventional anomaly inflow; a bulk with non-trivial topological excitations yields a non-invertible generalization. To describe the holographic theory of anomalies, we explain the distinction between uniquely defined topological order and many versions of topological quantum field theory and discuss the boundary-to-bulk map. As an application of the holographic theory of anomalies, we describe morphisms between local quantum theories (such as quantum field theories). These morphisms are built from a bulk domain wall and an invertible boundary junction, which give rise to a sandwich picture. Chiral boson/fermion theory, Laughlin edges, and boundaries of $\mathbb{Z}_2$ higher gauge theory with bosonic or fermionic point-like excitations illustrate the different kinds of information about non-invertible anomalies contained in transport coefficients, superselection sectors, and condensation data. In particular, a fermionic theory can be viewed as a theory with a particular non-invertible gravitational anomaly. Those examples also reveal that {\it symmetry $\sim$ gravitational anomaly $\sim$ topological order in one higher dimension}.

Abstract: We present a suite of 50 unpublished research-level theoretical physics problems with verifiable answers. As the amplitude increases into the heteroclinic regime ($A > φ_{v1}$), the field dynamically overflows the central barrier, yielding a vanishing time-averaged order parameter ($\langle φ\rangle = 0$) and driving a geometric curvature cancellation ($\langle V''(φ) \rangle_{t} \to 0$). Abstract: We study classical Bopp--Podolsky (BP) electrodynamics on four-dimensional de Sitter spacetime, focusing on its transverse retarded Green function and gauge-invariant electromagnetic response. Because de Sitter spacetime is maximally symmetric, its vector Green functions admit a covariant expansion in a finite basis of invariant bitensors, with scalar coefficients depending only on the invariant separation. For conserved sources, the fourth-order field operator factorizes into Maxwell and massive-vector operators, yielding the BP retarded kernel as the difference of their transverse Green functions. Using the invariant bitensor decomposition, we reduce the propagator equations to a hypergeometric master equation and determine the distributional structure of its solution. The common leading short-distance singularity of the Maxwell and massive-vector sectors cancels, leaving a logarithmic master singularity and a softened vector-potential wavefront. After one curl, the gauge-invariant field-strength kernel contains a null-cone delta distribution and a regular timelike tail. The smooth Maxwell master tail is annihilated by the curl, so the interior electromagnetic tail is generated entirely by the massive sector. We also derive an exact horizon-regular static potential, demonstrate the cancellation of the Coulomb divergence, recover the flat-space BP potential, and formulate the generalized Gauss law. On global de Sitter spacetime, the compactness of the spatial sections requires the total charge to vanish; we implement this constraint using an antipodal charge configuration with smooth factorized initial data. The analysis is classical and does not assume a quantum Lee--Wick contour or a preferred de Sitter vacuum.

Next, we obtain the spacetime spectrum of the horizonless geometry by directly performing the unfolded modular integrals, and recover the Maldacena-Ooguri-Son spectrum in the ungauged limit. When the chirally charged three-dimensional black string is dimensionally reduced, the target space is instead a two-dimensional charged black hole. We study the near extremal regime of the black hole and black string. For the two dimensional black hole, we propose two candidate towers of states that produce the expected $\frac{3}{2}\log T$ of the AdS$_2$ throat from the worldsheet discrete series representation. These towers appear only below the temperature at which the full amplitude becomes divergent. We also show that in the magnetic case the Green functions involving only Wilson loops are trivial and not so in the electric case.

Abstract: We study the imaginary part of the holographic timelike entanglement entropy (HTEE) in a charged asymptotically AdS$_5$ black-hole background whose dual field theory exhibits a critical point in the presence of a chemical potential $μ$ and temperature $T$. Identifying the imaginary part of the HTEE with the imaginary part of the pseudoentropy, we derive an analytical expression for this quantity and investigate its behavior on the stable and unstable thermodynamic branches. We find that the imaginary HTEE vanishes in the uncharged limit and is induced entirely by a nonzero chemical potential, signaling the emergence of quantum coherence between the initial and final states. As the dimensionless ratio $μ/T$ approaches its critical value, we obtain an inverse-square-root divergence in the derivative of the imaginary HTEE and extract the critical exponent $θ_I = 1/2$ analytically. We further show that the same exponent governs the temperature response at fixed chemical potential. Abstract: We compute the one-loop gauge-field two-point functions analytically in scalar QED on dS$_{d+1}$ for general dimension and scalar mass, using the EAdS reformulation of the in-in formalism arXiv:2007.09993. The Dirichlet EAdS gauge field acquires a Schwinger-type mass, with its longitudinal mode supplied by a composite of charged scalars obeying opposite boundary conditions, while the Neumann mode remains protected by gauge invariance. In dS, the Dirichlet correction induces an anomalous dimension $γ_J$ for the boundary current and modifies the late-time falloff of the electric field. This bulk calculation reproduces the boundary multiplet-recombination result of arXiv:2509.18888. Summing over the in-in contour branches gives the retarded dS bubble kernel, making direct contact with the Bros-Moschella framework. We establish a spectral Cutkosky rule that expresses the discontinuity of this kernel across the principal-series cut in the Casimir plane in terms of a two-particle cut, with both internal scalar lines placed on shell. Although the full bubble coefficient involves a ${}_7F_6$ hypergeometric function, the cut collapses to a closed product of Gamma functions and determines the complete non-local part of the kernel through a dispersion relation. Analytic continuation of the same cut reproduces the complete one-loop result for $γ_J$. In the complex Casimir plane, the loop-corrected Dirichlet photon appears as a pole on the second sheet and is therefore naturally interpreted as a dS resonance, or quasinormal mode, whose frequency is shifted at one loop.

### [51] [Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems for Knowledge-Grounded Material Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14123)
*Tarjei Paule Hage,Subhadeep Pal,Wei Lu,Yu-Chuan Hsu,Gayla Lyon,Jiezhu Jin,Markus J. Buehler*

TL;DR: MARS 是一种分层多智能体推理系统，用于在科学与制造约束下进行有知识依据的材料替代，并以 PFAS 替代为案例验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助科学发现需要从生成合理想法升级为可复现、感知约束的工作流，以支持实际工程决策；PFAS 替代问题要求替代物同时满足功能、环境、实验与加工等多重约束，极具挑战性。

Method: MARS 将材料替代分解为闭环工作流：需求分析、有依据的候选物生成、含工艺设计的可制造性评估；集成检索增强生成、知识图谱推理、实验室可用材料数据库、中间结果持久记忆及拒绝感知的迭代反馈。

Result: 在 PFAS 替代案例中，MARS 生成了结构化材料需求、找到有证据支撑的替代候选物，并开发出实验室规模制造路线；多种子消融评估显示，MARS 在三个子系统中总体性能最优，且在固定骨干模型时优势依然存在。

Conclusion: 分层分解、知识依据与可制造性感知的迭代优化能够超越模型本身能力，提升材料替代推理效果。

Abstract: AI-assisted scientific discovery increasingly seeks to move beyond generating plausible ideas toward reproducible, constraint-aware workflows that can support practical engineering decisions. We present MARS, a hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning System for knowledge-grounded material substitution under scientific and manufacturing constraints. We apply MARS to replace per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS), a demanding materials-design problem in which substitutes must satisfy functional performance, environmental, experimental, and processing requirements. MARS decomposes substitution into a closed-loop workflow comprising requirement analysis, grounded candidate generation, and manufacturability assessment with process design. The system integrates retrieval-augmented generation, knowledge-graph reasoning, a database of lab-available materials, persistent memory of intermediate results, and rejection-aware iterative feedback. In a PFAS-substitution case study, MARS generates structured material requirements, identifies an evidence-grounded substitute candidate, and develops a lab-scale manufacturing route. In a multi-seed ablation evaluation, MARS achieves the highest overall performance across all three subsystems, with gains that persist when the backbone model is held fixed. These results suggest that hierarchical decomposition, knowledge grounding, and manufacturability-aware refinement improve material-substitution reasoning beyond model capability alone.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了高熵合金（HEAs，以CoCrFeMnNi为例）纳米团簇在室温和高温下的相变行为，发现升温会使团簇粗化并产生成分不均匀性，并用分子动力学模拟佐证了原子迁移机制。

Conclusion: 高熵合金纳米团簇在预熔温度下由熵主导转变为焓主导，原子迁移引发团簇粗化与成分偏析。该研究结合HRTEM实验与分子动力学模拟，阐明了HEAs在升温过程中的相变行为和原子尺度机制，填补了高熵合金预熔温度区间行为认知的空白。

Abstract: The emergence of new materials has gone beyond conventional design principles. Among them are high entropy alloys (HEAs), in which multiple metallic elements coexist within a single unit cell. In this case, each atomic position in a solid material has a different chemical environment, leading to configurational entropy as high as that in the fully random or melted state, which has been frozen to a solid solution. However, the behaviour of such alloy systems at pre-melting temperatures is largely unknown, where atoms gain sufficient mobility and time to rearrange, leading to an enthalpic rather than an entropic interplay. This study demonstrated the phase transformation of HEA nanoclusters (CoCrFeMnNi) under high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HRTEM) at different temperatures, including room temperature (RT), 550 °C, and 850 °C. The clustered nanoparticles coarsen into larger clusters, accompanied by compositional heterogeneity due to surface and lattice diffusion. This experimental finding of atomic mobility was further corroborated using molecular dynamics, which provides a detailed mechanistic description.

Abstract: The luminescence of doped nanocrystals is usually optimised through the activator; here we show that the host cation ratio is an equally decisive, and largely overlooked, design variable. Mn-doped ZnGa$_2$O$_4$ nanocrystals (Mn:Zn = $4\times10^{-4}$) were grown hydrothermally at nominal Zn/Ga ratios between 0.30 and 0.75 (stoichiometric: 0.50) and post-annealed at 400--1100$^\circ$C, and the resulting composition--temperature matrix was mapped by X-ray- and UV-excited luminescence, EPR, Rietveld-refined X-ray diffraction, Raman spectroscopy and SEM. The as-grown crystals are phase-pure spinel at every composition and accommodate their off-stoichiometry internally as a point-defect inventory, reflected in a 0.4\% variation of the lattice parameter; on annealing, the excess gallium is exsolved as $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ in amounts set by the solution composition. The Mn$^{2+}$ emission separates into two optima, near-stoichiometric crystals annealed at 400--600$^\circ$C and Zn-excess crystals annealed at 1100$^\circ$C, the latter being twice as bright under X-ray excitation. Under X-ray and 365~nm excitation the two optima are separated by a minimum at 800$^\circ$C that coincides with a collapse of the Mn$^{2+}$ EPR population and has no counterpart in the Raman linewidths, identifying transient, defect-compensated oxidation of the dopant rather than lattice degradation as its origin. The most Zn-rich composition shows neither Mn$^{2+}$ nor Mn$^{4+}$ emission at any annealing stage, whereas its trace Cr$^{3+}$ emission is strong, indicating that Mn is rejected already during growth. Solution stoichiometry therefore acts on two levels, dopant capture and native-defect inventory, while annealing controls dopant valence and lattice order. The brightest high-temperature phosphor is thus not the nominally stoichiometric one, but the composition whose excess is consumed on its way to stoichiometry.

</details>

Abstract: Compression can change thermal conductivity differently along polymer chains and across them by altering phonon velocities and lifetimes. Between 3.452 and 5.056 GPa, both transverse components recover: at 300 K they increase by 23.7% and 13.7%, while the longitudinal conductivity decreases by 14.5%. An exact factorization separates the conductivity into a heat-capacity--velocity factor and a weighted effective lifetime. The transverse recovery follows from larger heat-capacity-weighted squared velocities, consistent with stiffer interchain restoring forces. Along the chains, shorter effective lifetimes dominate, and both conductivity-weighted Normal and Umklapp rates increase at 300 K. Comparing the same cubic force-constant blocks at all three pressures, we find that interchain coupling is already substantial at 1.503 GPa and increases under compression.

Here we report such measurements on textured thin films of SnTe grown on Bi2Te3 buffered c-plane sapphire (Al2O3) by pulsed laser ablation. A post deposition annealing protocol was implemented to control the crystallographic texture, which reveals improved crystallinity and enhanced epitaxial alignment along the [111] direction in films annealed at elevated temperatures. The higher carrier density and lower resistivity seen in films annealed at 100 oC are suggestive of Sn vacancies which act as native acceptor defects, while films annealed at 200 oC approach the 1:1 stoichiometry. The magnetoresistance of all samples at T < 10 K characterized by a cusp centered around zero-field is suggestive of weak antilocalization and interpreted in the framework of the Hikami-Larkin-Nagaoka theory in the strong spin-orbit coupling limit. At T > 20 K, the MR is small and positive, and deviates from conventional Kohler scaling. An approximate collapse of the MR curves between 50 and 250 K is obtained using an empirical temperature-dependent scaling factor, indicating that changes in the Hall-derived carrier density alone cannot account for the observed scaling behavior. These results demonstrate that post growth annealing strongly influences the interplay between stoichiometry, crystallinity, and coherent transport in SnTe thin films.

With this we assess their advantages and limitations regarding several aspects: varying training dataset size, robustness to noise, carbon footprint, as well as the ability to reproduce the radial distribution function as an example observable. Overall the MACE models are shown to most accurately reproduce the potential energy surface, with little sign of overfitting, but at a significant computational cost, highlighting important questions regarding the choice of models in computational materials science.

Abstract: Programmable hardware typically uses fixed architectures, whereas complex physical systems obtain diverse behavior by connecting reusable local elements through different interaction rules. Memristive devices combine writable states with nonlinear dynamics, but translating these local properties into reconfigurable collective order and symmetry-governed transport remains challenging. Here we develop modular circuits configured through programmable connections between identical memristive units. Experiments establish binary conductance states as Ising-type pseudospins and show that signed feedback between units selects uniform or staggered pseudospin order. Circuit-model simulations then examine controllable domain-wall motion and pattern recovery. A symmetry encoder combines measured conductance patterns with sublattice labels and a selected symmetry rule to configure a fixed transport layer, implementing circuit analogs of ferromagnetic, antiferromagnetic, and altermagnetic transport symmetries. Although the antiferromagnetic and altermagnetic encodings share a compensated pattern, their distinct voltage spreading illustrates how programmed circuit symmetry affects transport. Device-calibrated simulations show that memristive dynamics generates history-dependent features for reservoir computing. By separating local memory from programmable interactions, symmetry encoding and task-specific readout, this architecture provides a modular route from device dynamics to collective order, symmetry-dependent transport, and learning.

The population of the defect complex follows the law of mass action, and above a threshold Mn concentration, Mn re-partitions into EPR-silent octahedral configurations that quench both emissions. Many of these states are measured to be stable at least up to 1000 seconds confirming their non-volatility and their utility in storage of synaptic weights for ANN crossbar arrays. On the contrary, most prior experimental reports (including reports both on solution-processing-based and sputtering-based RRAM devices) require forming to exhibit non-volatility. Further, we confirm the presence of oxygen vacancies in our materials stack using X-ray photo-electron spectroscopy (XPS). Finally, we show successful data classification on MNIST and CIFAR-10 data sets using a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) model and a convolution neural network (CNN) model respectively, where the synaptic weight updates of these ANN models are calibrated against the experimentally measured LTP-LTD characteristics of the NiO RRAM devices.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Using Bi2O3 as a representative system, we identify two previously unreported polar monolayers with tetragonal and orthorhombic symmetries. Both structures exhibit vertically separated Bi and O sublayers, giving rise to pronounced intrinsic polarity with Berry-phase 2D polarizations of 34.12 and 37.15 pC/m, respectively. Notably, the o-Bi2O3 monolayer exhibits one of the largest reported out-of-plane polarizations among 2D oxides. Both monolayers exhibit dynamical stability, finite-temperature structural integrity, and wide band gaps (~3.8 eV). The large polarization originates from dimensionality-induced reconstruction of the nonlayered Bi-O network, which breaks local coordination symmetry and produces out-of-plane charge redistribution, while the stereochemically active Bi 6s2 lone pair further favors asymmetric Bi-O bonding. These findings suggest dimensionality-induced local-coordination symmetry breaking as a promising design motif for high-polarization 2D oxides, and demonstrate the utility of combining inverse design with machine-learning-assisted screening to explore unconventional polar phases in nonlayered oxide networks.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 所提出的基于涨落的方法在不同输运机制和结构构型下评估热导率均有效，验证了其作为免热库、多方向高效计算工具的可行性。

Abstract: We develop a fluctuation based Monte Carlo method for evaluating thermal conductivity within the phonon Boltzmann transport equation (BTE) framework. Starting from the energy based variance reduced formulation of the BTE, we derive a relation between the spatial spreading of deviational phonon energy and the macroscopic heat conduction equation. The resulting formulation expresses thermal conductivity in terms of the mean square displacement of deviational phonon particles, without imposing thermal reservoirs. Consequently, thermal conductivity along multiple directions can be evaluated from a single simulation. The method is demonstrated for crystalline silicon at room temperature in bulk, square nanowires, and anisotropic polycrystalline nanostructures. The calculated thermal conductivities agree with theoretical predictions, including the size dependence induced by phonon boundary scattering and the mode-resolved contributions. For anisotropic polycrystalline structures, the method simultaneously resolves the directional thermal conductivities and captures their dependence on grain morphology. These results demonstrate the validity of the proposed fluctuation-based approach for evaluating thermal conductivity across different transport regimes and structural configurations.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无矩阵的 Fisher 几何二阶优化方法，利用反向自动微分将观测似然的信息度量拉回原子坐标，实现对大型超胞粉末对分布函数（PDF）的精修，避免了稠密坐标空间分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粉末对分布函数精修需要优化大量耦合的原子坐标，但可用的径向观测量远少于坐标数。标准一阶优化器无法显式利用 PDF 灵敏度间的耦合来对坐标更新做预处理，而经典二阶方法（如 Levenberg-Marquardt）又需要构造大型 Jacobian 或坐标曲率矩阵，内存和计算代价过高。

Result: 对于高斯测量误差，该方法精确恢复经典 Levenberg-Marquardt 精修，同时规避了稠密坐标空间分解；同一计算框架可扩展到相关高斯误差与稳健似然更新，实现了似然感知的集体更新与无矩阵反向传播的结合。

Conclusion: 该方法将似然感知的集体坐标更新与无矩阵反向传播相结合，为大型原子超胞的直接二阶 PDF 精修提供了一种兼具计算效率与统计合理性的求解方案。

Abstract: Refining atomistic supercells against powder pair distribution functions (PDFs) requires optimizing many coupled atomic coordinates using a much smaller set of radial measurements. Reverse-mode differentiation makes gradients accessible for large systems, but standard first-order optimizers do not explicitly use coupled PDF sensitivities to precondition coordinate updates. We introduce a matrix-free, Fisher-geometric approach that pulls the information metric of the observation likelihood back onto atomic coordinates. The resulting update weights collective displacements according to their PDF response and experimental uncertainty. Under isotropic damping, the update is obtained by solving a damped linear system over $M$ PDF channels, using automatic differentiation to evaluate Jacobian--vector and vector--Jacobian products without constructing the full Jacobian or coordinate curvature matrix. For Gaussian measurement errors, the update recovers classical Levenberg--Marquardt refinement while avoiding dense coordinate-space factorization. The same computational framework accommodates correlated Gaussian errors and robust likelihood-based updates. This approach combines likelihood-aware collective updates with matrix-free backpropagation for direct second-order refinement of large atomistic supercells.

</details>

Abstract: Bismuth oxyselenide (Bi2O2Se) has emerged as a leading semiconductor candidate for next-generation electronics and quantum phenomena research. However, its strong ionic interlayer bonding of Se layers fundamentally restricts the full potential of this otherwise promising quasi-two-dimensional (2D) material. In this work, we demonstrate chlorine substitution as an effective strategy to introduce van der Waals gaps and modify the electronic structure of the system. Through comprehensive transport and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements on Bi6O6Se2Cl2 single crystal, an optimal Cl-doping compound, we show that the key electronic features, including indirect band gap, high electron mobility, and carrier effective mass, are well preserved, while the 2D character of the system is sufficiently enhanced. First-principles calculations attribute this selective tunability to the unique spatial separation between conductive Bi-O layers and dopant Se/Cl layers, which enables independent optimization of interlayer coupling and intralayer transport. Remarkably, our theoretical calculations combined with experimental observations project a broad bandgap engineering across a wide range (0.8 ~ 3.5 eV) within the Bi2O2Se system. Our findings not only expand the application potential of Bi2O2Se-based materials in optoelectronics, nanoelectronics, and fundamental research but also highlight spatial decoupling as a viable strategy for modifying detailed electronic structure of quasi-2D quantum systems.

### [67] [Finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory: a unified framework for i-DMFT and TAO-DFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14706)
*Jingang Han,Huanjing Gong,Kai Luo,Hongming Weng,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出有限温度绝热连接约化密度矩阵泛函理论（FT-AC-RDMFT），将 i-DMFT 与 TAO-DFT 统一为同一框架的不同约化，并证明其能显著改善强相关体系的键断裂描述和固体体模量计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述化学键断裂需要捕获强/静态电子关联，而 HF 与 KS-DFT 等单参考自洽场方法对此存在重大困难。分数占据虽能改善这一问题（如 i-DMFT 和 TAO-DFT），但二者基于截然不同的理论框架、带有一定半经验性，且彼此关系不明，缺乏统一的理论基础。

Abstract: This work explores the edge state conduction properties of twinned and spiraled, 2D Sb2Te3 topological insulator crystals. Motivation: 凝聚态物理将从人工智能的广泛应用中获益巨大，但当前ARPES领域以论文发表为中心的工作流程只保存了高度筛选后的少量实验数据，导致数十年来积累的大量谱学测量及其上下文信息碎片化甚至流失，阻碍了可复现性、数据复用以及AI驱动的发现。因此亟需建立一个共享的数据生态系统来整合这些分散的测量并形成持久的集体科学记忆。

Method: 本文采用Perspective（观点/展望）视角，报告最近一次社区研讨会成果，并借鉴现有科学数据库的经验，分析ARPES领域人工智能的现状，讨论社区在数据组织、访问、署名/激励、治理等方面的共识，进而提出一套分阶段试点的实用路线图，以构建标准化的数据生态系统。

Result: 研究提出了按三个时间尺度展开的收益路径：近期，标准化、可搜索、可引用的数据集可以独立于AI强化分析流程；中期，经社区验证、可在不同实验室间运作的工具将促进更广泛的数据复用和日益闭环的自动化实验；长期，多样化的实测电子谱语料库可支撑量子物质基座模型并催生新发现。

Conclusion: 构建ARPES共享数据生态系统是释放AI在凝聚态物理中潜力的关键一步，应采取分阶段试点的方式推进，在数据组织、访问、署名与治理等社区共识基础上，逐步实现从标准化数据管理到自动化实验和基座模型的跃升，最终推动新发现。

Abstract: Condensed matter stands to gain profoundly from widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI). However, its current means of data organization impedes this endeavor. As a premier measure of electronic structure underlying material properties, Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES) has accumulated over four decades of spectra, yet the community's publication-centered workflow preserves only a highly selected fraction of its experimental record. A shared data ecosystem could transform these fragmented measurements and their contexts into a persistent, collective scientific memory, creating immediate value for reproducibility and reuse while laying the foundation for AI-enabled discovery. In this Perspective, we report outcomes of a recent community workshop and propose a practical path toward such an ecosystem. Its benefits unfold along three timescales. In the near term, standardized, searchable, and citable datasets strengthen analysis pipelines independently of AI. In the intermediate future, community-validated tools operating across laboratories could enable broader reuse and increasingly closed-loop automated experiments. In the long term, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter. Looking forward, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter and new discoveries. We discuss lessons from existing scientific databases, the current state of AI in ARPES, as well as areas of community convergence on data organization, access, credit, governance, and a staged pilot program.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 EpNet-Si，一种基于等变网络的经验赝势方法（EPM），通过预测硅原子局部环境的平面波形因子，将经典 EPM 扩展到可跨晶体结构迁移，并以极少的 DFT 参考数据训练，实现高精度、低成本的能带结构计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典经验赝势方法（EPM）在每个 k 点只需一次对角化、无需自洽迭代，计算快速，但其原子形状因子固定，无法在不同晶体结构之间迁移，限制了其作为现代电子结构计算工具的适用性。

Result: 在训练结构类型的 231 个畸变中，142 个有带隙的晶胞平均能带误差与带隙误差分别为 15 meV 和 8 meV；在 192 个未见结构中，165 个有带隙的晶胞平均误差为 38 meV 和 49 meV。由 EpNet-Si 导出的物理性质（有效质量、带隙压力系数、形变势、费米能级态密度）与参考 DFT 结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，经典 EPM 在小规模参考数据上训练后，可以扩展为现代电子能带结构计算的快速替代方案，兼具高精度与低计算成本。

Abstract: The empirical pseudopotential method (EPM) computes band structures with one diagonalization per $k$ point and no self-consistency, but its fixed atomic form factors do not transfer between crystal structures. To address this limitation, we introduce EpNet-Si, an EPM for silicon in which an equivariant network predicts each atom's plane-wave form factor from its local environment: a species potential plus monopole, dipole and quadrupole corrections, mixed by a learned router over atomic densities, together with an $s/p$ nonlocal projector. With 6{,}195 parameters, the model is trained through the eigensolver on reference density functional theory (DFT) band energies of only 77 cells. Among 231 distortions of the trained structure types, the 142 gapped cells have mean band and gap errors of 15 and 8\,meV; among 192 unseen structures, the 165 gapped cells have mean errors of 38 and 49\,meV. Physical properties derived from EpNet-Si, including effective masses, gap pressure coefficients, deformation potentials and the density of states at the Fermi level, agree with those obtained from the reference DFT calculations. These results suggest that the classical EPM, fitted to a small set of reference data, can be extended into a fast alternative for modern electronic band-structure calculations.

### [76] [DiffGA: Steering Frozen Diffusion Models for Materials Design via Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14999)
*Shivang Agarwal,Rodrigo Wang,Kevin Ryczko,Aayush R. Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiffGA，一种免重训练框架，将进化搜索注入预训练扩散模型的采样轨迹，在任意黑盒目标下进行逆向材料设计，并利用基于 MLFF 图嵌入的贝叶斯代理降低评估成本；实验表明其在晶体设计任务中优于扩散、纯进化及微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向材料设计的关键是让候选生成朝用户定义的目标优化，但许多实际目标（来自仿真或实验）是黑盒、动态或不可微的。现有生成模型方法要么需要针对每个新目标重训练/微调（需标注数据和高算力），要么依赖对梯度噪声脆弱、对许多实际目标无定义的微分引导。

Method: 提出 DiffGA：在冻结的预训练扩散模型（MatterGen）采样轨迹中嵌入进化搜索，对部分去噪状态在任意黑盒预言机下进行演化，实现免重训练的目标引导。同时，因预言机评估常主导成本，用即时训练的贝叶斯代理加速：在 MLFF 图嵌入上拟合高斯过程，一旦其能可靠排序未见生成样本，就在不确定性感知的采集规则下替代昂贵的属性评估，降低预言机成本。

Result: 在三个晶体设计任务中，DiffGA 在稳定、唯一、新颖命中的预言机评估率上均优于纯扩散、算力匹配的纯进化及微调基线。通过密度泛函理论（DFT）量化 MLFF 预言机保真度，发现其在关键区域是忠实代理，但在其他区域存在系统性偏差，并据此界定了代理驱动生成发现的适用范围。代表性样本的 DFT 复评证实 DiffGA 的顶级命中物为物理现实命中，且代理加速管线在显著降低成本的同时保持了该保真度。

Conclusion: DiffGA 提供了一种免重训练、可适应任意黑盒目标的逆向材料设计方法，通过与 MLFF 驱动的贝叶斯代理结合大幅降低预言机成本；DFT 验证表明其顶级命中物理可靠，且代理加速不牺牲保真度，同时明确了代理可被信任的边界条件。

Abstract: Steering candidate generation toward user-defined objectives is central to inverse materials design, yet remains difficult when objectives are black-box, changing, or non-differentiable, as in many simulation- and experiment-derived properties. Generative models offer a solution but typically require retraining or fine-tuning for each new objective, demanding labeled data and substantial compute, or rely on differentiable guidance that is brittle to gradient noise and undefined for many practical targets. We present DiffGA, a retraining-free framework that injects evolutionary search into the sampling trajectory of a retrained diffusion model, evolving partially denoised states under an arbitrary black-box oracle. Because oracle evaluation often dominates campaign cost, we couple DiffGA with an on-the-fly Bayesian surrogate that reuses representations from state-of-the-art machine-learned force fields (MLFFs). A Gaussian process is fit during the campaign on the force fields's graph embeddings and, once it demonstrably ranks an unseen generation, replaces expensive property evaluations under an uncertainty-aware acquisition rule, reducing oracle cost with negligible loss of accuracy. Across three crystal-design tasks using a frozen MatterGen backbone, DiffGA outperforms diffusion-only, compute-matched, purely evolutionary, and fine-tuned baselines in oracle-assessed rates of stable, unique, and novel hits. Rather than proposing scientific compounds, we use density functional theory to quantify MLFF oracle fidelity, showing that it is a faithful proxy in key regimes but systematically biased in others, delineating when proxy-driven generative discovery can be trusted. On a representative sample, DiffGA's top hits survive first principles re-evaluation as physically realistic hits, while the surrogate-accelerated pipeline preserves this fidelity at a fraction of the cost.

</details>

Abstract: Platinum diselenide (PtSe$_2$), a member of the transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) family, is a promising material for next-generation electronic and optoelectronic applications due to its thickness-dependent properties, transitioning from semimetallic in bulk to semiconducting behavior at the few-layer limit. However, the scalable fabrication of large-area ultrathin PtSe$_2$ films remains challenging, as conventional selenization of pre-deposited Pt layers often leads to undesired three-dimensional clustered growth. The method involves coating a Pt layer with graphene oxide (GO), followed by annealing in a selenium atmosphere, which results in the simultaneous formation of PtSe$_2$ and the reduction of GO to graphene. The graphene overlayer acts as a physical confinement layer, suppressing vertical growth and enabling the synthesis of graphene-confined PtSe$_2$ heterostructures on centimetre-scale substrates and promoting lateral connectivity of PtSe$_2$ regions observed by STEM. Scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM), including temperature-resolved in situ atomic-resolution measurements, was used to investigate the influence of selenization temperature on the growth mechanism and structural evolution of PtSe$_2$ films. The results show that graphene confinement stabilizes lateral growth and promotes the formation of few-layer films with improved structural ordering. Beyond PtSe$_2$, this graphene-assisted approach provides a general strategy for fabricating and stabilizing ultrathin TMD layers, including those prone to environmental degradation, providing a new outlook for enabling detailed studies of their intrinsic properties.

Abstract: Corrosion of structural alloys remains a critical challenge for advanced molten salt reactors, where aggressive fluoride salts promote continuous dissolution at the salt-metal interface. While most mitigation strategies emphasize thermodynamic stability, kinetic control of atomic transport has received comparatively less attention. Here, we propose a corrosion mitigation strategy based on diffusion control and vacancy trapping by dilute yttrium (Y) solutes and yttrium oxide (Y$_2$O$_3$) dispersions in Ni alloys. A combined computational and experimental approach integrates density functional theory (DFT), self-consistent mean-field (SCMF) diffusion modeling, and an impurity trapping model to quantify the impact of defect trapping on effective vacancy mobility. Strong first-nearest-neighbor vacancy-Y binding, off-lattice vacancy configurations, and elevated rotational and dissociation barriers result in pronounced suppression of vacancy diffusivity, while Y$_2$O$_3$-associated sites treated as fixed traps in the model further reduce the mobile vacancy population. Ni-Mo (NM), Y-modified (NMY), and Y$_2$O$_3$-containing (NMYO) alloys were tested in molten fluoride salt at 750°C. Corrosion penetration decreases systematically from NM to NMY to NMYO, consistent with the predicted reduction in vacancy-mediated transport. The same trend is observed in mass change, with net weight loss decreasing from 0.52% for NM to 0.23% for NMY and 0.09% for NMYO. These results are consistent with an alloy-side transport contribution from defect trapping and reduced atomic transport. This study therefore suggests point defect engineering as a promising alloy design strategy for extreme nuclear environments.

The I(q) classification isolates, in every scan, an interface environment that holds 62-100% of the nucleus pixels (1.2-2.5-fold above depth) and persists when the nucleus pixels are removed before classification. The interface, rather than a distinct composition or density of the nucleating volume, therefore marks where nuclei form. Over 4.7e3 e/Å^2 the HAADF signal falls by 17%, the first shell shortens by 0.03 Å and the FSDP narrows by 9%, apparently ordering the film as it damages it. All analyses used PySTEMLab, an interactive 4D-STEM analysis environment developed for this work.

</details>

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) atomic-structure analysis is of central importance in (scanning) transmission electron microscopy (STEM).   <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 结合实验与理论的研究揭示并解释了Al₂MnCu中非磁性无序诱导Tc升高的罕见现象，为理解无序在磁性材料中的反直觉作用提供了新范式。

Our magnetic critical analysis, in the vicinity of the Curie temperature ($T_{\rm C}$), does not lead to any conventional single universality class expected for a standard ferromagnet; instead, the non-monotonic temperature dependence of effective critical exponents points towards more complex magnetic interactions, akin to inhomogeneous ferromagnets. Theoretical analysis establishes that the enhancement of the local magnetic ordering temperature arises directly from the strengthening of exchange interactions due to the small degree of non-magnetic disorder, whereas $T_{\rm C}$ is hardly influenced by the much larger level of magnetic-atom disorder. The combined experimental and theoretical investigations on the anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu reveal and explain the unusual observation of nonmagnetic disorder-induced elevation of $T_{\rm C}$, providing a paradigm for the counterintuitive role of disorder in magnetic materials.

### [84] [Hidden Spin-Valley Hall Effect and Gate-Switchable Electric Hall Effect in 2D Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15630)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 2D altermagnets with spin-valley-layer coupling (SVLC) have hidden spin-valley Hall currents; a gate field breaks the symmetry-enforced compensation and converts them into a net, switchable charge Hall response, enabling all-electrical valley-spin control and switchable magneto-optics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: altermagnets 的电荷霍尔响应消失，但自旋向上/向下霍尔电流并未缺失，而是因对称性被抵消隐藏；当这些自旋态锁定到不同谷时，构成隐藏的自旋-谷霍尔效应。作者希望找到一种机制（如栅场）来打破对称性补偿，从而将隐藏响应转化为可观测、可开关的净电荷霍尔信号。

Method: 采用共线自旋层群（collinear spin layer group, cSLG）理论，对全部 158 个 altermagnetic cSLGs 进行分类，识别出 32 个支持 SVLC 的群；预言单层 Mg2Os2(SO5)2 与 Ca(CoN)2 家族为候选材料；并对 Mg2Os2(SO5)2 进行第一性原理计算，研究栅场下的谷劈裂、霍尔响应和磁光克尔旋转。

Result: Mg2Os2(SO5)2 的第一性原理计算显示：可实现可逆的谷劈裂、栅场可开关的霍尔响应，以及超过 1 mrad 的克尔旋转，且克尔旋转符号随 EHE（电场霍尔效应）信号一同反转。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于对称性的全电谷-自旋调控路径，并表明 altermagnets 是高性能谷输运和电可开关磁光器件的理想平台。

Abstract: Hidden beneath the vanishing charge Hall response of altermagnets are spin-up and spin-down Hall currents of opposite sign---concealed, not absent---whose cancellation is enforced by symmetry. Here we show that in 2D altermagnets with spin-valley-layer coupling (SVLC), a gate field unveils this hidden spin-valley Hall response.~By lifting the symmetry-enforced compensation between valleys with opposite layer polarizations, the field converts the hidden response into a net charge Hall signal, yielding a gate-switchable electric Hall effect (EHE) enabled by SVLC. ### [85] [Indirect prediction and screening of frustrated magnetic materials using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15683)

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种机器学习工作流，通过分别预测居里-外斯温度和奈尔温度来间接预测磁阻挫指数，并对约67000种化合物进行筛选，识别出高置信度的阻挫磁性候选材料。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该机器学习工作流可有效间接预测阻挫指数并用于大规模筛选潜在阻挫磁性化合物，所发现的未探索氧化物体系值得进一步实验研究。

Abstract: Magnetic frustration is commonly quantified by the frustration index, calculated from the Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. In our work, composition-based models implemented via the CatBoost algorithm, trained on experimental data were optimized using chemically informed dataset subdivision. The highest predictive accuracy was achieved for the rare earth-oxide model in predicting Neel and for the rare earth model in predicting Curie-Weiss. Ensemble uncertainty was incorporated through a conservative frustration index to improve the reliability of candidate selection. Screening of approximately 67000 Materials Project compounds identified 20 high-confidence frustrated magnetic candidates, including several experimentally known frustrated magnets and previously unexplored oxide systems potential for further investigation.

</details>


### [86] [Reversible intrinsic-extrinsic crossover of the anomalous Hall effect in PrAlSi via Al/Si ratio tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15726)
*Huiyang Yang,Xiaohua Luo,Hongwei You,Wei Ren,Yuntao Gao,Changcai Chen,Zhishuo Zhang,Chunsheng Fang,Shengcan Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过调节PrAlSi单晶的Al/Si化学计量比（约1.05至0.75），可在同一材料体系中可逆地实现反常霍尔效应（AHE）机制在“内禀-外禀-内禀”之间的转变。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性Weyl半金属中的反常霍尔效应（AHE）具有内禀Berry曲率和外禀散射两种不同的微观起源，但在单一材料平台内实现这两种机制之间的可逆切换此前尚未实现。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in magnetic Weyl semimetals arises from distinct microscopic origins-intrinsic Berry curvature and extrinsic scattering mechanisms-yet reversible switching between these regimes within a single material platform remains elusive. Across four compositions spanning a narrow Si variation (PrAl0.93Si0.89 to PrAl0.79Si1.06), the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) exhibits a non monotonic arch (170-1413- 507-164 Ω-1cm-1). The intermediate composition PrAl0.90Si0.94 (Al/Si ~0.96) exhibits high carrier mobility, multi carrier transport, yielding a giant AHC of 1413 Ω-1cm-1 dominated by extrinsic skew-scattering. The Si-poor and Si-rich compositions exhibit lower AHC values and intrinsic Berry-curvature-dominated AHE, establishing a composition-driven intrinsic-extrinsic-intrinsic crossover. Deviating from the intermediate composition suppresses mobility and reverses carrier type from hole-like to electron-like. The Al/Si ratio thus serves as a chemical knob that primarily modulates the Fermi level via compositional band filling, while off-stoichiometry-induced chemical disorder plays a subordinate role in determining the AHE mechanism. These results position off-stoichiometric PrAlSi as a tunable platform where subtle compositional variation reversibly switches the AHE mechanism.

### [87] [Ionomer-free Iridium Anodes with Low Loading for Zero-gap Electrolyzers via Direct Atomic Layer Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15798)
*Peter M. Piechulla,Iris Blommestein,Anne van Gerven,Henri Pelzer,Ezgi Demiröz,Jonas Englhard,Mingliang Chen,Julien Bachmann,J. Ruud van Ommen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用原子层沉积（ALD）技术，在定制微孔基底上制备无离聚物（ionomer-free）的超低铱载量阳极催化剂层，将 PEMWE 电极铱载量降至 0.21 mg/cm²（等离子体增强 ALD 可达 0.04 mg/cm²），在仅约为商用膜电极 1/7 载量下实现了相当的电池性能与耐久性，并可与多种膜材料兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEMWE 是耦合间歇性可再生能源制氢的重要技术，但其规模化受限于阳极催化剂铱（Ir）供给稀缺、成本高昂。核心动机是大幅降低铱载量，同时保持或提升性能与耐久性。

Method: 采用定制微孔基底，结合两种基于 Ir(EtCp)(CHD) 前驱体的 ALD 工艺直接沉积催化剂：①热 ALD 以 O₃ 为共反应物，生成以金属态 Ir/IrOx 为主的催化剂，电极铱载量 0.21 mg/cm²；②等离子体增强 ALD（PE-ALD）通过调控催化剂空间分布将铱载量进一步降至 0.04 mg/cm²，但产物为活性较低的 IrO₂。该方法无需含离聚物的浆料，可直接在多种膜材料上沉积。

Abstract: Proton exchange membrane water electrolysis (PEMWE) is among the most promising techniques to provide hydrogen as both energy carrier and feedstock for the chemical industry, due to its compatibility with intermittent renewable electricity sources. However, a major bottleneck in scaling up PEMWE is the limited supply of the anode catalyst Ir. This work shows a novel approach to tailoring ionomer-free catalyst layers based on custom-made microporous substrates and two different atomic layer deposition (ALD) processes, both using Ir(EtCp)(CHD) precursor. Thermal ALD using O3 as counter-reactant yields mostly metallic Ir/IrOx and an electrode Ir loading of 0.21 mg/cm2. The PEMWE cell-level performance is comparable to a commercial catalyst-coated membrane anode despite 7x lower loading, and is maintained over an accelerated durability test equivalent to 600 h of intermittent operation. The Raman metrics exhibit reproducible temperature dependence that captures the transition window, while CaCl$_2$ induces systematic shifts and measurable modifications in the apparent latent-heat signature. These results highlight Raman spectroscopy as a quantitative probe of thermodynamic evolution and impurity effects in water and related systems.

Conclusion: PLD 的远离平衡生长与动力学冻结将生长动力学、外延约束和各阳离子化学行为耦合起来，使高熵氧化物薄膜在保持化学无序的同时获得极高结晶质量乃至完全相干外延，这为发现和设计新一代功能薄膜与异质结构开辟了广阔空间。

Abstract: Pulsed laser deposition (PLD) expands the compositional and structural possibilities of high-entropy oxide (HEO) thin films through far-from-equilibrium growth and kinetic arrest of metastable configurations. This review connects growth kinetics and epitaxial constraints to the roles of individual cations in shaping the resulting films. We focus primarily on rock salt MgCoNiCuZnO and its derivatives, with selected fluorite, spinel, and perovskite examples illustrating broader applicability. In MgCoNiCuZnO, Co valence and Cu coordination illustrate how chemistry and growth conditions jointly enable lattice tuning and coherent nanostructure formation, while aliovalent and size-misfit additions expand the accessible defect and compositional landscapes. These coupled degrees of freedom underpin anomalous crystallinity, the coexistence of extensive chemical disorder with exceptional crystalline quality and epitaxial order, including full coherence in selected systems, particularly rock salts grown on MgO. This combination opens opportunities to discover and engineer functional configurations in high-quality single-layer films and heterostructures.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用非微扰晶格动力学方法，结合第一性原理精度训练的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）；利用随时间变化的自洽简谐近似（TD-SCHA）分析声子谱性质，并计算谱函数与红外响应。

Result: 证明非谐性（而非仅体积膨胀）在决定Li₂O的相变温度与机理中起决定性作用；TD-SCHA 谱分析揭示该材料中准粒子图像发生破缺；谱函数与红外响应提供了可通过非弹性中子/X射线散射及红外光谱实验探测的相变特征。

Conclusion: 本文确立了Li₂O在超离子态附近具有极端非谐性，并提供了理解与调控此类材料快离子相变的晶格动力学框架，为超离子导体的设计与工程化奠定基础。

Abstract: Superionic conductors are promising electrolyte candidates for all-solid-state batteries, thanks to their liquid-like ionic mobility. In the prototypical lithium-ion oxide superionic conductor with antifluorite structure, the high-temperature transition to the fast-ion regime has long been attributed to volume-driven harmonic phonon softening. However, this quasi-harmonic picture neglects the strong lattice anharmonicity inherent to the fast-ion regime, leaving the microscopic origin of the transition unresolved. Here, we apply non-perturbative lattice dynamics combined with a first-principles-accurate machine-learning interatomic potential to study the phase transition in Li$_2$O. We demonstrate that anharmonicity, rather than volume expansion alone as commonly assumed, plays the decisive role in determining the transition temperature and the underlying mechanism. Finally, we analyze the spectral properties using the time-dependent self-consistent harmonic approximation, revealing the breakdown of the quasi-particle picture in this material. The spectral function and the infrared response provide signtures of the phase transition that can be investigated experimentally using inelastic neutron or X-ray scattering and infrared spectroscopy. All these results establish the extreme anharmonicity of Li$_2$O near the superionic regime and provide a lattice-dynamic framework for understanding and engineering the fast-ion transition in this type of materials.

### [91] [An empirical model for deriving information on ion dynamics from $μ$SR data via the conventional Kubo-Toyabe function analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16006)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用传统Kubo-Toyabe函数G_z^KT(t)分析离子扩散的μSR数据时，会观察到线宽Δ_KT(T)和涨落率ν_KT(T)的反常温度依赖，这些其实是由方法不当产生的假象（artifacts）。作者希望建立两者参数间的解析关系，以解释并利用这些假象。

Method: 构建一个经验模型，解析地将扩展Kubo-Toyabe函数G_z^EA(t;Δ,ν_i,Q)（含Edwards-Anderson参数Q，描述离子跳跃扩散）的参数关系到传统KT函数G_z^KT(t;Δ_KT,ν_KT)的参数上。再将模型应用于文献中已发表的电池材料Δ_KT与ν_KT数据，反推Arrhenius方程ν_i(T)=ν_i0 e^{-E_i/kT}中的活化能E_i和指前因子ν_i0。

Result: 所提出的公式成功重现了Δ_KT(T)和ν_KT(T)的反常温度依赖。应用于文献电池材料数据所得活化能E_i和指前因子ν_i0与其他技术推导结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 该模型将传统KT拟合产生的"反常假象"转变为提取离子扩散参数的强大诊断工具，可准确获得与其它技术一致的活化能和指前因子。

Abstract: We present an empirical model that analytically relates the parameters of the extended Kubo-Toyabe (KT) function $G_z^{\rm EA}(t;Δ,ν_{\rm i},Q)$, which describes muon spin relaxation ($μ$SR) induced by jump diffusion of ions using the Edwards-Anderson parameter $Q$, to those of the conventional KT function $G_z^{\rm KT}(t;Δ_{\rm KT},ν_{\rm KT})$ that serves as an approximation of $G_z^{\rm EA}(t)$. The proposed formulae successfully reproduce the anomalous temperature dependences of the linewidth $Δ_{\rm KT}(T)$ and the fluctuation rate $ν_{\rm KT}(T)$ which are artifacts observed upon analyzing $μ$SR data on ion diffusion using $G_z^{\rm KT}(t)$. Applying this model to $Δ_{\rm KT}$ and $ν_{\rm KT}$ published in the literature of battery materials yields activation energy $E_{\rm i}$ and pre-exponential factor $ν_{\rm i0}$ in the Arrhenius equation for ion diffusion, $ν_{\rm i}(T)=ν_{\rm i0} e^{-E_{\rm i}/kT}$, that are in excellent agreement with those deduced using other techniques, transforming peculiar artifacts into a powerful diagnostic tool.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的光致自旋重取向研究通常受自旋轨道耦合强度限制，需依赖重元素；作者希望突破这一限制，将光控自旋拓展到非相对论极限，从而扩宽实现平台。

Method: 采用自旋群（spin group）理论分析，考察自旋空间与晶格空间中对称算符如何支配自旋力矩及布洛赫波函数的量子几何（量子度规与贝里曲率）；给出位点分辨的自旋对称性并推导选择定则；结合 Kagomé 晶格模型与实际材料家族展示光辐照下的局域自旋力矩。

Conclusion: 该工作将光-自旋电子学从相对论极限推广到非相对论极限，显著拓宽了其实现平台与应用前景。

Abstract: Manipulating spin using contactless and non-invasive optical approaches is one of the forefront topic in the modern spintronic science and technology. Prior studies on optically-induced spin reorientation usually scales with spin-orbit coupling strength, setting a limitation on composing heavy elements. In this work, we extend such a limit into nonrelativistic regime by exploring noncollinear magnetic configuration. Using a general spin group-theoretical investigation, we show how the symmetric operators in both spin and lattice spaces control the spin torque and quantum geometry of Bloch wavefunction--including both quantum metric and Berry curvature. We describe their site-resolved spin symmetries and unravel their selection rules. Furthermore, we adopt a Kagomé lattice model and a realistic material family to showcase the local spin torque under light irradiation. Since the nonrelativistic spin splitting usually spans on the order of electron-volt, significantly larger than typical spin-orbit induced splitting, the nonrelativistic light-induced spin torque is concomitantly much stronger. This work extends the previous opto-spintronics from relativistic to nonrelativistic limit, significantly broadening its realization platform.

</details>


### [93] [Vibrational Fingerprints of Competing Structural and Polar Phases in CuInP2S6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16091)
*Pegah Mohammadi,Sobhit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理密度泛函理论，系统计算了CuInP2S6各候选结构相（顺电C2/c与P-31c、铁电Cc与P31c、反铁电P-1与P-3）的声子谱与振动模式，确定了各相的拉曼和红外活性模式及特征振动指纹，并用Porto记号建立了不同背散射几何下的偏振与取向依赖的拉曼选择定则，为通过振动光谱可靠鉴别其温度与压力驱动的竞争结构、铁电和反铁电相提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CuInP2S6存在丰富的温度与压力驱动相变序列，但各结构相因结构相似且振动响应复杂，难以被无歧义地区分；需建立可靠的振动光谱判定依据以识别其竞争的结构、铁电和反铁电相。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，系统研究六个候选相的振动性质：顺电C2/c与P-31c相、对应的动力学稳定铁电Cc与P31c相，以及反铁电P-1与P-3相。基于计算声子谱和对称性分析，确定各结构的拉曼与红外活性模式，提取特征振动指纹；并利用Porto记号建立不同背散射几何下偏振和晶体取向依赖的拉曼选择定则。

Result: 得到了各候选结构相的拉曼和红外活性模式及可区分不同相的特征振动指纹；确定了在特定实验配置下预期出现或消失的模式，建立了偏振与晶体取向依赖的拉曼选择定则。

Conclusion: 该工作为解释CuInP2S6的拉曼和红外测量提供了全面的理论框架，并能够可靠识别其温度和压力驱动相变中存在的竞争性结构、铁电和反铁电相。

Abstract: Layered van der Waals CuInP2S6 crystals exhibit a rich sequence of temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions, giving rise to multiple structural phases with distinct physical properties. Despite extensive experimental and theoretical efforts, unambiguous identification of these phases remains challenging because of their structural similarities and complex vibrational responses. In this work, we employ first-principles density functional theory calculations to systematically investigate the vibrational properties of the reference paraelectric C2/c and P-31c phases and their corresponding dynamically stable ferroelectric Cc and P31c phases, as well as the antiferroelectric P-1 and P-3 phases. From calculated phonon spectra and symmetry analysis, we determine the Raman- and infrared-active modes of each candidate structure and identify characteristic vibrational fingerprints that enable their phase determination through vibrational spectroscopy. Furthermore, using Porto notation, we establish polarization- and crystal-orientation-dependent Raman selection rules for different backscattering geometries and identify the modes that are expected to emerge or vanish under specific experimental configurations. Thus, our results provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for interpreting Raman and infrared measurements of CuInP2S6 and for reliably identifying its competing structural, ferroelectric, and antiferroelectric phases across temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions.

</details>


### [94] [Angle-Resolved Photoemission as a Sensitive Probe of Surface Quality: Sputtered versus Annealed Cu(001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16421)
*Hyeonhee Na,Jeonghwan Oh,Jinju Lee,Hanjun Sim,Myeongun Go,Jimin Sohn,Jeonggu Kang,Byungmin Sohn*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过对比 Cu(001) 单晶在不同表面处理阶段的 LEED、XPS 与 ARPES 结果，揭示了溅射表面尽管 LEED 和核能级谱看似干净有序，但其 ARPES 能带色散消失，说明 LEED 和 XPS 的"干净"并不能保证表面适合 ARPES 测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES 要求表面高度有序，而表面质量通常用 LEED 和 XPS 来判定；作者发现这一常规判据可能不足，因此想系统比较三种表征手段在不同表面状态下的响应，以检验 LEED/XPS 是否足以保证 ARPES 所需的表面质量。

Method: 作者对 Cu(001) 单晶在四种表面处理阶段——刚制备完、6 K 下老化 24 小时、Ar⁺ 溅射后、退火后——分别进行原位 LEED、XPS 与 ARPES 测量，并对比各阶段的衍射图案、Cu 2p 核能级谱以及能带色散。

Result: 溅射与退火后的表面呈现几乎相同的 (1×1) LEED 图案和 Cu 2p 核能级谱；但溅射表面的 ARPES 谱与动量无关（能带色散消失），退火后能带色散恢复。差异归因于各探针对溅射诱导缺陷的响应不同：LEED 反映仍保持周期性的平均晶格，而 ARPES 反映电子态，其晶体动量在缺陷作用下不再良定义。

Conclusion: 清晰的 LEED 图案和干净的核能级谱并不保证表面适合 ARPES 测量；在开展 ARPES 实验前不能仅依赖 LEED/XPS 判定表面质量。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) requires a well-ordered surface, whose quality is routinely judged from low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS). We compare LEED, XPS, and ARPES of a Cu(001) single crystal measured in situ at four stages of surface preparation: as-prepared, after aging for 24 h at 6 K, after Ar$^+$ sputtering, and after annealing. The sputtered and annealed surfaces show nearly identical $(1\times1)$ LEED patterns and Cu 2$p$ core levels, but the sputtered surface yields a momentum-independent ARPES spectrum, and the band dispersion recovers after annealing. We attribute this contrast to how each probe responds to sputtering-induced defects: LEED reflects the average lattice, which remains periodic, whereas ARPES reflects the electronic states, whose crystal momentum is no longer well defined. Our results indicate that a sharp LEED pattern and clean core-level spectra do not guarantee a surface suitable for ARPES.

</details>


### [95] [Decoupling Trap States in Amorphous Oxide Semiconductor Transistor Using a Conductive-Semiconducting-Conductive MOS Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16438)
*Kai Chen,Wei Zhang,Xuanyao Fong,Shengman Li,Yunlong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非晶氧化物半导体（AOS）的导电-半导体-导电（CSC）MOS测试结构，用于有效感知、区分和分析AOS晶体管中由不同陷阱态引起的电导损耗，克服了传统结构中金属-AOS非理想接触带来的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试结构中，非理想的金属-AOS接触会将各类陷阱态产生的电导损耗混叠在一起，难以区分界面态与浅能级态各自的贡献，从而无法准确表征陷阱对器件性能的影响。

Method: 设计CSC导电-半导体-导电MOS测试结构，消除接触相关伪影，从而解耦界面态与浅能级态的电导损耗；同时识别并校准泄漏电流、串联电阻和器件几何效应的影响，获得偏压分辨的陷阱分布曲线。

Result: 得到偏压分辨的陷阱分布，揭示三个不同区域：（1）耗尽区界面态主导电导损耗，其时间常数在亚微秒量级，密度随耗尽加深略有增加，关联施主型态；（2）弱至中等积累区浅能级态成为主要来源，呈现高斯型体密度态分布，响应时间从微秒到纳秒；（3）强积累区界面态重新占据主导，态数量增加但时间常数保持不变。

Conclusion: 偏压分辨的陷阱分布阐明了不同陷阱群体在不同工作条件下对AOS晶体管的影响，为理解可靠性退化提供了直观度量；CSC结构为AOS晶体管陷阱表征提供了一种简单、可扩展且稳健的平台。

Abstract: We propose a novel amorphous oxide semiconductor (AOS)-based conductive-semiconducting-conductive (CSC) MOS test structure to effectively sense, discriminate, and analyze distinct trap-induced conductance losses in AOS transistors. In conventional test structures, non-ideal metal-AOS contacts convolute the conductance loss generated by various trap states. The CSC architecture mitigates these contact-related artifacts, enabling the decoupling of interface states and shallow states. Furthermore, the contributions from leakage, series resistance, and device-geometry effects are identified and calibrated. Our results yield bias-resolved trap profiles and reveal three distinct regimes: (1) In the depletion region, interface states dominate the conductance loss. These states exhibit sub-microsecond time constants and their density increases slightly with deeper depletion, which is associated with donor-like states. (2) From weak to moderate accumulation, shallow states become the primary contributor, displaying a Gaussian-like volumetric density-of-states distribution with response times spanning from microseconds to nanoseconds. (3) In the strong accumulation regime, interface states regain dominance with an increased state quantity, while their time constants remain invariant. These bias-resolved profiles clarify how different trap populations impact AOS transistors under distinct operating conditions, providing intuitive metrics for understanding reliability degradation. Consequently, the CSC structure offers a simple, scalable, and robust platform for trap characterization in AOS transistors.

</details>


### [96] [Modelling the dynamics of the martensitic transformation in Ni$_2$MnGa with machine-learning force-fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16467)
*Markus E. Gruner,Yulia Klunnikova,Mike J. Bruckhoff,Konstatin P. Skokov,Olga N. Miroshkina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过构建由DFT训练的机器学习力场（ML-FF），克服了DFT在有限温度动力学模拟上的计算瓶颈，成功复现了Ni₂MnGa中软TA₂声子及纳米孪晶马氏体的偏好，并揭示了自适应纳米孪晶对奥氏体-马氏体相变双向动力学的促进作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ni₂MnGa等化学计量Heusler合金的马氏体相变受电子结构、磁性与晶格动力学之间的精细耦合支配。DFT虽能映射调制马氏体的能量景观，但在相关长度与时间尺度上模拟有限温度动力学时计算代价过高，亟需兼顾精度与效率的替代方案。

Method: 作者基于从头算分子动力学（ab initio molecular dynamics）数据，构建并插值出DFT训练的机器学习力场（ML-FF），随后利用经典分子动力学模拟，研究自适应纳米孪晶对奥氏体-马氏体相变的影响。

Result: ML-FF成功复现了Ni₂MnGa的关键特征，包括软TA₂声子模和对纳米孪晶马氏体的偏好；模拟表明自适应纳米孪晶促进了正、反两个方向的相变动力学；DFT在T=0下预测的整个相变路径上的负孪晶界能量，支持了有限温度下有序调制马氏体的形成。

Conclusion: 该工作为以近DFT精度开展磁性形状记忆合金（magnetic shape memory alloys）的大规模模拟开辟了道路，提供了一种兼顾精度与计算效率的研究途径。

Abstract: The martensitic transformations in stoichiometric Ni$_2$MnGa and related Heusler alloys are governed by a delicate interplay between electronic structure, magnetism, and lattice dynamics. While density functional theory (DFT) has successfully mapped the energy landscape of modulated martensites, it remains computationally prohibitive for capturing finite-temperature dynamics across relevant length and time scales. Here, we bridge this gap by constructing DFT-trained machine-learning force-fields (ML-FF) interpolated from ab initio molecular dynamics. We demonstrate that ML-FF trained on DFT data can reproduce key features of Ni$_2$MnGa, including the soft TA$_2$ phonon and a preference for nanotwinned martensites. Using classical molecular dynamics simulations, we explore the impact of adaptive nanotwinning on the austenite-martensite transition, which facilitates the dynamics of both, forward and backward transformation. We demonstrate, that the negative twin boundary energy along the entire transformations path predicted by DFT for $T=0$, supports the formation of ordered modulated martensites at finite temperatures. Our results pave the way for large-scale simulations of magnetic shape memory alloys with near-DFT accuracy.

</details>


### [97] [Crystal growth, magnetic, and transport properties of the candidate altermagnet Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17452)
*Nashra Pistawala,Martin R. Lees,Geetha Balakrishnan,Daniel A. Mayoh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文首次成功生长出高质单晶Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$，并通过X射线衍射、热学、磁学和输运测量，确认其六方晶系对称性及约141 K的反铁磁Néel温度，为实验研究交错磁性（altermagnetism）提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁性是凝聚态磁学中正在快速兴起的新范式，但目前已实验证实的交错磁候选材料仍然极为有限。Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$长期被理论预言为有前景的交错磁体，却缺乏单晶的实验研究。

Method: 成功生长高质量Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$单晶，并进行结构与物性表征：单晶X射线衍射确定晶体结构；电阻率与比热容测量确定磁相变温度；磁输运测量研究磁阻与霍尔响应。

Result: 单晶X射线衍射给出详细晶体学信息，确认六方晶系对称性（P6₃/mmc），这是交错磁行为判据之一。电阻率和比热测量明确给出反铁磁Néel温度T_N≈141 K。磁输运测量揭示磁阻和霍尔响应中的非平庸特征，指示量子扩散输运现象。

Conclusion: Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$首次以单晶形式合成，可作为实验平台用于探索交错磁材料所预言的各种丰富现象。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a rapidly emerging paradigm in condensed matter magnetism, yet the number of experimentally confirmed altermagnetic candidates remains remarkably limited. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ has long been theoretically proposed as a promising altermagnet, but there are no reports on experimental investigation of single crystals. Here, we report the successful growth of high-quality single crystals of Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ and present its structural, thermal, magnetic, and transport properties. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction provides detailed crystallographic information and confirms the hexagonal crystal symmetry ($P6_3/mmc$), one of the criteria for altermagnetic behavior. Resistivity and heat capacity measurements unambiguously identify an antiferromagnetic Néel temperature of $T_N \sim 141$ K. Magnetotransport measurements reveal non-trivial features in the magnetoresistance and Hall response, indicative of quantum diffusive transport phenomena. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$, synthesized here for the first time in single-crystalline form, therefore serves as an experimental platform for probing the rich spectrum of phenomena predicted for altermagnetic materials.

</details>


### [98] [Theoretical Investigation of Oxygen-Vacancy Diffusion in SrTiO$_3$ using Neural Network Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16521)
*Kazutaka Nishiguchi,Ryota Yamamoto,Seiichiro Ten-no*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于第一性原理数据的神经网络势（NNP），用于模拟SrTiO₃中氧空位的形成与扩散，在大规模、长时间尺度上获得了与实验高度一致的扩散系数和迁移焓（0.64 eV vs. 实验0.67 eV），显著优于经典分子动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧空位的形成与扩散决定SrTiO₃的电子与原子输运性质，但第一性原理分子动力学（AIMD）计算成本过高，限制了系统尺寸和时间尺度，难以直接研究氧空位动力学。

Method: 基于AIMD生成的参考数据集，训练适用于纯净及缺氧SrTiO₃的神经网络势（NNP）；验证NNP对DFT能量、原子力与维里的复现能力及超胞尺寸效应，随后进行大规模、长时间NNP分子动力学（NNP-MD）模拟，结合Arrhenius分析研究氧空位扩散。

Result: NNP准确复现了DFT参考能量、原子力和维里，并重现了总能量及氧空位形成能随超胞尺寸的变化（含稀释极限外推值）；NNP-MD得到的氧空位扩散系数与实验数据的一致性远优于传统经典MD，迁移焓为0.64 eV，与实验值0.67 eV接近。

Conclusion: 基于第一性原理数据训练的神经网络势为钙钛矿氧化物中的缺陷形成与长时间空位输运研究提供了准确且计算高效的框架。

Abstract: Oxygen-vacancy formation and diffusion play important roles in determining the electronic and atomic transport properties of SrTiO$_3$ (STO). Accurate analysis of such point defects requires sufficiently large-scale and long-time molecular dynamics (MD) simulations; however, direct investigations of oxygen-vacancy dynamics using {\it ab initio} MD (AIMD) based on density functional theory (DFT) are limited to relatively small systems and short time scales because of their high computational cost. In this study, we develop a neural network potential (NNP) for pristine and oxygen-deficient STO using a reference dataset generated from AIMD simulations. The trained NNP model accurately reproduces the DFT reference energies, atomic forces, and virials for both pristine STO and STO containing an oxygen vacancy. The NNP also reproduces the supercell-size dependence of the total energies and oxygen-vacancy formation energies, including the formation energy extrapolated to the dilute limit. Large-scale and long-time MD simulations using the NNP (NNP-MD) are then performed to investigate oxygen-vacancy diffusion. The resulting oxygen-vacancy diffusion coefficients show substantially better agreement with experimental data than those obtained from conventional classical MD simulations. An Arrhenius analysis yields an oxygen-vacancy migration enthalpy of 0.64 eV, in close agreement with the experimental value of 0.67 eV. These results demonstrate that NNPs trained on first-principles data provide an accurate and computationally efficient framework for investigating defect formation and long-time vacancy transport in perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [99] [Interlayer Registry Controls Polar Topology in Twisted Bilayer h-BN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16528)
*Di Fan,Changming Ke,Shi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理训练的深度势能分子动力学模拟，系统揭示了扭转双层h-BN中莫尔极化拓扑如何由层间堆叠配准（registry）控制，并随扭转角、温度和应变演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转范德华双层可产生周期性变化的层间堆叠配准，从而诱导空间图案化的极化，但此前缺乏对配准控制的极化拓扑随扭转角、温度和机械应变演化规律的系统理解。

Method: 采用基于第一性原理训练的深度势能分子动力学模拟，研究不同扭转角、温度及应变下的莫尔极化织构；并提出失配矩阵配准映射（mismatch-matrix registry-mapping）模型，用于解释形变引起的极化拓扑重构。

Result: 随扭转角减小，莫尔极化织构从紧凑的近圆形涡旋状演变为扩展的三角形AB/BA畴，原因在于小扭转角下晶格弛豫增强，扩展了低能、反向极化的AB/BA区域并将面内极化限制在畴界；尽管如此，局部面内缠绕仍与层间配准绑定，并在高达1000 K下保持，显示拓扑对热涨落的稳健性。均匀形变仅引起弱畸变，而层选择性形变则强烈重构莫尔极化织构；失配矩阵模型能解释大扭转角下涡旋半径及小扭转角下三角形畴几何的形变响应。

Conclusion: 层间配准是形成与调控扭转双层h-BN莫尔极化拓扑的核心控制参数，为设计极化拓扑结构提供了系统性依据。

Abstract: Twisting van der Waals bilayers creates a moir'e stacking field with periodically varying local interlayer registry, enabling spatially patterned polarization. However, a systematic understanding of how this registry-controlled polar topology evolves with twist angle, temperature, and mechanical strain is still lacking. Here, using first-principles-trained deep-potential molecular dynamics simulations, we show that spatially varying interlayer registry governs the polarization texture and can be directly tuned by twist and relative layer deformation. As the twist angle decreases, the moir'e polarization texture evolves from compact, nearly circular vortex-like patterns to extended triangular AB/BA domains. This crossover arises from enhanced lattice relaxation at small twist angles, which expands low-energy, oppositely polarized AB/BA regions and confines in-plane polarization mainly to domain boundaries. Despite this reconstruction, the local in-plane winding remains tied to interlayer registry and persists up to 1000 K, indicating robust topology against thermal fluctuations. We further show that the mechanical response is dictated by whether deformation changes the relative registry: homogeneous deformation of both layers produces only weak distortions, whereas layer-selective deformation strongly reconstructs the moiré polarization texture. A mismatch-matrix registry-mapping model is developed to capture this response, explaining deformation-induced changes in vortex radii at larger twist angles and triangular-domain geometry at smaller twist angles. These results identify interlayer registry as the central control parameter for forming and tuning moir'e polar topology in twisted bilayer h-BN.

</details>


### [100] [Coherent Phonon Generation from Prethermal Carrier Populations in a Semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18279)
*Mingyu Shi,Matteo Savoini,Patrick Friebel,Andrea Annunziata,Thomas Metzger,Yunpei Deng,Laura Cattaneo,Steven L. Johnson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
