TL;DR: 本文通过系统测量与第一性原理计算，研究了碲中飞秒激光激发波长跨越带隙时相干光学声子的产生，发现完全对称A₁模的相干振幅随波长变化微弱，与“热化载流子”模型的预期相反；其光谱依赖可由跃迁分辨的预热力（prethermal force）模型重现，表明跃迁选择性而非热化总载流子密度是近带隙激发下相干声子产生的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，半导体中超快激光激发产生的完全对称相干光学声子通常被理解为热化瞬态载流子群的结果。作者旨在检验这一普遍认知是否成立，尤其是在激发波长跨越电子带隙附近时，载流子热化假设是否仍然有效。

Method: 作者以碲为模型体系，采用激发波长跨越带隙的系统性超快激光测量，结合第一性原理计算，获取相干光学声子的光子能量依赖；并提出一个跃迁分辨的预热力模型，该模型由光学矩阵元以及带和动量依赖的A₁形变势加权，用以拟合实验数据并提取激发跃迁态寿命。

Result: 实验发现完全对称A₁模的相干振幅在波长跨带隙时仅微弱变化，与热化载流子模型预期相悖；测量到的光谱依赖可由跃迁分辨预热力模型准确重现，拟合得到激发跃迁态寿命仅为数飞秒。因此，相干声子产生主要由跃迁选择性控制，而非热化后的总载流子密度。

Conclusion: 跃迁选择性（transition selectivity）是近带隙激发下半导体相干声子产生的关键控制因素，而非传统认为的热化总载流子密度；这一结论修正了对超快激光激发半导体中相干声子产生机制的理解。

Abstract: Ultrafast laser excitation of carriers in semiconductors often results in the creation of fully symmetric coherent optical phonons, typically understood in terms of a thermalized transient carrier population. We test this understanding with systematic measurements and first-principles calculations of the photon-energy dependence of ultrafast-laser-driven coherent phonon generation in tellurium, using excitation wavelengths around the electronic band gap. We observe that, contrary to the expectations of models assuming a thermalized carrier population, the coherent amplitude of the fully symmetric $A_1$ mode changes only weakly when tuning wavelength across the band gap. The measured spectral dependence is instead reproduced by a transition-resolved prethermal force weighted by optical matrix elements and band- and momentum-dependent $A_1$ deformation potentials. Fitting this model to the data yields an excited transition state lifetime of a few femtoseconds. These results identify transition selectivity, rather than a thermalized total carrier density, as a key factor controlling coherent-phonon generation in semiconductors excited near a band gap.

</details>


### [101] [Real-space obstruction control of the shift current in two-dimensional Quantum Spin Hall insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16544)
*Michele Bagaglini,Mattia Iannetti,Jonas Erhardt,Jörg Schäfer,Ralph Claessen,Giorgio Sangiovanni,Gianni Profeta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究三角晶格量子自旋霍尔绝缘体中的体光伏效应，发现受阻拓扑相（obstructed topological phases）尽管能带结构相似，却表现出显著增强的位移电流（shift current）响应，并将增强归因于不同万尼尔中心扇区态间的光学跃迁，最终指出铟烯（indenene）是潜在的实现材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找具有大体光伏效应的材料近来已扩展到能在红外波段工作的窄带隙体系，有望用于光电子器件；位移电流因其几何起源不仅取决于光跃迁概率，还取决于伴随光激发的实空间位移，因此具有阻塞电子波函数的非中心对称材料尤其有前景。然而，实空间电子阻塞如何具体调控位移电流仍缺乏系统理解。

Method: 作者在三角晶格上构建量子自旋霍尔绝缘体的紧束缚模型，遍历平凡相、量子自旋霍尔相和高阶拓扑绝缘相，计算这三种拓扑相下的非线性光电流（位移电流），并对比它们在能带结构几乎相同情况下的响应差异。

Result: 研究发现，尽管电子能带结构相似，受阻拓扑相表现出大幅增强的位移电流响应。增强的根源在于属于不同万尼尔中心扇区的态之间的光学跃迁，这类跃迁允许光激发时产生较大的实空间位移；且位移电流的符号取决于拓扑相类型。

Conclusion: 该工作将可控体光伏效应的探索扩展到具有实空间电子阻塞特征的材料，并指出 SiC(0001) 上的单层铟（铟烯，indenene）是实现这一现象的有前景候选材料。

Abstract: The search for materials exhibiting a large bulk photovoltaic effect has recently expanded to low-band-gap systems capable of operating in the infrared regime, with potential applications in optoelectronics. Among the microscopic mechanisms contributing to this phenomenon, the shift current is one of the dominant sources of photocurrent generation. Owing to its geometric origin, the shift current is not only determined by the optical transition probability but also by the real-space displacement associated with the optical excitation, making non-centrosymmetric materials with obstructed electronic wave functions particularly promising candidates. In this work, using a tight-binding model of a quantum spin Hall insulator on a triangular lattice, we investigate the nonlinear photocurrent across trivial, quantum spin Hall, and higher-order topological insulating phases. We show that obstructed topological phases exhibit a substantially enhanced shift-current response, despite the similar electronic band structure. We identify the origin of this enhancement in optical transitions between states belonging to distinct Wannier-center sectors, which allow for a large real-space displacement upon photoexcitation and, depending on the topological phase, control the sign of the shift current. Our results extend the search for controllable bulk photovoltaic effects to materials characterized by real-space electronic obstruction and identify indenene, a monolayer of In on SiC(0001), as a promising candidate for realizing this phenomenon.

</details>


### [102] [Domain-wall-to-domain interconversion in a polar conducting oxide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16681)
*Martin F. Sarott,Majid Ahmadi,Johanna van Gent González,Ewout van der Veer,Mads C. Weber,Jacopo Pinna,Daniel A. Chaney,Arijit Sinha,Hamideh Hassani,Horatio R. J. Cox,Thomas Perrault,Amir Sabet Ghorabaei,Maria A. Loi,Philippe Ghosez,Bart J. Kooi,Beatriz Noheda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示在极性外延三氧化钨（WO₃）中，将纳米尺度的畴壁可逆地扩展为三维功能区域，实现畴壁与畴之间的相互转换，从而在极性(畴)与导电性(畴壁)两种互斥属性间进行可逆空间调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁因对称性与畴内部不同而具备宿主材料中本不允许的功能，但其被严格限制在二维界面上，阻碍了对畴壁本质的揭示并限制了其应用，因此需要突破维度限制。

Method: 利用导电、非极性的畴壁具有母相结构这一特性，通过扫描探针针尖施加交流电场产生局域加热，将该畴壁相扩展进入相邻极性畴中，从而将其从二维畴壁扩展为三维功能区域。

Result: 实现了畴壁到畴的可逆相互转换，以及纳米尺度畴壁向三维功能区域的可逆维度控制，可在极性(畴)与导电性(畴壁)两种互斥属性之间进行可逆空间切换。

Conclusion: 该畴壁-畴相互转换方法为可重构氧化物电子学提供了一种新的范式，赋予器件可逆空间调控的能力。

Abstract: Domain walls in ferroic crystals are topological defects with a symmetry distinct from the domain interior, allowing them to display functionalities that are otherwise forbidden in the host material. Despite their unique properties, domain walls are strictly confined to two-dimensional interfaces, which hinders unveiling their true nature and limits their applicability. Here, we demonstrate reversible dimensionality control by expanding nanoscale domain walls into three-dimensional functional regions in polar epitaxial tungsten trioxide ($\text{WO}_3$). This domain-wall-to-domain interconversion is enabled by the conducting, non-polar domain walls adopting the parent-phase structure. An alternating electric field applied through a scanning probe tip induces localized heating, which expands this domain-wall phase into the adjacent polar domains. This approach enables reversible spatial control to switch between mutually exclusive properties of domains (polarity) and domain walls (conductivity), offering a new paradigm for reconfigurable oxide electronics.

</details>


### [103] [Engineering Ferroelectric Phases in BaTiO$_3$ Films via Buffer-Layer Induced Misfit Strain Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16766)
*A. S. Mikheykin,N. V. Lyanguzov,A. S. Konstantinov,V. B. Shirokov,E. D. Linnik,T. Bondar,A. G. Razumnaya*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过薄膜导电缓冲层（LSMO）能够改变MgO衬底上BaTiO₃薄膜的有效应变状态，从而调控其铁电相序与极化构型，为定制极化纹理的氧化物异质结构提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过应变工程控制铁电相对称性对氧化物器件中极化拓扑和功能特性的调控至关重要，但衬底诱导的应变往往难以灵活调节，需要寻找新的手段来覆盖或反转衬底应变。

Method: 在MgO(001)衬底上沉积500 nm BaTiO₃薄膜，并插入50 nm La₀.₆₇Sr₀.₃₃MnO₃（LSMO）导电缓冲层；利用变温偏振拉曼光谱、X射线衍射和压电力显微镜（PFM）等手段，对比有无缓冲层时的应变状态与相变序。

Result: 尽管MgO(001)晶格参数预期诱导张性失配应变，但在500 nm BTO薄膜中热膨胀失配占主导，冷却时产生有效压应力；插入LSMO缓冲层后，有效应变由压缩反转为拉伸。降温过程中出现非常规相序：顺电→正交相（aa相，约407 K）→单斜相（r相，约300–345 K），表现为面内及倾斜极化分量。LSMO既作为底电极，又改变应变状态，界面电荷重新分布诱导内建电场，体现为自极化和不对称PFM电滞回线。

Conclusion: 导电缓冲层在控制铁电对称性方面起关键作用，可实现具有定制极化纹理的氧化物异质结构，有望应用于低功耗自旋电子学、可调微波器件和神经形态计算。

Abstract: Controlling ferroelectric phase symmetry through strain engineering is crucial for tailoring polarization topology and functional properties in oxide-based devices. Here, we show that a thin conductive buffer layer can override substrate-induced strain in BaTiO$_3$ (BTO) films on MgO. Although the lattice parameter of MgO(001) would be expected to induce tensile misfit strain, in 500~nm BaTiO$_3$ films the thermal-expansion mismatch dominates, resulting in effective compressive stress upon cooling. Inserting a 50-nm La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$ (LSMO) buffer layer reverses the effective strain state from compressive to tensile. Using temperature-dependent polarized Raman spectroscopy, X-ray diffraction, and piezoresponse force microscopy, we reveal an unconventional phase sequence upon cooling: paraelectric $\rightarrow$ orthorhombic ($\textit{aa}$-phase, $\sim$407 K) $\rightarrow$ monoclinic ($\textit{r}$-phase, $\sim$300-345 K), characterized in-plane and tilted polarization components. The LSMO layer not only serves as a bottom electrode but also modifies the strain state, while interfacial charge redistribution induces a built-in electric field, evidenced by self-polarization and asymmetric PFM hysteresis. These findings highlight the critical role of conductive buffer layers in controlling ferroelectric symmetry and enabling oxide heterostructures with tailored polarization textures for low-power spintronics, tunable microwave devices, and neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [104] [Molecular Precursor Chemistry Shapes Early-Stage Reaction Pathways in Nitrogen-Doped Carbon Nanotube Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16831)
*Narayan N. Som,Lukasz Nowicki,Tomasz Gizewski,Karolina Z. Milowska,Anna Wroblewska,Caghan Tuncer,Maciej Trzaskowski,Olga Iwasinska-Kowalska,Tomasz Plocinski,Mariusz Zdrojek,Krzysztof K. Koziol,Malgorzata Jakubowska,Agnieszka Lekawa-Raus*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与反应分子动力学模拟，揭示了含氮前驱体（吡啶、吡嗪）在碳纳米管成核前的催化剂形成初期（前驱体分解的头几纳秒内）即已影响其最终结构，且氮掺杂量与供氮量并非简单正比关系，前驱体化学起着关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对碳纳米管结构特性的讨论多聚焦于已成形的催化剂纳米颗粒上的成核与后续生长过程，作者希望证明碳纳米管的结构轨迹可以更早地开始——即前驱体分解与催化剂形成的最初几纳秒内，并阐明前驱体化学对早期催化剂演化的影响。

Method: 将无氮碳纳米管合成与两种结构相近的芳香族含氮前驱体（吡啶，每分子含1个氮原子；吡嗪，每分子含2个氮原子）进行对比，采用实验表征与反应分子动力学（ReaxFF）模拟相结合的方法，考察前驱体分解路径、Fe-碳与Fe-SiO2相互作用及催化剂演化过程。

Result: 实验上，两种含氮前驱体均促进竹节状（bamboo-like）碳纳米管形成，并产生比无氮参照物更多结构无序的碳纳米管；氮掺入量并不随供氮量线性增加，表明前驱体化学贡献显著。反应分子动力学模拟显示，含氮前驱体在催化剂演化过程中显著改变了局部Fe-碳和Fe-SiO2相互作用；吡啶与吡嗪呈现不同的分解路径，且吡嗪在模拟时间尺度内分解程度显著更高。

Conclusion: 前驱体化学影响局部化学环境与早期催化剂演化，凸显了其在碳纳米管生长初始阶段中的潜在作用，说明碳纳米管的结构轨迹在前驱体分解和催化剂形成的最初阶段就已开始。

Abstract: The structural characteristics of carbon nanotubes are usually discussed in terms of processes occurring during nanotube nucleation and subsequent growth from an already formed catalyst nanoparticle. Here, we show that their structural trajectory can begin much earlier, within the first nanoseconds of precursor decomposition and catalyst formation. We combine experiment and reactive molecular dynamics to compare nitrogen-free CNT synthesis with two closely related aromatic nitrogen precursors, pyridine and pyrazine, containing one and two nitrogen atoms per molecule, respectively. Experimentally, both nitrogen-containing precursors promote bamboo-like CNT formation and produce substantially more structurally disordered CNTs than the nitrogen-free reference. Notably, nitrogen incorporation does not scale simply with the amount of nitrogen supplied, demonstrating a strong contribution from precursor chemistry. Reactive molecular dynamics reveals that nitrogen-containing precursors significantly modify local Fe-carbon and Fe-SiO2 interactions during catalyst evolution, while pyridine and pyrazine exhibit distinct decomposition pathways, with pyrazine showing significantly greater decomposition within the simulated timescale. These results demonstrate that precursor chemistry influences the local chemical environment and early catalyst evolution, highlighting its potential role in the initial stages of CNT growth.

</details>


### [105] [Trust-Limited Machine-Learning-Accelerated Nudged Elastic Band Calculations for Small-Polaron Hopping in Rutile TiO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16850)
*Ashish Kumar Singh,Irina V. Lebedeva*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种高斯过程辅助的NEB工作流，用于计算小极化子跳跃路径，并在金红石TiO2体系中验证了其可重复的势垒与全带宽力收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小极化子跳跃的NEB计算极具挑战性，因为核运动与自洽局域电子态耦合；在电荷转移区域附近，自洽场收敛缓慢、局域化切换以及电子解相互竞争，使代理辅助优化难以进行。

Method: 提出高斯过程辅助NEB工作流，采用联合能量-力的高斯过程（Matérn协方差模型）推进弹性带，并以活性原子均方根位移和最大原子位移限制约束代理运动；达到限值时触发第一性原理采样，无需代理完全收敛。此外，在15-image计算中通过对中心镜像重复采样激活局部GP。

Result: 三个独立的DFT验证的96原子[001]计算（11、13、15个镜像）分别得到0.1645、0.1637和0.1640 eV的势垒，跨度仅0.793 meV；15-image计算的最大投影NEB力为0.0346 eV/Å。324原子计算得到更低的0.1302 eV势垒，表明存在超胞敏感性。

Conclusion: 该工作流结合受控代理外推、定向电荷转移区域插值和独立DFT验证，可获得可重复的势垒和全带宽力收敛的小极化子迁移路径，并展示了对更大GP观测维度的可行性。

Abstract: Nudged elastic band (NEB) calculations of small-polaron hopping are demanding because nuclear motion is coupled to a self-consistent localized electronic state. Near the charge-transfer region, slow self-consistent-field convergence, localization switching, and competing electronic solutions complicate surrogate-assisted optimization. We develop a Gaussian-process-assisted NEB workflow for this regime and demonstrate electron small-polaron hopping in rutile TiO2 using spin-polarized density functional theory with a Hubbard U correction (DFT+U ) with SIESTA. A joint energy-force Gaussian process (GP) with a Matérn covariance model advances the elastic band, while active-atom root-mean-square and maximum-atom displacement limits constrain surrogate motion relative to evaluated configurations. Reaching these limits triggers first-principles sampling without requiring full surrogate convergence. Three independently DFT-verified 96-atom [001] calculations using 11, 13, and 15 images and different surrogate settings and GP parameterizations yield barriers of 0.1645, 0.1637, and 0.1640 eV, respectively, spanning 0.793 meV. In the 15-image calculation, repeated central-image sampling activates a separately calibrated local GP during surrogate optimization. Final full-band DFT verification gives a maximum projected NEB force of 0.0346 eV/Å. A 324-atom calculation yields a lower barrier of 0.1302 eV, indicating supercell sensitivity and demonstrating feasibility at larger GP observation dimension. The workflow combines controlled surrogate extrapolation, targeted charge-transfer-region interpolation, and independent DFT verification to obtain polaron migration pathways, emphasizing reproducible barriers and full-band force convergence.

</details>


### [106] [First-Principles Calculations of the Electron-Hole-Pair Creation Energy in Semiconductors and Dielectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16949)
*Dallin O. Nielsen,Massimo V. Fischetti*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算结合全带蒙特卡罗模拟，研究了AlN、3C-SiC、4H-SiC、Si和SiO2等半导体及电介质中电子-空穴对产生能量，并验证了GW/BSE方法可显著提高Si的计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-空穴对产生能量是辐射探测、半导体器件等领域的核心参数，但现有计算（尤其对Si）与实验值存在约14.9%的偏差，需要更精确的理论方法来解释并改进这一偏差。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算能带结构，密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）计算声子色散，含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）计算介电函数及电子能量损失函数；在此基础上进行能量高达约100 eV的全带蒙特卡罗电子热化模拟，计算平均电子-空穴对产生能量。针对Si进一步采用GW近似与Bethe-Salpeter方程（BSE）计算准粒子能带修正及介电函数，以提高精度。

Result: 计算得到AlN、3C-SiC、4H-SiC、Si和SiO2的电子-空穴对产生能量分别约为12.2、6.5、7.3、3.10和16.7 eV/pair，与实验数据吻合良好，其中Si偏差最大（约比实验值3.6 eV/pair低14.9%）。采用GW/BSE后，Si的产生能量提升至约3.50 eV/pair，精度显著改善；改善主要源于GW/BSE方法使低于约10-15 eV能量区间的电子能量损失函数幅度略微下降。

Conclusion: 电子-空穴对产生能量主要由声子散射率与电离率交点附近的声子发射与电离竞争决定，另一关键因素是能量损失函数中等离子体峰的位置与形状；GW/BSE方法通过对低能区介电响应的修正有效提高了计算精度。

Abstract: We present first-principles calculations of the electron-hole-pair creation energy in semiconductors and dielectrics, focusing in particular on AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2. We have employed density functional theory (DFT) to calculate the band structure, density-functional perturbation theory to obtain the phonon dispersion, and time-dependent DFT (TDDFT) to calculate the electron energy loss function (via the dielectric function). We performed full-band Monte Carlo simulations of electron thermalization for energies up to ~100 eV and calculated the average energy required to excite an electron-hole pair. We calculated pair-creation energies of approximately 12.2, 6.5, 7.3, 3.10, and 16.7 eV/pair for AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2, respectively. These values are in good agreement with measured data, with the largest discrepancy being that of Si (about 14.9% lower than the accepted experimental value of ~3.6 eV/pair). To improve the agreement for Si, we used the GW approach and the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) to calculate the quasiparticle shifts of the electronic band structure and the dielectric function, respectively. Finally, using the GW/BSE data, we calculated a creation energy of ~3.50 eV/pair. We find that the marked improvement in the accuracy of the calculated creation energy, when using the GW/BSE approach, is primarily attributable to the slight decrease in the magnitude of the electron energy-loss function when using the GW/BSE approach for energies below ~10-15 eV. Overall, it is clear that the competition between phonon emission and ionization near the intersection of the phonon scattering and ionization rates is largely responsible for determining the value of the creation energy, with the other major factor being the location and shape of the plasmon peak in the energy-loss function.

</details>


### [107] [Capturing Coordination Core Topology in Metal--Organic Frameworks Using Persistent Mayer Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17056)
*Caleb Simiyu Khaemba,Chun-Long Chen,Guo-Wei Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出持续Mayer同调（PMH）方法，用于捕捉金属-有机框架（MOFs）与多金属氧酸盐（POMs）中局部配位核心的几何变异性，并通过与元素分辨持续同调（ERPH）结合，显著提升了对八种氮氧相关吸附和自扩散性质的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部配位核心对MOFs和POMs的化学与结构性质至关重要，但现有方法（如ERPH）难以将局部配位核心转化为定量、可扩展且可解释的信息，且经典拓扑无法检测其几何变异性，亟需新的拓扑表征工具。

Method: 作者引入持续Mayer同调（PMH），聚焦于由周期性金属中心的重原子、碳和给体通道构成的配位核心，区分局部配位环境、连接子框架和潜在配位原子的不同排布。进一步考察了可解释的N=3和N=5阶PMH，并分别与刻画扩展框架中元素基团多尺度组织的ERPH结合，用于预测八种氮气和氧气相关的吸附与自扩散性质。

Result: 配位核心PMH表征相比单独使用ERPH持续提升预测性能，其中三阶Mayer表征整体性能最强。这表明PMH捕捉的内部配位构型含有传统元素分辨拓扑描述子未能充分代表的关键结构信息。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解局部配位环境提供了一种定量且可解释的拓扑框架，有望促进MOFs及POM基材料的理性设计。

Abstract: Local coordination cores are critically important in metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) and polyoxometalates (POMs), as they govern the fundamental chemical and structural properties of these materials. Existing methods, including element-resolved persistent homology (ERPH), have limited ability to translate local coordination cores into quantitative, scalable, and interpretable information. Here, we introduce persistent Mayer homology (PMH) to enable the detection of the geometric variability invisible by classical topology. By focusing on coordination cores formed by periodic metal-centered heavy-atom, carbon, and donor channels, PMH distinguishes different arrangements of the local coordination environment, linker framework, and potential coordinating atoms. We examine explainable PMH of orders $N=3$ and $N=5$ and separately combine each with ERPH, which characterizes the multiscale organization of chemically defined element groups across the extended framework, to predict eight nitrogen- and oxygen-related adsorption and self-diffusion properties. The resulting coordination-core PMH representations consistently improve predictive performance over ERPH alone, with the third-order Mayer representation providing the strongest overall performance. These results demonstrate that the inner coordination configurations captured by PMH contain essential structural information that is not fully represented by conventional element-resolved topological descriptors. This finding provides a quantitative interpretable topological framework for understanding local coordination environments and may facilitate the rational design of MOFs and POM-based materials.

</details>


### [108] [Symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl points in nonmagnetic crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17071)
*Ze-Xin Xue,Ke-Xin Pang,Yun-Yun Bai,Yanfeng Ge,Yan Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了非磁性晶体中电荷三Weyl点(C-3 WPs)与电荷一Weyl点(C-1 WPs)共存的新型拓扑构型，并通过对称性分析与第一性原理计算给出具体材料实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高电荷Weyl半金属(尤其蕴含C-3 WPs的体系)因非常规拓扑性质与增强的边界效应而备受关注，但理想的C-3 WSMs十分罕见；更重要的是，非磁性晶体中C-3与C-1 WP共存所受到的对称性约束尚未被系统揭示。

Method: 作者引入一种混合型"不可约Weyl分子"{3,-1,-1,-1}构型作为研究对象，结合拓扑电荷守恒定律与空间群对称性分析，对所有230个空间群做系统分类；并利用第一性原理计算与表面态计算，在两种硼同素异形体上加以验证。

Result: 在无自旋轨道耦合(SOC)情形下识别出12个候选空间群，含SOC情形下识别出23个候选群。无SOC计算表明硼同素异形体TBR-B48实现沙漏型C-3 WP，而手性OBD-B48实现非沙漏型C-3 WP；表面态计算显示C-3对的共享投影处有6支费米弧，单个投影处各有3支。反转OBD-B48的结构手性会反转所有Weyl电荷符号，建立晶体手性与动量空间手性的直接对应关系。此外，破坏TBR-B48中的保护性C6对称会使每个C-3 WP劈裂为3个C-1 WP，并重构表面费米弧。

Conclusion: 该工作为非磁性Weyl半金属中C-3与C-1 WP共存体系的设计与搜索提供了基于对称性的指导原则，并通过具体材料验证了理论分类的可行性。

Abstract: High-charge Weyl semimetals (WSMs), particularly those hosting charge-three Weyl point (C-3 WPs), have attracted considerable interest owing to their unconventional topological properties and enhanced boundary phenomena. However, ideal C-3 WSMs remain rare, and the symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl point (C-1 WPs) in nonmagnetic crystals remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate a representative eight-node Weyl configuration, consisting of two C-3 WPs and six C-1 WPs with opposite chirality, corresponding to two identical $\{3,-1,-1,-1\}$ irreducible Weyl molecules. By combining topological charge conservation with space-group symmetry analysis, we systematically classify all 230 space groups and identify 12 and 23 candidate groups without and with spin--orbit coupling (SOC), respectively. First-principles calculations without SOC reveal that the boron allotropes TBR-B$_{48}$ and chiral OBD-B$_{48}$ realize this configuration, hosting hourglass-type and non-hourglass-type C-3 WPs, respectively. Surface-state calculations reveal six Fermi-arc branches at the shared projection of the charge-three pair and three at each individual projection. Reversing the structural chirality of OBD-B$_{48}$ reverses the signs of all Weyl charges, establishing a direct correspondence between crystal and momentum-space chirality. Furthermore, breaking the protecting $C_6$ symmetry in TBR-B$_{48}$ splits each C-3 WP into three C-1 WPs and reconstructs the surface Fermi arcs. Our work establishes symmetry-based guidelines for designing and searching for nonmagnetic WSMs hosting charge-three and charge-one Weyl-point coexistence.

</details>


### [109] [Comprehensive Workflow for Uncertainty-Aware Ionic Liquid Solvent Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17171)
*Alexander Lobo,Urvi Awasthi,Leonid Zhukov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将数据处理、深度学习建模与物理计算耦合的工作流，用于离子液体的性质预测与最优候选筛选，并通过CO2捕集场景验证了其在稀缺、噪声及分布外数据下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子液体因可通过改变阴阳离子组合实现性质"可调"，是设计目标性质材料的理想对象；但现有数据驱动性质模型在数据稀缺、噪声大、覆盖不全的情况下泛化能力差，分布外筛选脆弱——即使点预测看似合理，也难以一致比较候选、量化下游影响或信任排序，难以指导分子发现。

Method: 提出一个将数据处理、深度学习建模与基于物理的计算视为耦合系统的工作流，把性质预测与不确定性估计、基于物理的过滤相结合；通过收紧预测性质与降选准则之间的联系，支持可靠的分布外排序，并优先筛选出预测权衡有利的未测试离子液体候选。

Result: 将该工作流应用于设计用于烟气CO2捕集的新型离子液体，预测其性质并基于CO2溶解度、离子液体粘度和工作容量筛选出Pareto最优候选。

Conclusion: 该工作流通过耦合深度学习与物理约束，提升了离子液体在分布外场景下的性质预测可靠性与候选排序可信度，实现了对未测试候选的有效优先排序，为面向CO2捕集等目标性质的材料设计提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Designing synthesizable materials with targeted properties remains one of the central objectives in materials sciences. Ionic liquids (ILs) are well suited to this goal due to their "tunability", where physical and chemical properties can be precisely customized by modifying their molecular structure, specifically by varying the combination of cations and anions. At the same time, current data-driven property models for ionic liquids often degrade in scarce, noisy, and uneven data regimes, where measurements cover only a small fraction of chemical space and a limited range of thermodynamic conditions. This makes out-of-distribution screening brittle: even when point predictions look plausible, it is difficult to compare candidates consistently, quantify downstream implications, or trust rankings enough to guide molecular discovery. We present a property-prediction and optimal candidate selection workflow that treats data processing, deep learning modeling, and physics-based calculations as a coupled system, linking property prediction with uncertainty estimation and physics-based filtering. By tightening the link between predicted properties and the criteria used for down-selection, the workflow supports reliable out-of-distribution ranking and enables prioritization of previously untested ionic liquid candidates with favorable predicted trade-offs. We demonstrate the application of our workflow by designing novel ionic liquids for CO2 capture from flue gas, predicting their properties and selecting Pareto-optimal candidates based on CO2 solubility, IL viscosity and working capacity.

</details>


### [110] [Evidence of an anisotropic Griffiths phase in a two-dimensional van der Waals magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17315)
*Anupam K. Singh,Dhananjay Nair,Holger Meyerheim,Stuart S. P. Parkin,Banabir Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了在自插层范德华磁体 Fe5GeTe2 中发现的一种高度各向异性的 Griffiths 相：居里温度以上、Griffiths 温度以下，短程有序铁磁团簇嵌入顺磁基体中主导磁响应，且该非解析行为仅在面内磁场下出现，面外磁场会骤然改变这一行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年，二维范德华磁性研究主要集中于长程有序磁相，而紊乱（无序）磁相在很大程度上仍未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究范德华磁体中的无序驱动磁相及其与各向异性、降维效应的关系。

Method: 以自插层范德华磁体 Fe5GeTe2 为研究对象，通过磁化率异常幂律发散、普适 Lee-Yang 标度以及独特的熵异常等多重实验证据，表征居里温度与 Griffiths 温度之间由嵌入顺磁基体的短程有序铁磁团簇主导的 Griffiths 相，并系统比较面内与面外磁场下的响应差异。

Result: 在 Fe5GeTe2 中确认了高度各向异性的 Griffiths 相：磁化率呈现非解析幂律发散，满足 Lee-Yang 标度关系，并伴随明显熵异常。关键发现是该非解析 Griffiths 响应仅在面内磁场下出现，面外磁场会骤然改变该行为，表明纳米尺度团簇内的局域磁相互作用与层状晶格几何紧密耦合。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一种维度受限、无序驱动的磁性机制，并将自插层范德华磁体确立为研究磁无序、各向异性与降维三者相互作用的理想平台。

Abstract: Over the last decade, the emerging field of two-dimensional van der Waals magnetism has focused mainly on long-range-ordered magnetic phases. Disordered magnetic phases, however, remain largely unexplored. Here, we report a highly anisotropic Griffiths phase driven by self-intercalation in the van der Waals magnet Fe5GeTe2. We find evidence of short-range-ordered ferromagnetic clusters embedded in a paramagnetic matrix, which dominate the magnetic response between the Curie temperature and a higher characteristic Griffiths temperature. An anomalous power-law divergence in the magnetic susceptibility, universal Lee Yang scaling, and distinct entropy anomalies provide further evidence for this phase. Strikingly, the non-analytic Griffiths response emerges exclusively under in-plane magnetic fields, whereas out-of-plane fields abruptly alter this behavior. This directional dependence indicates that the local magnetic interactions within nanoscale clusters are closely coupled to the layered lattice geometry. Our findings establish a regime of dimensionally constrained, disorder-driven magnetism and identify self-intercalated van der Waals magnets as a platform for investigating the interplay between magnetic disorder, anisotropy, and reduced dimensionality.

</details>


### [111] [Can Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials Capture Small Polarons? A Geometry-Based Benchmark in Transition-Metal Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17352)
*Vladislav Korostelev,Pjotrs Žguns,Konstantin Klyukin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文系统考察了通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）能否描述过渡金属氧化物中小极化子的形成与行为，发现其表现高度依赖训练数据和电子关联处理，且普遍无法稳定空间分离的极化子-施主构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小极化子由过量电子经晶格畸变局域化形成，对过渡金属氧化物的电荷输运、催化和降解至关重要。通用机器学习原子间势缺乏显式的电子自由度，其能否刻画小极化子尚不清楚，因此有必要进行系统验证。

Method: 以氢诱导的电子局域化为研究对象，覆盖多种过渡金属氧化物；采用基于TM-O键长膨胀（Z-score）的几何描述符识别极化子畸变；对比不同uMLIPs与DFT+U预测，评估电子局域化程度、亚稳态小极化子以及耦合质子-极化子转移过程中的极化子跳跃势垒。

Result: uMLIPs表现出从离域态到亚稳态小极化子的不同程度的电子局域化，行为强烈依赖训练数据与电子关联处理；训练数据中缺少特定TM阳离子的Hubbard U修正会导致电子离域（与DFT+U相反）；部分uMLIPs能再现耦合质子-极化子转移的极化子跳跃势垒，但所有uMLIPs均无法稳定空间分离的极化子-施主构型，而倾向于在H掺杂剂附近局域化。

Conclusion: 通用机器学习原子间势目前对小极化子的描述存在局限：虽然可以部分再现跳跃势垒，但难以正确描述电子局域化与空间分离构型，其可靠性受训练数据和电子关联处理方式制约。

Abstract: Small polarons, formed when excess electrons become localized through lattice distortions, play a critical role in charge transport, catalysis, and degradation processes in transition-metal (TM) oxides. Whether universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs), which lack explicit electronic degrees of freedom, can describe them remains unclear. Here, we investigate H-induced electron localization across a range of TM oxides using a geometric descriptor based on TM-O bond-length expansion (Z-score) to identify polaronic distortions. We find that uMLIPs exhibit varying degrees of electron localization, from delocalized states to metastable small polarons, with their behavior strongly dependent on the training data and treatment of electronic correlations. In particular, the absence of Hubbard U corrections for specific TM cations in the training data leads to electron delocalization, in contrast to DFT+U predictions. Although some uMLIPs reproduce polaron hopping barriers during coupled proton-polaron transfer, all of them fail to stabilize spatially separated polaron-donor configurations, instead favoring localization near the H dopant.

</details>


### [112] [Collision Cascade Morphology and Defect Nucleation in Pure Tungsten and an Equimolar Refractory W-Ta-Cr-V Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17361)
*Mark Fedorov,Francisco Javier Dominguez-Gutierrez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过分子动力学模拟（PKA能量0.1–100 keV，采用tabGAP势并补充ZBL短程相互作用）研究纯W和WTaCrV难熔高熵合金中的碰撞级联与缺陷形成机制。结果表明，WTaCrV因更频繁的级联分裂（subcascade）以及Cr、V等轻元素以点缺陷形式容纳损伤，表现出比纯W更好的初级辐照损伤抗性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难熔高熵合金（如WTaCrV）被认为是核聚变等极端辐照环境下潜在的候选结构材料，但对其初级辐照损伤（碰撞级联与缺陷形成）的微观机理以及与纯W的对比尚缺乏系统理解，因而需要开展原子尺度模拟研究。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟，PKA能量范围0.1–100 keV；使用机器学习表格化的高斯近似势（tabGAP），并补充短程Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark（ZBL）相互作用以准确描述高能碰撞。通过分析热峰寿命、受影响区域、原子迁移和存活缺陷，考察级联分裂对能量分布、位错与空洞形核的影响，以及Cr、V等轻元素在间隙缺陷容纳中的作用。

Result: 级联分裂成亚级联使PKA能量分布在更大区域，从而形核更少的位错和更小的空洞（空洞仅存在于纯W中，WTaCrV中未出现）；级联分裂在WTaCrV中更频繁发生，因此该合金比纯W具有更好的初级辐照损伤抗性。此外，Cr（以及较小程度的V）作为更轻、更小的元素，以点缺陷形式容纳损伤，有助于抑制位错环的形成。

Conclusion: WTaCrV难熔高熵合金凭借更频繁的级联分裂以及轻元素（Cr、V）对点缺陷的容纳作用，展现出优于纯W的初级辐照损伤抗性，为进一步发展耐辐照难熔高熵合金提供了原子尺度的机理依据。

Abstract: Collision cascades and defect-formation mechanisms in pure W and the WTaCrV refractory high-entropy alloy were investigated using molecular dynamics simulations over primary knock-on atom (PKA) energies of 0.1-100 keV. Machine-learning tabulated Gaussian approximation potentials (tabGAP), complemented by short-range Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) interactions, were employed. Analysis of the heat spike lifetime, affected region, atomic relocations and surviving defects reveals an importance of subcascade formation and presence of light elements in the alloy. Cascade splitting into subcascades contributes to the distribution of the PKA energy over a larger region in the material, nucleating less dislocations and smaller voids - latter being present only in W and absent in WTaCrV. Since cascade splitting is more frequently observed in WTaCrV, the alloy shows better resistance to primary irradiation damage compared to pure W. Apart from cascade splitting, analysis of interstitials shows that Cr and to lesser extent V, being lighter and smaller elements, accommodate the damage in form of point defects, contributing to the mitigation of formation of the dislocation loops.

</details>


### [113] [High-Resolution X-ray Spectro-Microscopy of Au Microcrystallites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17417)
*Chaitali Sow,Martin Seyrich,Johannes Hagemann,Giridhar U. Kulkarni,Thomas F. Keller,Christian G. Schroer,Andreas Schropp*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用X射线谱学叠层成像（spectro-ptychography）在金L3边（~12 keV）以上能量对金微晶实现高分辨率纳米级化学成像，同时获得振幅与相位对比，空间分辨率达45 nm，并验证了该方法对高能段功能材料化学成像的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非均相催化剂的性能需要高分辨率纳米级化学成像工具。X射线谱学叠层成像可通过XANES提供振幅与相位对比的高空间分辨成像，但在10 keV以上能量（如Au L边）的演示仍受限于X射线吸收截面弱、信号水平低。

Method: 对同时含有常规面心立方（fcc）与非传统非立方晶格的金微晶（厚度150 nm至1.71 μm）进行多模态谱学显微成像，横跨Au L3边解析振幅与相位对比，实现45 nm空间分辨率并定量提取~12 keV处的反常相位变化。

Result: 在薄金微晶上成功以45 nm空间分辨率解析L3边两侧的振幅与相位对比；所得XANES谱与传统透射和荧光对比高度一致，同时以更高空间分辨率提供了互补的化学信息；还定量提取了~12 keV反常相位变化。

Conclusion: 该方法在Au L3边（~12 keV）成功实现高分辨化学成像，证明X射线谱学叠层成像适用于更广泛功能材料的高能段高分辨率化学成像。

Abstract: Understanding the functionalities of heterogeneous catalysts requires high-resolution nanoscale chemical imaging tools. X-ray spectro-ptychography offers this capability by enabling X-ray absorption near edge structure (XANES) based amplitude and phase contrasts imaging with high spatial resolution. However, such demonstrations at energies above 10 keV, such as the Au L-edges, remain limited due to weak X-ray absorption cross-sections and correspondingly, low signal levels. Here, multimodal spectro-microscopy has been performed on Au microcrystallites that host both conventional face-centered cubic (fcc) and unconventional non-cubic lattices, particularly important for catalysis. Amplitude and phase contrasts across the $\mathrm{L}_3$ edge are resolved on thin Au crystallites (150 nm to 1.71 $μ\mathrm{m}$) with 45 nm spatial resolution. The resulting XANES spectra exhibit excellent agreement with standard transmission and fluorescence contrasts, while providing complementary chemical insights with improved spatial resolution. Quantitative retrieval of anomalous phase variations ~12 keV further demonstrates the suitability of this method for high-resolution chemical imaging across a wide range of functional materials.

</details>


### [114] [Electric Edelstein Effect in Two Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17535)
*Xiaoran Lv,Tianqi Zhang,Chaoxi Cui,Yaling Li,Yi Shen,Weikang Wu,Ziming Zhu,Yugui Yao,Zhi-Ming Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出二维系统中的电场Edelstein效应（EEE），由面外与面内交叉电场产生自旋极化，具有栅压可调性；对称性分析表明其在多种磁性与非磁性体系普遍存在，并通过第一性原理在MnSe2与SnSe中验证了效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性Edelstein效应（EE）产生的自旋极化由材料自身固定，且在某些对称性（如中心对称）体系中是被禁止的；需要一种能突破对称性限制、并实现栅压可调自旋极化的新机制，以提升电学操控自旋的自由度。

Method: 提出电场Edelstein效应：利用面外栅场与面内电场的交叉作用产生自旋极化；对所有磁层点群进行对称性分析以判定EEE的允许条件；建立基于能带几何量的微观理论；并采用第一性原理计算预测了中心对称铁磁体MnSe2和非磁体SnSe中的效应。

Result: 对称性分析显示EEE在铁磁、反铁磁、交错磁等多种磁性和非磁体系中普遍允许；EEE既能在线性EE被对称性禁止的中心对称材料（如MnSe2）中产生自旋极化，又能在EE已存在的材料（如SnSe）中增加一个与常规自旋极化非共线的、栅压可调的新分量，使自旋极化的调控维度由一维提升至二维；效应在小能隙附近因能带几何量而显著增强。

Conclusion: EEE是一种由对称性引导、栅压可调的纯电学自旋操控途径，为二维量子材料中的多态自旋电子学开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We propose the electric Edelstein effect (EEE) in two-dimensional (2D) systems, where crossed out-of-plane and in-plane electric fields generate a spin polarization. The gate field converts the Edelstein response from a material-fixed spin polarization into a gate-tunable one. A symmetry analysis over all magnetic layer point groups shows that the EEE is allowed in a wide range of magnetic and nonmagnetic systems, including ferromagnets, antiferromagnets, and altermagnets. The EEE plays two distinct roles: it generates spin polarization where the linear Edelstein effect (EE) is symmetry-forbidden, as in centrosymmetric materials; and it adds a new gate-tunable component, generally non-collinear with the conventional one, where the EE is already present. The two fields then act as independent knobs and raise the control dimension of the spin polarization from one to two. Our microscopic theory traces the EEE to band-geometric quantities, which are strongly enhanced near small band gaps. First-principles calculations predict pronounced effects in the centrosymmetric ferromagnet MnSe2, where the linear EE is symmetry-forbidden. In the nonmagnet SnSe where the EE and EEE coexist, the two knobs cover an extended region of the spin sphere. Our findings establish the EEE as a symmetry-guided, gate-tunable route to electrical spin manipulation and open avenues for multistate spintronics in 2D quantum materials.

</details>


### [115] [A grand potential for molten-salt electrochemical phase diagrams from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17611)
*Lorenzo Bastonero,Lucio Colombi Ciacchi,Nicola Marzari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于第一性原理的框架，用于构建多组分体系的电化学相图，以加速熔盐电解中电极、电解质及操作条件的设计，并在硅-熔融CaCl₂和铝电解筛选中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清洁能源转型引发对金属的庞大需求，而金属提取是碳排放的主要工业来源之一。熔盐电解可实现铝、铁、钛及关键金属的无碳生产，但腐蚀性工况和复杂多组分原料使实验开发成本高、难度大，亟需理论工具加速设计。

Method: 作者提出基于巨势（grand potential）的第一性原理框架，用于大规模构建多组分体系电化学相图；自由能数据由经过微调的基础机器学习原子间势（foundation machine-learned interatomic potential）基于第一性原理计算得到。该框架首先在两种广度下重构硅在熔融CaCl₂中的相图，并对十种碱金属/碱土金属卤化物电解质进行铝电解筛选。

Result: 对硅在熔融CaCl₂体系，该框架在有限结构集上复现了实验相图，且扩展到更大晶体结构集后预测出更多相域。对铝电解的十种卤化物电解质筛选表明，三个热力学判据一致识别出NaF，其中冰晶石（cryolite）成为稳定的含铝相，重现了Hall和Héroult于1886年凭经验确立的化学过程。

Conclusion: 该框架为低碳金属生产中兼容电极、电解质和操作条件的选择提供了第一性原理依据，有望加速复杂来源原料的无碳金属提取。

Abstract: The clean-energy transition is driving unprecedented demand for metals, whose extraction is among the largest industrial sources of carbon emissions. Molten-salt electrolysis offers a path toward carbon-free production of aluminum, iron, titanium, and critical metals. However, corrosive operating conditions and complex, multicomponent feedstocks make experimental development costly and challenging. Here, we introduce a first-principles framework for constructing electrochemical phase diagrams of multicomponent systems to accelerate electrode and electrolyte design and guide the selection of compatible materials and operating conditions for electrochemical cells. We derive a grand potential that generates these diagrams at scale, and supply free energies using first-principles data obtained through a fine-tuned foundation machine-learned interatomic potential. For silicon in molten CaCl$_2$, the construction not only recovers the experimental diagram when using the first-principles framework on a limited set of structures, but also predicts further domains when extended to a larger set of crystal structures. Finally, we demonstrate the predictive power of the approach by screening ten alkali and alkaline-earth halide electrolytes for aluminum electrowinning. Three thermodynamic criteria identify NaF, where cryolite emerges as the stable aluminum-bearing phase, recovering the chemistry that Hall and Héroult established empirically in 1886. The framework provides a first-principles basis for selecting compatible electrodes, electrolytes, and operating conditions toward low-carbon metal production from complex source materials.

</details>


### [116] [A Parameter-Free Reference Hamiltonian for Tight-Binding Vanadium Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17687)
*Jun-qiang Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文构建了一种无参数可调、可迁移的 VO₂ 紧束缚模型，通过修正二阶密度泛函紧束缚中两个互相抵消的近似，使参考能和电荷响应均与 PBE/PAW 参考高度一致，并指出 Magnéli 相中的对称性破缺需由电子-晶格耦合或显式自旋/关联项驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准二阶密度泛函紧束缚（DFTB）对钒氧化物尚无法实现可迁移描述。作者发现其两个近似——中性双中心参考（遗漏电子伏级环境项）与 Mulliken 电荷模型（低估 V 3d 壳层的自洽势位移）——虽各偏离数电子伏，却因相互抵消掩盖了问题。因此需要建立一个无需可调参数、且能量与电荷响应均精确的紧束缚模型。

Method: 构建一个紧束缚模型，其参考哈密顿量由叠加的中性原子、完整 PBE 交换关联势和冻芯正交性导出，不含任何可调参数；轨道分辨电荷响应采用由 PBE 线性响应得到的三个屏蔽因子与一个非屏蔽库仑范围。以 PAW 为基准，验证固定几何下的参考能量及 VO₂ 的平面波 Harris-Foulkes 能量差，并测试 ±3% 体积压缩/膨胀下的表现。

Result: 参考能量在固定几何与所述数值设置下与 PAW 匹配：M1 相对金红石的符号化偏差为 +2.6 meV/原子（能量差本身为 92 meV/原子）；3% 体积压缩与膨胀下偏差分别为 +6.2 和 -5.2 meV/原子。电荷响应在容差内捕获 22 个拟合响应中的 21 个及全部 8 个留出测试响应，使 V 3d 壳层获得与 PBE 几乎相同的自洽位移（金红石中 -3.89 对 -3.92 eV）。

Conclusion: 该无自旋紧束缚模型在固定几何下具有凸性，在其实施算符与采样所遵从的对称性下无法自发区分对称等价位点，因此 Magnéli 相中的此类位点分化必须源于电子-晶格耦合或显式自旋/关联项。

Abstract: Density-functional tight binding has yet to yield a transferable description of vanadium oxides; for the standard second-order construction and the tested cells, we demonstrate that two of its approximations deviate by electron volts and offset each other in the band-index centroid separation between the V 3d and O 2p bands: the neutral two-centre reference omits electron-volt environmental terms, whereas the Mulliken charge model assigns the compact V 3d shell less than half of the self-consistent potential shift observed in PBE. Instead, we build a tight-binding model where the reference Hamiltonian derives from superposed neutral atoms, the full PBE exchange-correlation potential and frozen-core orthogonality, without adjustable terms, and where the orbital-resolved charge response employs three screening factors obtained from PBE linear response and an unscreened Coulomb range. Against the primary PAW reference, the reference energy at fixed geometry and the stated numerical settings matches the plane-wave Harris-Foulkes energy differences of VO2, exhibiting signed deviations of +2.6 meV/atom for M1 relative to rutile (an energy difference of 92 meV/atom) and +6.2 and -5.2 meV/atom under 3% volume compression and expansion; meanwhile, the charge response captures 21 of 22 fitted and all 8 held-out matched PBE responses within tolerance, providing the V 3d shell with nearly the same self-consistent shift as PBE (-3.89 versus -3.92 eV in rutile). The convexity of the spinless model at fixed geometry prevents it from spontaneously differentiating symmetry-equivalent sites under the symmetries respected by its implemented operators and sampling, so within this model such differentiation in the Magnéli phases must arise from electron-lattice coupling or an explicit spin or correlation term.

</details>


### [117] [Hydrogen-Bond Density Drives the Order-Disorder Phase Transition from Orientational to Vibrational in Polyalcohol Plastic Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17774)
*Ares Sanuy,Carlos Escorihuela-Sayalero,Pol Lloveras,María del Barrio,Hassan Allouchi,Josep-Lluís Tamarit,Luis Carlos Pardo,Claudio Cazorla*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过经典分子动力学模拟三种新戊烷衍生塑晶，发现相变熵的微观来源随氢键连接度增加发生完全反转：NPG 以取向无序为主，PE 以晶格振动为主且对压力稳健，PG 处于中间平衡态；氢键密度可同时调控卡路里效应的幅度与压力稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新戊烷衍生塑晶的有序-无序相变伴随巨大熵变，是储热和压卡致冷的理想固态材料，但熵变的微观本质究竟是化合物的固有属性，还是可被普遍理解并通过分子设计系统调控，目前尚不清楚。

Method: 对 neopentyl glycol（NPG）、pentaglycerine（PG）和 pentaerythritol（PE）进行经典分子动力学模拟，并以实验的转变温度、焓变和相图进行验证；通过分解相变熵在取向、构象和振动通道上的贡献，并分析有序/无序相声子态密度的低频带隙及分子对称性，揭示其微观机制。

Result: 相变熵特征随氢键连接度增大发生完全反转：NPG 由取向无序主导；PE 由晶格振动主导且对压力异常稳健；PG 处于中间态，取向、构象、振动三通道贡献相当。该反转源于两个耦合机制：氢键网络致密化使有序相与无序相声子态密度间的低频带隙增大，以及分子对称性提高逐步抑制取向无序。

Conclusion: 振动通道对压力的敏感度远低于取向通道，因此氢键密度是一个实际且化学上可及的调控手柄，能同时调节氢键塑晶卡路里响应的幅度与压力稳健性，对寻找新型压卡材料和储热材料具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Neopentane-derived plastic crystals undergo order-disorder phase transitions accompanied by exceptionally large entropy changes, making them attractive solid-state materials for thermal energy storage and barocaloric cooling. Yet it remains unclear whether the microscopic nature of this entropy change is an intrinsic property of each compound or can be understood generally and tuned systematically through molecular design. Here, we use classical molecular dynamics simulations of neopentyl glycol (NPG), pentaglycerine (PG), and pentaerythritol (PE), validated against experimental transition temperatures, enthalpy changes, and phase diagrams, to show that the character of the phase-transition entropy undergoes a complete inversion as the hydrogen-bond connectivity of the molecule increases: orientational disordering dominates in NPG, whereas lattice vibrations dominate in PE and become remarkably robust against pressure. PG occupies a balanced intermediate regime in which the orientational, conformational, and vibrational channels contribute comparably. This inversion reflects two coupled microscopic mechanisms: a growing low-frequency gap between the phonon densities of states of the ordered and disordered phases as the hydrogen-bond network densifies, and an increase in molecular symmetry that progressively suppresses orientational disordering. Because the vibrational channel is substantially less sensitive to pressure than the orientational one, hydrogen-bond density emerges as a practical, chemically accessible design handle for tailoring not only the magnitude but also the pressure robustness of the caloric response in hydrogen-bonded plastic crystals, with direct implications for the search for new barocaloric and thermal-energy-storage materials.

</details>


### [118] [Learning from fracture: Teaching language models to design through physical experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17804)
*Bo Ni*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 FracLearn——一个"经验-抽象-泛化"框架，通过一个刻意陌生化的类俄罗斯方块断裂游戏来验证语言模型能否从经验中学习优化物理设计，最终在无权重更新的情况下取得比经验阶段记录高 11.0% 的复合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型能否通过自身经验（而非权重更新）学会优化物理设计，从而为材料发现和工程设计提供可复用的物理知识，并迈向能够将计算实验转化为知识的科学智能体。

Method: 提出 FracLearn 框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 经验(Experience)——模型提出架构并观察模拟的断裂响应以积累经验；(2) 抽象(Abstraction)——将观察结果组织为条件化原理，把强化体放置、裂纹演化与载荷传递联系起来，并附支持证据与失效条件；(3) 泛化(Generalization)——要求模型在新架构中组合这些原理以超越此前的设计记录。学习完全通过外部知识与决策实现，全程不更新模型权重。

Result: 多轮campaign展现出通过假设修正实现的渐进式优化与纠错恢复，最佳复合得分比经验阶段记录高出11.0%。断裂分析表明性能提升源于裂纹偏转与暂时捕获等增韧机制，以及更高的残余承载能力。

Conclusion: FracLearn 表明语言模型可以在不更新权重的情况下，通过经验积累、抽象归纳和泛化组合来优化物理设计，为构建将计算实验转化为可复用物理知识的科学智能体提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: How can language models learn to optimize physical designs through experience? We introduce FracLearn, an experience-abstraction-generalization framework evaluated through a deliberately unfamiliar, Tetris-inspired fracture game. The model first proposes architectures and examines simulated fracture responses to accumulate experience. Abstraction organizes these observations into conditional principles linking reinforcement placement, crack evolution and load transfer, with supporting evidence and failure conditions. Generalization challenges the model to combine these principles in new architectures and surpass the earlier design record. Campaigns demonstrate progressive refinement and recovery through hypothesis revision, yielding a best composite score 11.0% above the experience-stage record. Fracture analyses connect improved performance to crack deflection and temporary trapping as toughening mechanisms, alongside greater residual load capacity. Learning occurs through external knowledge and decisions, without model-weight updates. This approach offers a route to scientific agents that turn computational experiments into reusable physical knowledge for materials discovery and engineering design.

</details>


### [119] [Polarons in Lead-Halide Perovskites: What the Structural Evidence Does and Does Not Establish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17809)
*Inhee Maeng,Young Mi Lee,Jinwoo Park,Seung Jae Oh,Min-Cherl Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文质疑了铅卤钙钛矿中光生载流子以"大极化子"运动并屏蔽缺陷的传统观点，指出近期三项发现对这一图景构成挑战，并提出通过测量晶格畸变随激发密度的变化来区分极化子形成与缺陷容忍的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，学界普遍认为铅卤钙钛矿中的光生载流子以伴随晶格畸变的大极化子形式运动，且这种畸变被认为可屏蔽带电缺陷、解释材料的缺陷容忍性。但近期实验与计算对这一图景提出质疑：热晶格运动本身即可导致载流子有效质量增大（此前被当作极化子存在的证据），且首次缺陷容忍的直接测试并未指向极化子屏蔽。因此需要厘清极化子形成与缺陷容忍之间的因果关系。

Method: 作者梳理并批判性分析了三类最新证据：（1）比较时间分辨结构探针测得的晶格畸变与纯热晶格运动引起的有效质量变化，判断畸变是否真正构成极化子；（2）对比3D到2D钙钛矿（Ruddlesden-Popper与Dion-Jacobson两大家族）中载流子-晶格耦合的不同通道及其主导机制；（3）重新审视缺陷容忍的直接测试与新输运数据，区分极化子屏蔽对"载流子间散射"与"载流子-缺陷散射"的不同作用。在此基础上，作者提出测量晶格畸变随激发密度变化（覆盖太阳光下的低密度到超快实验中的高密度）的实验方案来分离两种效应。

Result: 分析表明：（1）仅凭载流子有效质量增大无法确认极化子，必须区分热晶格运动与真实极化子畸变；（2）2D钙钛矿中载流子-晶格耦合的主导通道（而非单纯晶格刚度）决定了两大家族的相反行为；（3）极化子屏蔽确能减少载流子间散射，但并未证据表明它能保护载流子免受缺陷影响，因此即使极化子形成成立，也不能将其确立为缺陷容忍的原因。

Conclusion: 极化子的形成（即便得到证实）并不等于极化子就是缺陷容忍的成因。要分离二者，必须测量晶格畸变随激发密度的变化，且密度范围需跨越太阳能电池日光照射的低密度与多数超快实验所用的高密度。据此作者提出了可澄清铅卤钙钛矿中光生载流子行为的实验方案。

Abstract: Photogenerated carriers in lead-halide perovskites are widely believed to move as large polarons, whose accompanying lattice distortion is thought to screen the carrier from charged defects. Three recent findings call this picture into question. First, time-resolved structural probes now measure the photoinduced lattice distortion directly; yet calculations show that thermal motion of the lattice alone can raise the carrier effective mass-an increase previously taken as evidence for polarons-so that it remains unclear whether the lattice distortion around a carrier is really a polaron. Second, the carrier-lattice coupling strengthens as the material is thinned from 3D to 2D. In the two main families of 2D perovskites, Ruddlesden-Popper and Dion-Jacobson, the carrier couples to the lattice through different channels, and their contrasting behavior is set by which coupling channel dominates rather than by lattice stiffness alone. Third, the first direct test of defect tolerance does not point to polaron screening; its authors attribute defect tolerance to the soft, disordered lattice. New transport data show that polaron screening reduces scattering between carriers, but not that it protects carriers from defects. Polaron formation, even if established, therefore does not establish polarons as the cause of defect tolerance. Separating the two requires measuring the lattice distortion as a function of excitation density, over a range spanning the low densities of a solar cell under sunlight and the much higher densities used in most ultrafast experiments. On this basis, we propose experiments that can clarify how photogenerated carriers behave in lead-halide perovskites.

</details>


### [120] [Orthoexciton-to-paraexciton conversion rate in Cu$_2$O at sub-Kelvin temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17812)
*Yusuke Morita,Yukihiro Akiyama,Yuki Haita,Yuga Hirota,Kosuke Yoshioka*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文测量了Cu2O中1s正激子(orthoexciton)向1s仲激子(paraexciton)的转化率，发现在64 mK时转化率为5.9×10^7 s^-1，且随温度(64–800 mK)变化微弱，仅为2 K下报告值的一半，表明转化率在2 K以下继续下降，与先前预期不符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前认为Cu2O中正激子–仲激子转化率在约1.7 K以下几乎恒定，但低温区(<2 K)的实验数据匮乏。作者希望精确测定极低温下的转化率，以检验并修正现有理论预期。

Method: 使用稀释制冷机在1 K以下测量，利用应变诱导的陷阱势将激子空间局域，通过时间分辨发光光谱测量1s正激子和1s仲激子的时间演化。具体从正激子的Γ3⁻-LO声子辅助发光的强度衰减中提取转化率，测量在2.2 kbar外加应力、混合室温度64–800 mK下进行。

Result: 在64 mK、2.2 kbar下得到转化率5.9×10^7 s^-1；在64–800 mK之间转化率仅微弱依赖温度，且不足同一应力下2 K时报告值1.6×10^8 s^-1的一半，说明转化率在2 K以下继续下降。

Conclusion: 正激子–仲激子转化率在2 K以下并未趋于恒定而是继续下降，作者将其归因于低温下正激子动量分布未与晶格达到热平衡。现有理论无法同时解释转化率的较小数值及其1 K以下的弱温度依赖，亟需新的理论描述。

Abstract: We measured the conversion rate from 1s orthoexcitons to 1s paraexcitons in Cu$_2$O below 1 K using a dilution refrigerator. To determine the conversion rate, we performed time-resolved luminescence spectroscopy of 1s orthoexcitons and 1s paraexcitons confined in a strain-induced trap potential. From the time evolution of the $Γ$$_3$$^{-}$-LO-phonon-assisted luminescence of 1s orthoexcitons, we obtained a conversion rate of $5.9 \times 10^7$ s${^{-1}}$ at a mixing-chamber temperature of 64 mK under an applied stress of 2.2 kbar. Between 64 and 800 mK, the conversion rate depends only weakly on temperature, and it is less than half of the value of $1.6 \times 10^8$ s${^{-1}}$ reported at 2 K under the same applied stress. The conversion rate therefore continues to decrease below 2 K, contrary to the previous expectation that it becomes nearly constant below about 1.7 K. We discuss these results in terms of the momentum distribution of orthoexcitons, which does not reach equilibrium with the lattice during the conversion at these low temperatures. These findings call for a theory that accounts for both the small magnitude of the conversion rate and its weak temperature dependence below 1 K.

</details>


### [121] [Colossal Spin--Lattice--Charge Coupling in Noncollinear Ferrimagnetic NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17849)
*Peng Wang,Nanshu Liu,Hongkuan Yuan,Xue Jiang,Jijun Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算、对称性分析、原子自旋模拟和输运建模，发现NbMnX₂（X=S、Se、Te）单层为非共线亚铁磁体，居里温度最高达313 K，兼具机械、热学和电学多功能响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线亚铁磁体兼具净磁矩和内部自旋自由度，有望在同一材料中耦合磁、力、热、电响应；但缺少稳定且居里温度接近室温的二维材料平台来充分挖掘这一潜力。

Method: 作者采用第一性原理计算结合对称性分析确定基态与稳定性，用原子自旋模拟研究竞争性的海森堡交换、Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用和磁各向异性导致的倾斜磁态，并通过输运建模分析磁化旋转对能带和电导的影响。

Result: 研究发现NbMnX₂（X=S、Se、Te）单层具有稳定非共线亚铁磁基态，居里温度最高313 K。磁化旋转使NbMnTe₂弹性模量改变达36%；晶格熵显著增强NbMnS₂和NbMnSe₂在近室温的磁热响应。在NbMnSe₂中，旋转恢复镜面保护的能带交叉，连续闭合约0.13 eV带隙，并在100 K下产生高达10,000%的基于电导的各向异性磁阻。

Conclusion: 非共线NbMnX₂单层成为二维自旋电子学、纳米力学和热控制的多功能平台，为磁-力-热-电多场耦合器件提供了新的材料基础。

Abstract: Noncollinear ferrimagnets combine a net magnetic moment with internal spin degrees of freedom, creating opportunities to couple magnetic, mechanical, thermal, and electronic responses. Using first-principles calculations, symmetry analysis, atomistic spin simulations, and transport modeling, we identify stable NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) monolayers with noncollinear ferrimagnetic ground states and Curie temperatures up to 313 K. Competing exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions, and magnetic anisotropy produce field-responsive canted states that couple strongly to the lattice and charge carriers. Magnetization rotation changes the elastic modulus of NbMnTe$_2$ by up to 36\%, while lattice entropy substantially enhances the near-room-temperature magnetocaloric response of NbMnS$_2$ and NbMnSe$_2$. In NbMnSe$_2$, rotation restores a mirror-protected crossing, continuously closes an approximately 0.13 eV gap, and produces a conductivity-based anisotropic magnetoresistance ratio up to 10,000\% at 100 K. The combined results establish noncollinear NbMnX$_2$ monolayers as a multifunctional platform for two-dimensional spintronics, nanomechanics, and thermal control.

</details>


### [122] [ARES: a plane-wave density-functional-theory code in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17854)
*Zehan Liu,Qian Wang,Xiangyu Zheng,Jiaxiang Li,Chuntian Wu,Yongshuo Chen,Xuecheng Shao,Qiang Xu,Yu Xie,Yanming Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ARES 是吉林大学开发的完全用 Rust 编写的平面波密度泛函理论（DFT）代码，采用 22 个 crate 的分层工作区结构。其核心特色是独特的验证体系：所有数值组件都与 Quantum ESPRESSO 逐项比对，并以编号夹具（fixtures）形式保存在仓库中，参考数据由脚本可再生地重新生成。在六种材料上，费米能级附近 1 eV 范围内的能带能量与参考值的中位偏差低于 0.4 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机是构建一个新的从头算原子材料模拟软件，特别是强调计算结果的可靠性验证。区别于多数 DFT 代码，ARES 将验证作为一等工程选择：每个数值组件都与成熟软件 Quantum ESPRESSO 在相同物理和数值输入下对照，并把比较结果组织为可再生、版本化的测试夹具，从而保证代码重构和演进过程中数值正确性持续得到检验。

Method: ARES 采用平面波基组结合模守恒赝势和投影缀加波（PAW）方法，支持 LDA 和 PBE 两种泛函；提供 Γ-only 与显式 k 点采样、展宽与电荷混合、受力、应力、弛豫、能带结构、态密度（DOS）以及色散校正；执行方式为串行或 MPI 分布式（按 k 点池、能带组和平面波行划分）。软件完全以 Rust 编写，组织为分层的 22-crate 工作区。验证方面，其所有数值组件均与 Quantum ESPRESSO 在相同物理与数值输入下比对，比对结果用带编号的夹具存储于仓库中，参考数据由脚本而非一次性抓取来再生。赝势也对照全电子参考数据验证。

Result: 在横跨一种半导体、三种金属和两种二维体系的六种材料上，费米能级附近 1 eV 范围内的能带能量与 Quantum ESPRESSO 参考值的中位偏差低于 0.4 meV。赝势验证中，全部 65 个配对元素的中位 delta 因子为 0.696 meV/atom。

Conclusion: ARES 证明可以用现代系统语言 Rust 从零实现功能完整的平面波 DFT 代码，并通过对 Quantum ESPRESSO 的逐项自动化验证达到可靠的数值精度。作者同时如实列出限制：仅支持两种泛函、无自旋极化或自旋轨道耦合路径、应力实现有明确适用范围、且不支持加速器执行。

Abstract: ARES (Ab initio atomic mateRial modEling Software at JLU) is a plane-wave density-functional-theory (DFT) code written entirely in Rust as a layered 22-crate workspace. It supports norm-conserving pseudopotentials and the projector augmented-wave (PAW) method with the local-density approximation (LDA) and Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) functionals, Γ-only and explicit k-point sampling, smearing and mixing, forces, stress, relaxation, band structures, densities of states (DOS) and dispersion corrections; execution is serial or MPI-distributed over k-point pools, band groups and plane-wave rows. The code's distinguishing engineering choice is its validation apparatus. Every numerical component is checked against Quantum ESPRESSO driven from the same physical and numerical inputs, and the comparison is organized as numbered fixtures kept in the repository whose reference data is regenerated by a script instead of being captured once. On six materials spanning a semiconductor, three metals and two two-dimensional systems, band energies within one electronvolt of the Fermi level agree with the reference to a median below 0.4 meV. The pseudopotentials the code reads are validated against all-electron reference data as well: the median delta factor is 0.696 meV/atom over all 65 paired elements. Restrictions are stated with the capabilities: two functionals, no spin-polarized or spin-orbit path, a stress implementation with an explicit scope, and no accelerator execution.

</details>


### [123] [Thermal stability and structural ordering of thick Co$_2$MnAl films grown on MgO and Si substrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17955)
*David Robles-Cuenca,Juan Antonio González,Juan Pedro Andrés,Miguel Ángel Arranz,Mohamed Salaheldeen,Ricardo López Antón*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了750 nm厚Co2MnAl(CMA)薄膜在MgO(001)与Si衬底上的结构与热稳定性，确定了其在多功能CMOS兼容平台中的工艺集成窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMA研究多集中于用于自旋电子结的超薄体系，而反常能斯特效应(ANE)能量收集器、集成功率电感、磁性MEMS等应用随磁性体积扩展，需要显著更厚的薄膜，因此需要建立厚CMA薄膜的热集成路线图。

Method: 在MgO(001)和Si衬底上生长750 nm厚的CMA薄膜，通过结构分析考察B2有序度随退火温度的变化，并研究Si衬底上界面扩散、硅化物与Kirkendall空洞形成及电阻突变行为。

Result: MgO衬底在600 °C时B2有序度最优；Si衬底样品在500 °C以上发生Si向Heusler层互扩散，触发界面硅化物与Kirkendall空洞，导致微裂纹、电阻率突跳，最终转变为完全无序的A2相。尽管薄膜为贫Mn化学计量(Co2.25Mn0.7Al1.05)，仍保持约600 emu/cm3的稳健饱和磁化强度，与近期非化学计量Heusler合金理论模型一致。

Conclusion: 研究明确了厚CMA层在多功能CMOS兼容平台中集成的加工窗口，并为非化学计量Heusler合金的磁学行为提供了实验依据。

Abstract: While most studies on Co$2$MnAl (CMA) focus on the ultrathin regime for spintronic junctions, applications scaling with magnetic volume--such as Anomalous Nernst Effect (ANE) energy harvesters, integrated power inductors, and magnetic MEMS--require significantly thicker films. In this work, 750 nm thick CMA films were grown on MgO (001) and Si substrates to establish a roadmap for their thermal integration. Structural analysis reveals that B2 ordering is optimized on MgO at 600 $^\circ$C, which is important for functional devices. In contrast, samples on Si substrates exhibit a disorder transition toward the A2 phase, closely linked to diffusion-driven degradation processes. Above 500 $^\circ$C, Si interdiffusion into the Heusler layer triggers the formation of interfacial silicides and Kirkendall voids, leading to microcracking, electrical resistivity jumps, and a final fully disordered A2 phase. Despite the Mn-poor stoichiometry (Co$2.25$Mn$0.7$Al$1.05$), the films maintain a robust saturation magnetization of ~600 emu/cm$^3$, consistent with recent theoretical models for off-stoichiometric Heusler alloys. These results define the processing windows for integrating thick CMA layers into multifunctional CMOS-compatible platforms.

</details>


### [124] [Field-free Magnetoacoustic Nonreciprocity via Exchange-bias Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17979)
*Weiqi Du,Mingyuan Ma,Zheng Sun,Chong Chen,Shixuan Liang,Deqi Tao,Shihai An,Yifan Fu,Sulei Fu,Feng Pan,Cheng Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用交换偏置工程在零外加磁场下实现磁声非互易传播，通过在IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB多层膜中稳定一个同时破缺时间反演与空间反演对称性的剩磁态，实现谐振磁声耦合，归一化隔离度达21 dB/mm，并可通过磁场扫描历史编程其符号；数值优化预测零场隔离度可提升至91 dB/mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易波传播对定向信号处理与隔离至关重要，磁声系统因体积小、无源工作而具吸引力，但显著非互易性通常需要外加偏置磁场，限制了器件小型化与集成。因此需要一种无需外磁场的磁声非互易方案。

Method: 将IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB多层膜与水平剪切波延迟线集成，利用交换偏置工程稳定一个同时破缺时间反演和空间反演对称性的剩磁态，并调控自旋波色散以实现零场下的谐振磁声耦合，从而获得场自由的磁声非互易性；进一步结合数值优化（增大动力学磁性不对称度）预测性能上限。

Result: 实验实现了归一化隔离度21 dB/mm，且隔离符号可由磁场扫描历史编程；数值优化预测在增大动力学磁不对称度后，零场归一化隔离度可达91 dB/mm。

Conclusion: 交换偏置工程为构建紧凑型射频隔离器和环行器提供了一条有前景的途径，并可作为无需外磁场实现磁声器件的通用策略。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal wave propagation is essential for directional signal processing and isolation. Magnetoacoustic systems are attractive platforms for nonreciprocal devices because of their small footprint and passive operation. However, pronounced nonreciprocity typically requires an external bias field,limiting device miniaturization and integration. Here, we demonstrate field-free magnetoacoustic nonreciprocity in an IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB multilayer integrated with a shear-horizontal-wave delay line. Exchange-bias engineering stabilizes a remanent magnetic state with broken time-reversal and spatial-inversion symmetries while tailoring the spin-wave dispersion to achieve resonant magnetoacoustic coupling at zero field. The normalized isolation reaches 21 dB/mm, with its sign programmable by the field-sweep history. Further numerical optimization predicts a zero-field normalized isolation of 91 dB/mm by increasing the dynamical magnetic asymmetry. Our work establishes exchange-bias engineering as a promising route toward compact RF isolators and circulators and as a general strategy for realizing magnetoacoustic devices without external magnetic fields.

</details>


### [125] [Knowledge-Guided Construction of Machine Learning Potentials for Reactive Behavior in Energetic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17987)
*Guanchen Dong,Huanrong Liu,Lin Wang,Yuxuan Chen,Zhengyu Tu,Qing-an Li,Bin Xu,Jian Li,Shan Zhang,Xiaotong Li,Linghua Tan,Guangcheng Yang,Jun Kang,Rui Su,Pengfei Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种增强型神经网络原子间势（NNAP），通过改进的球谐基组sphmulti与主动学习采样策略，仅用13680个构型即可准确描述含能材料的异构化、热解和冲击Hugoniot行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含能材料涉及复杂的多体相互作用，传统上构建可靠的机器学习势（MLP）需要海量数据集，成本高昂且难以覆盖关键反应事件。作者旨在开发一种能在有限数据下高效、准确描述含能材料体系的MLP方法。

Method: 作者提出多方面的改进：1) 设计sphmulti精化球谐基组，在每个径向通道中引入额外径向基函数和元素特定的化学权重，增强NNAP对分子体系的描述能力；2) 集成超活跃学习（hyperactive learning）、最远点采样（farthest-point sampling）与迭代充分性判据，并结合知识引导的关键事件，使NNAP保留描述材料体系所必需的信息。在3,3'-二氨基-4,4'-偶氮氧化呋咱上训练得到的NNAP仅需13680个构型。

Result: 所得NNAP在仅13680个训练构型下准确复现了关键性质，包括异构化能垒、热解活化能和冲击Hugoniot行为。NNAP驱动的分子动力学模拟进一步揭示：热解过程经由呋咱环开环后发生链式反应；冲击加载则引发由层间结构形变驱动的各向异性氢转移。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了面向含能材料的高效、可靠机器学习势，为在有限数据条件下构建精确描述复杂含能体系的MLP提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Energetic materials involve multiple interactions, making reliable machine-learning potentials (MLPs) difficult to construct without extensive datasets. Here, we enhance the ability of the neural network interatomic potential (NNAP) to describe molecular systems using sphmulti, a refined spherical-harmonic basis set that combines extra radial basis with element-specific chemical weighting in each radial channel. We further integrate hyperactive learning, farthest-point sampling, and iterative sufficiency criteria with knowledge-guided key events, enabling the NNAP to retain essential information for describing the material system. For 3,3'-diamino-4,4'-azoxyfurazan, the resulting NNAP is trained on only 13,680 configurations yet captures key properties, including the isomerization barrier, pyrolysis activation energy, and shock Hugoniot behavior. NNAP-driven molecular dynamics simulations further reveal that pyrolysis proceeds through furazan-ring opening followed by chain reactions, whereas shock loading induces anisotropic hydrogen transfer driven by interlayer structural deformations. This work enables efficient and reliable MLPs for energetic materials.

</details>


### [126] [Hydrogen partial pressure as a key parameter in the reactive sputtering of photochromic YHO thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18068)
*E. Strods,M. Zubkins,E. Letko,V. Vibornijs,A. Sarakovskis,J. Purans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究了反应磁控溅射中氢氩气流量比（H2/Ar）对含氧氢化钇（YHO）薄膜结构、光学及光致变色性能的影响，发现低氢分压有利于获得更高的光学透过率、更强的光致变色对比度和更快的漂白动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YHO薄膜的光致变色性能通常被报道与氧含量相关，但氢沉积条件对其微观结构、光学和光致变色行为的综合影响尚不清晰。作者旨在系统探究不同H2/Ar流量比下生长的YHO薄膜，厘清制备条件与性能之间的关联，以优化光致变色材料。

Method: 采用反应磁控溅射在宽范围H2/Ar流量比下制备YHO薄膜，并在空气中后氧化；利用等离子体发射光谱原位监测沉积过程，用X射线衍射分析织构与晶粒尺寸，用光学透射谱测透过率，用太阳模拟器照射10分钟测光致变色对比度，用双指数模型分析漂白动力学，并用X射线光电子能谱测定氧含量。

Result: XRD显示织构强烈依赖于H2/Ar比：低H2/Ar比时形成高度<100>择优取向薄膜，随氢含量增加逐渐演变为更接近多晶的微观结构且晶粒尺寸略减小。透过率随H2/Ar比升至0.6时由约85%逐渐降至70%，更高氢含量时急剧下降。690-820 nm厚薄膜的光致变色对比度随H2/Ar从0.08增至0.60由14%降至7%，较薄薄膜（360-490 nm）趋势类似但更弱。双指数模型显示H2/Ar<0.25时漂白更快（有效时间常数约1-15 min）。XPS表明低H2/Ar薄膜氧含量更高，这与通常报道的氧含量与光致变色响应的相关性不一致。

Conclusion: 反应溅射中低氢分压有利于YHO薄膜获得更高的光学透过率、更强的光致变色对比度和更快的漂白动力学；并且氧含量并非唯一主导光致变色响应的因素，沉积条件引起的织构与微观结构同样起重要作用。

Abstract: Oxygen-containing yttrium hydride (YHO) thin films were synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering over a wide range of hydrogen-to-argon gas flow ratios (H2/Ar) during yttrium hydride deposition, followed by post-oxidation in air. The deposition process was monitored in situ by plasma optical emission spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals a strong dependence of crystallographic texture on the H2/Ar ratio: highly <100>-textured films formed at low H2/Ar ratios progressively evolve into more polycrystalline microstructures with slightly reduced crystallite size as the hydrogen content increases. The optical transmittance gradually decreases from approximately 85 % to 70 % as the H2/Ar ratio is raised to 0.6, followed by a sharp drop at higher hydrogen contents. The photochromic contrast of 690-820 nm thick YHO films, measured after 10 min of illumination with a solar simulator, decreases from 14 % to 7 % as the H2/Ar ratio increases from 0.08 to 0.60. Thinner films (360-490 nm) exhibit a similar but less pronounced trend. Bleaching kinetics, analysed using a double-exponential model, indicate faster recovery, with effective bleaching time constants of approximately 1-15 min for films deposited at H2/Ar < 0.25. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals higher oxygen incorporation in films deposited at lower H2/Ar ratios, deviating from the commonly reported correlation between oxygen content and photochromic response in YHO. These results demonstrate that low hydrogen partial pressure during reactive sputtering is advantageous for achieving higher optical transparency, enhanced photochromic contrast, and faster bleaching kinetics in YHO thin films.

</details>


### [127] [Tellurium trioxide as an n-type wide-gap semiconductor with ultrahigh work functions from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18076)
*Tianwei Wang,Teruya Nagafuji,Kota Hanzawa,Hidenori Hiramatsu,Fumiyasu Oba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算发现β-TeO3是一种带隙超过3 eV的宽禁带n型半导体，具有高达约8 eV的超高功函数，并系统研究了其点缺陷和表面构型对电子性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有极端功函数的下一代宽禁带半导体，对于电子与光电器件的持续发展至关重要。超高功函数材料在空穴注入/提取/传输层及透明电极等应用中具有关键价值。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，系统研究β-TeO3的电子结构；识别间隙H、Li、Na、Cu、Mg、Zn杂质及替位F杂质是否为浅施主；分析O空位等补偿缺陷的电子俘获行为；并对一系列低指数非极性表面上功函数进行评估，考察有序O空位对功函数的影响。

Result: β-TeO3带隙超过3 eV，是n型半导体，功函数最高可达约8 eV。间隙H、Li、Na、Cu、Mg、Zn及替位F为浅施主；O空位是次要的载流子补偿缺陷，会在邻近Te位局域俘获电子。表面上有序O空位的形成在能量上有利，且倾向于降低相应功函数。

Conclusion: 需精确控制O化学势以抑制表面O空位形成，从而保持β-TeO3与超高功函数相关的优异电子性能。该材料在发光器件和光伏电池的空穴注入/提取/传输层及透明电极方面具有应用潜力，同时为载流子掺杂和表面调控提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The exploration of next-generation wide-gap semiconductors with extreme work functions is highly desirable for the continued advancement of electronic and optoelectronic devices. By using first principles calculations, we show that \b{eta}-TeO3, with a wide band gap exceeding 3 eV, emerges as a promising n-type semiconductor exhibiting ultrahigh work functions of up to ~8 eV. Intentionally or unintentionally incorporated interstitial H, Li, Na, Cu, Mg, and Zn impurities and substitutional F impurities are identified as shallow donors, whereas O vacancies act as minor carrier-compensating defects that locally trap electrons at neighbouring Te sites. The work function is evaluated across a series of low-index nonpolar surfaces. The presence of ordered O vacancies is energetically favoured on these surfaces and tends to reduce the corresponding work functions. These findings underscore the significance of precise control over the O chemical potential to suppress surface O-vacancy formation, thereby preserving the exceptional electronic performance of \b{eta}-TeO3 associated with ultrahigh work functions. Collectively, the results highlight the potential of \b{eta}-TeO3 for hole injection/extraction/transport layers as well as transparent electrodes for applications such as light-emitting devices and photovoltaic cells, while providing useful guidance on carrier doping and surface tuning for these practical applications through elucidating the effects of the point-defect and surface configurations on the electronic properties.

</details>


### [128] [Local-resolvent formulation and assessment of linear-scaling Kohn-Sham calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18137)
*Albert Linda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出局部预解算符密度泛函理论（LRes-DFT），仅利用一电子预解算符的对角元在若干复能量点上的取值，避免使用遍布全系统的轨道，从而降低绝缘体系 Kohn-Sham 计算的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn-Sham 密度泛函理论计算昂贵，因为需要扩展到整个体系的轨道；对绝缘体，可以通过只使用一电子预解算符的对角元在少量复能量点上的取值来规避这一问题。

Method: 将密度和能带能量表达为预解算符对角元 g(r,z) 的线性泛函，无需轨道或动能泛函。选取 M 个等距分布在圆环上的复能量点（偏移半个间距，避免落在实轴），求和预解算符等价于以 1/(1+u^M) 填充每个能级，其中 u 为能级相对圆心、以半径为单位的能量。该填充函数是在圆内趋近 1、圆外趋近 0 的光滑阶跃，且对任意 M 取值都在 0 到 1 之间。当圆环随化学势移动时，固定电子数的力是精确的。小带隙时可采用 Gauss-Legendre 和 Zolotarev 规则以减少所需点数。三维情形用围绕每个分子的小窗口内的本征对代替预解算符，这是采用不同占据函数的划分-征服（DC）方法。

Result: 对最多 1024 个电子的 H2 分子晶体，LRes-DFT 与 Kohn-Sham 结果一致，误差达每电子 2.5×10^-7 Ha。在单核上，约 500 电子以下时比稠密对角化慢，在 1024 电子时快 3.5 倍。对无序或压缩体系以及弥散基函数，精度下降。

Conclusion: LRes-DFT 为绝缘体提供了一种无需轨道和动能泛函的高效 DFT 方案，在大体系上相比稠密对角化具有速度优势，但其精度在无序、压缩体系及弥散基函数情形下会退化。

Abstract: Kohn-Sham density-functional theory is expensive because it needs orbitals that extend over the whole system. For insulators this can be avoided by using only the diagonal of the one-electron resolvent, $g(\mathbf{r},z)=\langle\mathbf{r}|(z-\hat h)^{-1}|\mathbf{r}\rangle$, at a few complex energies $z$. We call the scheme local-resolvent density-functional theory (LRes-DFT). The density and the band energy are linear in $g$, so neither orbitals nor a kinetic-energy functional are needed. The complex energies are $M$ equally spaced points on a circle that encloses the occupied levels, offset by half a spacing so that no point lies on the real axis. Summing the resolvent over them is equivalent to occupying each level with $1/(1+u^M)$, where $u$ is the level's energy measured from the circle's center in units of its radius. This is a smooth step that is near 1 inside the circle and near 0 outside, and it stays between 0 and 1 for every $M$. If the circle moves with the chemical potential, forces at fixed electron number are exact. Gauss-Legendre and Zolotarev rules need fewer points when the gap is small. In three dimensions we use eigenpairs of small windows around each molecule instead of resolvents. This is divide-and-conquer (DC) with a different occupation function, and it is as accurate as DC with Fermi-Dirac occupations. For H$_2$ molecular crystals of up to 1024 electrons it agrees with Kohn-Sham to $2.5\times10^{-7}$ Ha per electron. On one core it is slower than dense diagonalization below about 500 electrons and 3.5 times faster at 1024 electrons. It loses accuracy for disordered or compressed systems and for diffuse basis functions.

</details>


### [129] [High-throughput screening of Heusler alloys for thermoelectrics combining machine-learned densities of states and first-principles transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18179)
*Shivam Randive,Ayana Ghosh,Rahul Dixit,Himanshu Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文评测了以电子态密度（DOS）作为筛选判据在热电材料搜索中的有效性：在DXMag数据库中10.6万个Heusler结构上，用DFT态密度筛选51,257个、用神经网络预测态密度筛选54,978个，并以3,625个能带结构与输运计算为金标准验证。结果表明，由DOS导出的Seebeck指标能将通过全部输运与稳定性测试的化合物排在其总体的前1%，且11个已知half-Heusler热电材料中有10个通过；但预测谱会丢失大部分带隙，且谱相似度指标无法察觉这一缺陷。最终认证80个计算候选材料（61个尚无实验报道）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找新热电材料需要昂贵的输运计算，无法覆盖整个数据库，因此需要靠电子态密度（尤其是机器学习预测的态密度）来挑选计算对象，但这类基于DOS的筛选方法此前很少被系统检验。

Method: 在DXMag数据库全部106,235个Heusler结构上开展大规模筛选：51,257个用密度泛函（DFT）态密度筛查，54,978个用神经网络预测态密度筛查；以3,625个能带结构和输运计算作为验证基准；提出一个从DOS导出的Seebeck指标进行排序，并用谱相似度指标评估预测谱质量。

Result: DOS导出的Seebeck指标能将通过全部输运与稳定性测试的化合物排在其所在总体的前1%；11个已知half-Heusler热电材料中有10个通过该筛选。神经网络预测的态密度丢失了大部分带隙，但谱相似度指标未能发现这一问题，而预测谱仍能将合格化合物排在靠前位置。最终认证80个计算候选材料，其中61个尚无实验报道。

Conclusion: 无论用计算谱还是预测谱，态密度都能有效决定“该算什么”；但只有能带结构才能判断带隙是否存在。DOS适合做筛选，带结构判断则必须依赖真实能带计算。

Abstract: Thermoelectrics turn waste heat into electricity, but finding new ones requires transport calculations too costly for whole databases. Searches therefore choose what to calculate from the electronic density of states (DOS), increasingly predicted by machine learning, yet such choices are rarely tested. We test them on all 106,235 Heusler structures in the DXMag database, screening 51,257 on density-functional spectra and 54,978 on neural-network predictions, and judging both by 3,625 band-structure and transport calculations. A DOS-derived Seebeck indicator ranks most compounds passing every transport and stability test within the top 1% of their population, and ten of the eleven established half-Heusler thermoelectrics calculated pass. Predicted spectra lose most band gaps, unnoticed by spectral-similarity metrics, yet still rank passing compounds near the top. The screen certifies 80 computational candidates, 61 without reported measurements. Computed or predicted, the DOS can choose what to calculate; only a band structure shows whether a gap exists.

</details>


### [130] [Orientation-Resolved Nanoindentation of Single Crystal Ti-15Mo: Inverted Elastic Anisotropy and Yield-Onset Behavior Beyond Simple CRSS Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18181)
*Kidus Abebe,David Gaetano,Aurelia Moriyama-Gurish,Jana Šmilauerová,Yanfei Gao,Udo D. Schwarz,Amit Datye*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过EBSD与纳米压痕联合，定量表征Ti-15Mo单晶在〈111〉、〈124〉、〈011〉、〈023〉、〈013〉等晶体取向下的纳米力学各向异性（硬度、压痕模量、应变速率敏感性、屈服响应），发现弹性模量各向异性发生反转、屈服起始响应无法被简单CRSS/Schmid因子模型解释，并提出残留非热ω相（通过{111}_β平面上的β→ω塌缩及可逆ω→β转变）是造成这些异常行为的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ti-15Mo是成熟的β稳定合金，其力学响应受单晶尺度上的相稳定性和变形机制强烈控制；这些晶内机制本质各向异性，因此理解弹性与塑性响应的取向依赖对织构工程化的锻件加工至关重要。

Method: 结合EBSD与纳米压痕，在〈111〉、〈124〉、〈011〉、〈023〉、〈013〉等晶体表面法向附近测量硬度、压痕模量、应变速率敏感性和压痕屈服响应；并通过应变速率跳变实验测定激活体积V*，评估CRSS/Schmid因子模型的适用性。

Result: 模量各向异性较小（约10%），但与A>1立方晶体的预测相反，〈111〉取向附近呈现最低模量；屈服起始在〈111〉附近最软且跨度达41%，硬度跨度仅6.6%且不遵循模量或屈服排序；简单CRSS/Schmid因子模型无法捕捉屈服行为；应变速率跳变测得V*=18-31b³，与弯结对形核控制的塑性一致，且按V*可将取向分为滑移主导与孪晶/相变参与两组。

Conclusion: 揭示了均匀立方β相预期弹性各向异性的反转，以及无法被简单Schmid因子模型复现的取向依赖屈服起始响应；提出残留非热ω相是致因——β→ω塌缩发生在{111}_β平面，并可通过可逆ω→β转变提供滞弹性顺应性。

Abstract: Ti-15Mo (wt%) is a well-established $β$-stabilized alloy whose mechanical response is strongly governed by phase stability and deformation mechanisms that operate at the single-grain scale. Because these intragranular mechanisms are intrinsically anisotropic, understanding the orientation dependence of the elastic and plastic response is critical to the texture-engineered processing of wrought components. Here, we quantify the nanomechanical anisotropy of single-crystal Ti-15Mo by combining EBSD with nanoindentation to correlate hardness, indentation modulus, strain rate sensitivity, and indentation yield response with the crystallographic surface normal near $\langle111\rangle,\langle124\rangle,\langle011\rangle,\langle023\rangle$, and $\langle013\rangle$ directions. While the modulus anisotropy is small (~10%), contrary to the prediction for a cubic crystal with A>1, orientations near $\langle111\rangle$ exhibit the lowest moduli. Yield onset is likewise softest near $\langle111\rangle$ and spans 41%, while hardness spans only 6.6% and follows neither the modulus nor the yield ordering. The alloy also exhibits yield behavior not captured by the simple CRSS/Schmid-factor models evaluated here. Strain-rate-jump tests yield V*=18-31b$^3$, consistent with kink-pair-nucleation-controlled plasticity; based on V*, the orientations separate into two groups suggestive of glide-dominated versus twin/transformation-participating response. We reveal an inversion of the elastic anisotropy expected for a homogeneous cubic $β$-phase and an orientation-dependent yield-onset response that is not reproduced by the simple Schmid-factor models examined here. Retained athermal $ω$ is proposed as the contributor because $β\rightarrowω$ collapse occurs on {111}$_β$ planes and may provide anelastic compliance through the reversible $ω\rightarrowβ$ transformation.

</details>


### [131] [MINFF: A Family of Classical Force Fields for Minerals with Tunable Angle Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18220)
*Michael Holmboe,Nathan Lavauzelle,Rogers E. Swai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出MINFF力场族，用于描述含Si、Al、Fe、Mg、Ca、Ti、Li和F的层状与非层状氧化物/氢氧化物矿物，不设M-O键项，通过静电与色散及单一谐波键角项并结合DDEC6电荷和电荷抹平方案来描述结构，在晶胞参数、原子距离/角度、弹性常数及水合蒙脱石衍射匹配上均优于CLAYFF与INTERFACE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矿物原子模拟受限于力场精度与可迁移性。现有矿物力场（如CLAYFF、INTERFACE）在再现晶胞指标、局域配位几何、弹性性质及水合结构等方面存在明显不足，亟需一个无需显式M-O键项、兼具高精度与可调迁移性-保真度权衡的通用力场。

Method: 构建MINFF力场族：取消M-O键项，用静电加色散决定M-O距离，并在所有M-O三重组上施加单一谐波键角项控制配位几何；金属电荷取自参考矿物结构的DDEC6电荷，氧电荷按最近邻电荷抹平方案分配（涵盖基底、顶端、羟基、边缘位点及类质同象取代邻位）；氧的Lennard-Jones参数基于OPC3水模型（逐矿物再优化变化<5%，实现与OPC3的兼容）；将金属LJ参数同步拟合到30多种参考矿物（通用GMINFF）及单独矿物（定制TMINFF），各设4档键角力常数（0、250、500、1500 kJ/mol/rad²）。

Result: 含键角项时，MINFF将实验晶胞指标再现到0.5%以内，原子类型距离和角度分别在0.7–1.4%和0.1–0.4%以内；全局不稳定指数（GII）低至0.11，优于CLAYFF（0.34）和INTERFACE（0.21）。中等键角常数可再现层状硅酸盐面内刚硬、剪切柔软的弹性常数并改善高岭石天平动（librational）谱带；在水合Na-蒙脱石中，MINFF对2W 00l测试图样的残差因子最低。通用与定制力场在4档键角刚度下可直接调控迁移性-保真度权衡。

Conclusion: MINFF通过无M-O键项、静电+色散+键角项及DDEC6电荷/电荷抹平的组合，实现了高精度、可迁移且兼容OPC3的矿物力场，并可通过通用/定制力场与键角刚度参数直接控制迁移性-保真度权衡，拓扑结构可由atom（MATLAB）与atomipy（Python）库及atomipy.io生成。

Abstract: Atomistic simulations of minerals are limited by the accuracy and transferability of the force field. We introduce MINFF, a force field family for layered and non-layered oxides and hydroxides of M being Si, Al, Fe, Mg, Ca, Ti, Li and F. MINFF has no M-O bond terms. Instead M-O distances follow from electrostatics and dispersion, while a single harmonic angle term on all M-O triplets controls coordination geometry. Metal charges are DDEC6 charges from reference mineral structures, and oxygen charges are assigned by nearest-neighbour charge smearing, covering basal, apical, hydroxyl and edge sites and sites adjacent to isomorphous substitutions. The oxygen Lennard-Jones parameters are based on the OPC3 water model and change by less than 5% when re-optimised per mineral, so compatibility with OPC3 and related ion-pair potentials is measured rather than assumed. Metal Lennard-Jones parameters were fitted to unit-cell metrics, bond distances and angles, both synchronously across more than 30 reference minerals (general, GMINFF) and for individual minerals (tailored, TMINFF), each at four angle force constants (0, 250, 500 and 1500 kJ/mol/rad^2). With angle terms, MINFF reproduces experimental unit-cells metrics to within 0.5% and atom-type distances and angles to within 0.7-1.4% and 0.1-0.4%, with Global Instability Index values as low as 0.11, below those of CLAYFF (0.34) and INTERFACE (0.21). A moderate angle constant reproduces the stiff in-plane and soft shear elastic constants of phyllosilicates and improves the Kaolinite librational bands, and in hydrated Na-Montmorillonite MINFF gives the lowest residual factor against the 2W 00l pattern of the models tested. The general and tailored sets at four angle stiffnesses give direct control over the transferability-fidelity trade-off. Topologies can be generated with the atom (MATLAB) and atomipy (Python) libraries and at www.atomipy.io.

</details>


### [132] [A Topological Spin on Racetrack Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18297)
*Benjamin Zingsem*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明在空心铁磁管中，360°畴壁可作为磁性赛道存储的局域载流子，兼具快速运动、抗扰动性和可读信号，为高速、可分辨的管状磁比特奠定物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁赛道存储器需要局域载流子同时具备快速运动、抗扰动能力和可检测信号，但现有方案难以在单一连续磁壳内同时满足这些要求。

Method: 通过解析能量分析与微磁学模拟，研究空心铁磁管中由两次连续磁化反转形成的360°畴壁（束缚对象）；测试其有限间距、对扰动的恢复能力及无需微观Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用或维持场的电流驱动输运；进一步通过相对自旋旋转、弯曲轨道和差分电流实验检验径向刚度与驱动均匀性对完整运动的影响。

Result: 360°畴壁的卷绕特性抵抗解卷，径向旋转伴随能量代价，提供了对稳定性的特定控制；畴壁支持数百米每秒的运动，强驱动下瞬态速度接近每秒一公里；制备的七位模式在脉冲输运与恢复后保持其载流子和局部读出对比度。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了快速、局部可分辨管状磁比特的物理基础，并识别出一条可通过定制激发来靶向的连续切换路径。

Abstract: Magnetic racetrack memory requires localized carriers that combine rapid motion, resistance to perturbations and a detectable signal. Here we show how a 360° domain wall in a hollow ferromagnetic tube combines these properties within a single continuous magnetic shell. Two successive magnetization reversals form a bound object whose surrounding domains point in the same direction. Analytical energetics and micromagnetic simulations establish its finite separation, recovery from tested perturbations and current driven transport without microscopic Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction or a maintaining field. The texture's winding resists unwinding while radial rotation carries an energy cost, providing a specific control over stability. Relative rotation of the constituent spins accounts quantitatively for additional displacement during current onset. The wall supports motion at hundreds of metres per second, with transient velocities approaching one kilometre per second at stronger drive. A prepared seven-bit pattern retains its carriers and local readout contrast after pulsed transport and recovery. Curved-track and differential-current tests identify radial stiffness and drive uniformity as controls for intact motion.
