These results establish a physical basis for fast, locally distinguishable tubular magnetic bits and identify a continuous switching pathway that can be targeted by tailored excitation.

</details>


### [133] [Transition-Metal-Induced Uniaxial Anisotropy in YFe$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18303)
*M. Nur Hasan,Bruno L. Fertig,Santa Pile,Rafael M. Vieira,Alena Vishina,Alexander Kovacs,Gotthard Rieger,Stefan Denneler,Samuel J. R. Holt,Martin Lang,Swapneel Pathak,Andrea Petrocchi,Hans Fangohr,Thomas George Woodcock,Thomas Schrefl,Heike C. Herper*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理筛选发现V、Nb、Ta掺杂可使YFe₃实现易面到易轴的各向异性转变，其中YFe₂.₃₃Ta₀.₆₇具有2.3 MJ/m³的单轴磁晶各向异性能，并保持高铁磁有序与热稳定性；实验证实低V浓度下可保留YFe₃相，且MAE符号反转发生在约11–14 at.% V。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发富铁永磁体的核心挑战在于同时实现单轴磁晶各向异性、高磁化强度和高热稳定性。YFe₃虽具有优异的饱和磁化强度和居里温度，但其本征易面各向异性限制了其作为永磁材料的应用，因此亟需寻找实现易轴转变的方法。

Method: 采用系统性第一性原理筛选过渡金属掺杂元素，计算磁晶各向异性能（MAE）；通过交换相互作用计算与原子尺度蒙特卡罗模拟评估磁序和热稳定性；利用微磁学模拟分析矫顽力和抗不可逆退磁能力；并对V合金化YFe₃样品进行实验表征，包括相分析、随浓度的MAE计算、电子背散射衍射（EBSD）和克尔显微镜磁畴观测。

Result: 筛选出V、Nb、Ta为有效掺杂元素；YFe₂.₃₃Ta₀.₆₇具有2.3 MJ/m³的大单轴磁晶各向异性能，并保持稳健的铁磁序和高热稳定性。微磁学模拟表明YFe₂.₃₃Ta₀.₆₇矫顽力最高、抗不可逆退磁能力最强，而YFe₂.₃₃V₀.₆₇在300 K下达到相当的最大磁能积。实验上，低V名义浓度（1–3 at.%）下可保留YFe₃相，更高V含量导致多相化；MAE符号反转发生在约11–14 at.% V，对应易面到易轴转变；低V区样品呈现面内磁畴结构。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种在保持高居里温度的同时调控YFe₃磁晶各向异性的有效途径，通过选择性过渡金属掺杂实现易面到易轴的转变，为富铁永磁体的开发开辟了前景。

Abstract: Achieving uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy while preserving high magnetization and thermal stability remains a central challenge in the development of Fe-rich permanent magnets. YFe$_3$ is a promising candidate owing to its saturation magnetization and Curie temperature, but its intrinsic easy-plane anisotropy limits its potential for permanent-magnet applications. Here, we show that selective transition-metal substitution drives an easy-plane-to-easy-axis transition in YFe$_3$. Systematic first-principles screening identifies V, Nb, and Ta as effective dopants, with YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibiting a large uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy energy of 2.3~MJ/m$^{3}$. Calculated exchange interactions and atomistic Monte Carlo simulations show that the enhanced anisotropy is achieved while preserving robust ferromagnetic order and high thermal stability. Micromagnetic simulations further reveal that YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibits the highest coercivity and strongest resistance to irreversible demagnetization, whereas YFe$_{2.33}$V$_{0.67}$ achieves a comparable maximum energy product at 300~K. Experimental characterization of V-alloyed YFe$_3$ shows that the YFe$_3$ phase can be retained at low nominal V concentrations (1-3 at.\%) under the employed synthesis and heat-treatment conditions, whereas higher V contents lead to increasingly multiphase samples. Concentration-dependent MAE calculations reveal a sign reversal between approximately 11 and 14~at.\% V, marking the transition from easy-plane to uniaxial easy-axis anisotropy. Consistent with this concentration dependence, electron backscatter diffraction and Kerr microscopy reveal in-plane magnetic domain structures in the experimentally accessible low-V regime. Therefore, our work offers a promising approach to tuning the magnetocrystalline anisotropy of YFe$_3$ while retaining high Curie temperatures.

</details>


### [134] [Accurately Generalizing Deep-Learning Hamiltonian Methods to Plane-Wave Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18317)
*Chuntian Wu,Jiaxiang Li,Qiang Xu,Xuecheng Shao,Congwei Xie,Yu Xie,Yanming Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可投影性引导的方法，从少量平面波（PW）本征态与本征值构造非正交原子轨道（AO）哈密顿量，用于混合泛函下的电子结构学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络可从材料结构预测DFT哈密顿量，但将局域原子轨道哈密顿量学习扩展到带混合泛函的平面波计算，需要一个紧凑的AO表示来保留非局域算符的效应，而现有方法依赖经验移位且学习目标存在任意性。

Method: 提出可投影性引导的构造方法：从一小组精选的平面波本征态与本征值出发，保留高可投影性态，并选取较高能态补全AO子空间，从而避免经验移位及其给学习目标带来的任意贡献；由于直接使用PW本征态与本征值，该方法无需显式重构非局域算符的AO矩阵元即可纳入参考计算中的非局域贡献。

Result: 在MoS2和钠相上的基准测试表明，该方法能以比实空间重构更小的AO基组精确复现平面波能带；所得的PBE和HSE哈密顿量可被等变神经网络学习，连接了平面波参考计算与电子结构预测。

Conclusion: 该方法拓宽了可用于AO哈密顿量学习的平面波DFT计算范围，并为开发更广泛适用的电子结构模型提供了紧凑、准确的训练目标。

Abstract: Deep neural networks that predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonians from material structures offer a route to efficient electronic-structure calculations. However, extending localized atomic-orbital (AO) Hamiltonian learning to plane-wave (PW) calculations with hybrid functionals requires a compact AO representation that preserves the effects of nonlocal operators. Here we propose a projectability-guided construction of nonorthogonal AO Hamiltonians from a small, selected set of PW eigenstates and eigenvalues. High-projectability states are retained, and selected higher-energy states complete the AO subspace, avoiding an empirical shift and its arbitrary contribution to the learning targets. Because the construction uses PW eigenstates and eigenvalues, it incorporates nonlocal contributions present in the reference calculation without explicitly reconstructing their AO matrix elements. Benchmarks for MoS2 and sodium phases show accurate reproduction of PW bands with smaller AO bases than real-space reconstruction. The resulting PBE and HSE Hamiltonians can be learned by equivariant neural networks, connecting PW reference calculations with electronic-structure prediction. This approach broadens the range of PW DFT calculations available for AO Hamiltonian learning and enables compact, accurate training targets for developing more widely applicable electronic-structure models.

</details>


### [135] [Reversible Magneto-ionic Modification of Metallic Magnetic Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18396)
*Md Golam Hafiz,Hari Babu Vasili,Philippa Shepley,Mannan Ali,Andrew J. Britton,Oscar Cespedes,Weibin Li,Manuel Valvidares,Rohit Pachat,Wilfried Fotso,Mourad Cherif,Shimpei Ono,Yves Roussigne,Mohamed Belmeguenai,Gavin Burnell*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用离子液体门控通过电驱动氧迁移对具有结构反演不对称性、垂直磁各向异性(PMA)和大Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用(DMI)的金属磁性多层膜进行可控、非易失且可逆的局域改性的研究，为基于磁畴壁和斯格明子的数据处理应用提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有结构反演不对称性、PMA和大DMI的磁性多层膜被广泛研究用于磁畴壁和斯格明子器件，但如何对这类全金属结构实现可控、非易失且可逆的局域磁学性能调控，是推动可编程自旋电子学器件发展的关键需求。

Method: 采用离子液体门控技术，通过电驱动氧离子穿过金属薄膜结构发生迁移，实现对重金属(HM)/铁磁体(FM)界面氧化态的可逆调控，并系统测量了磁各向异性、矫顽力、磁矩、DMI及磁畴行为的变化。

Result: 实验直接证明HM/FM界面氧化态可被调控：氧化使有效各向异性常数(Keff)和矫顽力(Hc)增大，氧离子还原则使其减小。正栅压从磁性层中抽取氧离子，显著降低磁畴成核场；负栅压将氧离子驱入磁性层，可逆地增强近饱和处的畴壁钉扎。负栅压同时降低磁矩和DMI。

Conclusion: 这种全金属结构中的磁离子(magneto-ionic)调控对自旋电子学器件应用十分有益，尤其适用于现场可编程的磁畴壁和斯格明子器件。

Abstract: Magnetic multilayers with structural inversion asymmetry, perpendicular magnetic anisotropy (PMA), and large Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) are widely studied for magnetic domain wall- and skyrmion-based data processing applications. In this work, we report a controlled, nonvolatile, but reversible local modification of such a system by electrically driven oxygen migration through the metallic film structure by using ionic liquid gating. Our findings provide direct evidence for the modification of the oxidation state at the heavy metal (HM)/ferromagnet (FM) interface. As a result, we observed changes in fundamental magnetic properties, such as an increase in the effective anisotropy constant (Keff) and coercive field (Hc) with oxidation, and a decrease with the reduction of oxygen ions. Positive gate voltages relative to the thin film extract oxygen ions from the magnetic layer, significantly reducing the domain nucleation field. In contrast, negative voltages drive oxygen ions into the magnetic layer, leading to a reversible increase in the extent of domain wall pinning near saturation. We observe a corresponding decrease in the magnetic moment and DMI, with negative voltage reducing both. This magneto-ionic modulation in fully metallic structures is beneficial for spintronic device applications, particularly in field-programmable domain wall and skyrmion devices.

</details>


### [136] [Substrate-controlled epitaxial-like growth and photochromic properties of yttrium oxyhydride thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18448)
*M. Zubkins,E. Strods,E. Letko,H. Mändar,D. Moldarev,K. Mizohata,K. Sarakinos,V. Vibornijs,K. Kundzins,J. Purans*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在YSZ(111)和YSZ(100)单晶衬底上通过反应磁控溅射制备的钇氧氢化物（YHO）薄膜的结构、成分与光致变色性能，发现衬底取向强烈影响薄膜微观结构与织构，但光致变色响应主要由成分及局域O/H构型决定，而非长程阳离子有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究衬底取向对YHO薄膜微观结构、结晶织构与成分的影响，并厘清结晶织构、成分与光致变色性能之间的关系，以深化对该光电材料机制的理解。

Method: 以β-YHx为前驱体，在200 °C下用反应磁控溅射法于YSZ(111)和YSZ(100)单晶衬底上沉积，随后在空气中进行沉积后氧化；同时在非晶SiO2和钠钙玻璃衬底上于200 °C和室温制备参照薄膜。采用X射线衍射、扫描电子显微镜表征结构，弹性反冲检测分析成分，并系统测定光学性能及光致变色行为。

Result: 衬底取向显著影响薄膜微观结构和织构，使薄膜呈类外延生长，镶嵌散布约3–4°。YSZ(100)上生长的YHO晶粒尺寸（10–20 nm）大于YSZ(111)上的（约8 nm），且存在面内残余压应变，但相对衬底基本弛豫。200 °C沉积薄膜的氧含量（O/Y=0.71–0.82）低于室温沉积薄膜（O/Y=0.92–1.26）。所有薄膜在太阳模拟器光照下均表现出光致变色行为，10分钟光照后相对透射率变化为7–22%。

Conclusion: 尽管类外延薄膜具有明显的结构差异，其光致变色响应并无系统性提升，说明该效应主要受成分及局域O/H构型支配，而非长程阳离子有序。该研究为理解YHO薄膜中织构、成分与光致变色性能的关系提供了新见解，有助于该材料在光学与光电子领域的应用。

Abstract: Yttrium oxyhydride (YHO) thin films are synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering of \b{eta}-YHx precursor films onto single-crystalline yttria-stabilised zirconia (YSZ) (111) and (100) substrates at 200 °C, followed by post-deposition oxidation in air. For comparison, reference films are also prepared on amorphous SiO2 and soda-lime glass substrates at both 200 °C and room temperature (RT). The structural (X-ray diffraction and scanning electron microscopy), compositional (elastic recoil detection analysis), and optical properties, including photochromic behaviour, of the films are systematically investigated. The results reveal a strong influence of substrate orientation on the film microstructure and crystallographic texture, leading to epitaxial-like growth with a mosaic spread of ~3-4°. YHO deposited on YSZ(100) exhibits larger crystallite sizes (10-20 nm) than YHO grown on YSZ(111) (~8 nm) and exhibits residual in-plane compressive strain while remaining largely relaxed with respect to the substrate. Films deposited at 200 °C exhibit lower oxygen concentrations (O/Y = 0.71-0.82) than films deposited at RT (O/Y = 0.92-1.26). All films exhibit photochromic behaviour under solar simulator illumination, with relative transmittance changes ranging from 7 to 22 % after 10 min of illumination. Despite the pronounced structural differences, the epitaxial-like films show no systematic improvement in photochromic response, indicating that the effect is governed primarily by composition and the local O/H configuration rather than by long-range cation ordering. These findings provide new insights into the relationship between crystallographic texture, composition, and photochromic properties in YHO thin films, contributing to the understanding of this material for optical and optoelectronic applications.

</details>


### [137] [Quantitative Modelling of Solvation in 2D Materials via Alchemical Decoupling and Potential Energy Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18490)
*Lindsey N. Ioos,Duncan A. Peterson,Scott C. Warren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用溶剂化吉布斯自由能校准两个不相连势能面的方法，以更准确地模拟二维材料在溶液中的稳定性，结果显示溶剂单层会残留在看似聚集的材料层间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维材料为何能悬浮于溶液中对阐明其聚集与剥离机制至关重要，但此类动力学因涉及长时间尺度和较大活化能而难以建模，亟需改进的模拟策略。

Method: 采用改进的建模策略：利用溶剂化吉布斯自由能精确校准完全去溶剂化与完全溶剂化两种状态下二维材料的两个不相连势能面，再通过溶剂化能将其连接，从而预测材料在不同溶剂中的稳定性。

Result: 所得预测与先前模拟在定性上不同，并与实验结果高度吻合；该方法预测材料完全去溶剂化具有更高能垒，表明看似聚集的二维材料层间实际仍保留着溶剂单层。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了溶剂与二维材料间相互作用的关键认知，为未来实验研究提供了指导。

Abstract: Understanding why 2D materials remain suspended in solutions is essential to understanding the mechanisms of aggregation and exfoliation. These dynamics have been difficult to model because of the long timescales and large activation energies involved in these simulations. Here we describe an improved strategy for modelling the solvation and stability of 2D materials that uses the Gibbs free energy of solvation to accurately calibrate two disconnected potential energy surfaces for the fully desolvated and fully solvated 2D materials. By joining these surfaces using solvation energy, we obtain predictions for 2D material stability in various solvents that are qualitatively different than prior simulations and that have excellent agreement with experimental results. Our approach predicts a much higher barrier to full desolvation of the material, suggesting that materials that appear to be aggregated in fact still retain monolayers of solvent between adjacent layers of 2D materials. This work provides much needed understanding on the interactions between the solvent and 2D material that can guide future experimental work.

</details>


### [138] [Flexoelectricity and Flexomagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18525)
*Alexander Edström,Massimiliano Stengel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了应变梯度（及其在低维弯曲体系中的体现——曲率）如何作为空间反演破缺场，解锁原本被晶体对称性禁止的电学与磁学耦合，涵盖挠曲电性、挠曲磁性及相关非线性、磁电与Rashba型现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀应变不改变空间反演对称性，而应变梯度在空间反演下是奇函数，能解锁在母体晶体中被严格禁止的耦合。在纳米尺度和弯曲低维系统中，这种梯度易得且常不可避免，曲率因此成为“写入晶格本身”的对称性破缺场。作者旨在系统梳理这类耦合在电极化与磁性上的体现，并超越既有的曲率几何处理框架。

Method: 本文为综述章节。首先建立应变梯度与曲率之间的几何关系；随后以挠曲电性为参考案例，综述其体贡献、表面贡献与动态贡献、微观机制、在二维材料中的表现及第一性原理计算方法；再以磁电效应为概念桥梁，广义地发展“挠曲磁性”，讨论磁化对曲率的线性响应及曲率诱导的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，并拓展到非线性效应、非均匀磁电与Rashba型现象，以及与交变磁性（altermagnetism）的联系。

Result: 挠曲电性被确立为理解应变梯度-电耦合的成熟基准；挠曲磁性被拓展为涵盖磁性-应变梯度任意耦合的广义框架，具体包括曲率诱导的线性磁化响应与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，并延伸至非线性效应、非均匀磁电与Rashba型现象及altermagnetism关联。上述效应均可归结为曲率作为局域空间反演破缺场所引发的现象。

Conclusion: 曲率充当对称性破缺场，解锁了均匀晶体中禁戒的电极化与磁性耦合。挠曲磁性作为广义概念，将曲率诱导的磁学现象（线性磁化响应、DMI乃至非线性、磁电与Rashba型效应和altermagnetism联系）统一纳入，显示出弯曲低维系统在磁电功能材料研究中的重要潜力。

Abstract: Strain gradients, unlike homogeneous strain, are odd under space inversion and unlock couplings that are strictly forbidden in the parent crystal. At the nanoscale, and in curved low-dimensional systems in particular, such gradients are readily attainable and often unavoidable, so that curvature acts as a symmetry-breaking field written into the lattice itself. This chapter reviews the resulting couplings to electric polarization and magnetism. After establishing the geometric relation between strain gradients and curvature, we review flexoelectricity as the better-established reference case: the bulk, surface and dynamic contributions that complicate its definition, its microscopic mechanisms, its manifestation in two-dimensional materials, and the first-principles methodology now available for its calculation. Using the magnetoelectric effect as a conceptual bridge, we then develop flexomagnetism in a broad sense, covering any coupling phenomena between magnetism and strain gradients, and specifically discuss the linear magnetization response to curvature and curvature-induced Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, beyond the established curvilinear treatment. Finally, we briefly describe some additional effects fitting under this broad umbrella of flexomagnetism, including nonlinear ones, inhomogeneous magnetoelectric and Rashba-type phenomena, and connections to altermagnetism.

</details>


### [139] [Imaging Gate-Tunable Single-Layer Molecular Kagome Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18539)
*Ziyi Wang,Rafal Zuzak,Weichen Tang,Haowen Pang,Yutang Qiao,Dhanvanth Balakrishnan,Yuqiao Yan,Aining Hu,Andrew Sun,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Feng Wang,Steven G. Louie,Michael F. Crommie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了在范德华衬底（如基于石墨烯的场效应器件）上利用金属盐分子束外延生长单层Ni₃(HITP)₂金属有机框架，通过低温扫描隧道谱观测到kagome衍生的平带和范霍夫奇点特征，并通过栅压调控实现了对二维kagome电子晶格能谱的静电控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统kagome材料的相关电子态往往嵌入在化学复杂的三维晶体中，难以隔离和调控；而分子基kagome网络虽具结构精度和可调性，却大多局限于与二维器件不兼容的金属衬底上。因此需要一种能在器件兼容衬底上实现可调控kagome电子态的途径。

Method: 采用金属盐分子束外延（MBE）在范德华衬底（包括基于石墨烯的场效应器件）上生长单层Ni₃(HITP)₂金属有机框架；利用低温扫描隧道谱（STS）分辨kagome衍生的平带与范霍夫奇异性谱学特征，并与第一性原理计算对照；通过对混合二维MOF器件施加栅压，实现能谱特征的能动移位。

Result: 成功在范德华衬底上生长出单层Ni₃(HITP)₂ MOF；低温STS观测到与第一性原理计算一致的kagome平带和范霍夫奇点谱学特征；通过栅压调控，能够能动地移动kagome谱学特征，证实了对化学可编程二维kagome电子晶格的静电控制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在器件兼容衬底上生长并静电调控化学可编程二维kagome电子晶格的方案，为探索和利用kagome平带及相关量子行为提供了新平台。

Abstract: Kagome lattices provide a simple geometry for engineering electronic states that exhibit novel quantum behavior. In conventional kagome materials, however, the relevant states are often embedded in chemically complex three-dimensional crystals and are difficult to isolate or tune. Molecule-based kagome networks offer structural precision and tunability, but have largely been confined to metallic substrates incompatible with 2D devices. Here we report growth of single-layer Ni$_3$(HITP)$_2$ metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) on van der Waals substrates including graphene-based field-effect devices using metal-salt molecular beam epitaxy. Low-temperature scanning tunneling spectroscopy resolves kagome-derived flat-band and van Hove singularity-based spectral features consistent with first-principles calculations. Gating hybrid 2D MOF devices allows us to energetically shift kagome spectral features, thus establishing electrostatic control of chemically programmable 2D kagome electronic lattices.

</details>


### [140] [A Transferable Machine-Learned Potential Enabling Large-Scale Copper-Carbon Simulations from Equilibrium to Extreme Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18545)
*Feiyu Zhou,Wei Qiu,Yan Chen,Jiahui Liu,Yilun Liu,Shengying Yue,Penghua Ying*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了NEP-CuC——一种可迁移的神经进化势，用于描述金属Cu、多样碳键合及Cu-C异质界面，并通过ZBL短程修正和D3(BJ)色散修正扩展适用范围；该势在结构、弹性、缺陷、界面、振动、输运及非平衡模拟中均表现出良好的物理保真度，并成功应用于Cu/金刚石界面的沉积-退火-拉伸全流程模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu-C材料与界面在金刚石热管理、金属化、石墨烯生长和碳涂层技术中处于核心地位，但跨这些应用的原子模拟需要一个能够同时描述金属Cu、多样碳键合、异质Cu-C相互作用以及近距离原子碰撞的势函数。

Method: 开发了NEP-CuC神经进化势，训练数据来自从异质Cu-C候选池中均匀重新标记的2727个构型；采用五个独立训练模型集成的不一致性作为模型敏感性的内部指标；引入Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark（ZBL）修正描述短程核排斥，并采用运行时D3(BJ)修正增强色散敏感计算；通过源数据留出集、独立外部数据集以及结构、弹性、缺陷、界面、振动和有限温度输运等基准进行全面验证。

Result: NEP-CuC在元素Cu、多样碳环境和异质Cu-C构型上均建立了广泛但依赖性质的物理保真度；成功进行了非晶碳形成和取向依赖的Cu冲击压缩等强非平衡大尺度模拟；对Cu/金刚石界面，同一势函数描述了沉积、退火和拉伸响应，发现制备历史会在相间结构中留下持久差异，且当初始沉积阶段入射Cu能量从1 eV增加到50 eV时，500 K下的平均峰值名义拉伸应力从12.35±0.71 GPa降至6.65±0.98 GPa。

Conclusion: NEP-CuC提供了一个连接加工工艺、界面结构与物理响应的原子模拟框架，可直接应用于Cu/金刚石热管理界面、金刚石金属化以及非平衡Cu-C加工等领域。

Abstract: Cu-C materials and interfaces are central to diamond thermal management, metallization, graphene growth and carbon-coating technologies, but atomistic simulations across these applications require a potential that describes metallic Cu, diverse carbon bonding, heterogeneous Cu-C interactions and close atomic encounters. Here we develop NEP-CuC, a transferable neuroevolution potential trained on a dataset of 2,727 uniformly relabelled configurations selected from a heterogeneous Cu-C candidate pool. Disagreement within an ensemble of five independently trained models provides an internal indicator of model sensitivity. A Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) correction describes short-range nuclear repulsion, while a runtime D3(BJ) correction supplements dispersion-sensitive calculations. Tests on source-derived held-out and independent external datasets, together with structural, elastic, defect, interfacial, vibrational and finite-temperature transport benchmarks, establish broad but property-dependent physical fidelity across elemental Cu, diverse carbon environments and heterogeneous Cu-C configurations. Large-scale simulations of amorphous-carbon formation and orientation-dependent shock compression of Cu further test transferability under strongly non-equilibrium conditions. For Cu/diamond interfaces, the same potential describes deposition, annealing and tensile response: preparation history leaves persistent differences in interphase structure, while the mean peak nominal tensile stress at 500 K decreases from $12.35\pm0.71$ to $6.65\pm0.98$ GPa as the incident Cu energy during the initial deposition stage increases from 1 to 50 eV. NEP-CuC provides an atomistic framework for linking processing, interfacial structure and physical response across Cu-C systems, with direct relevance to Cu/diamond thermal-management interfaces, diamond metallization and non-equilibrium Cu-C processing.

</details>


### [141] [Direct growth of crystalline spinel GaON films: A wide-bandgap mixed-anion semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18550)
*Baikui Li,Zecheng Yang,Wenhao Yin,Zijing Wu,Haitao Zhao,Yunwei Sheng,Honglei Wu,Jiannong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了通过氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积直接生长结晶尖晶石结构γ-GaON薄膜，并结合结构和光电表征确认其带边跃迁约为4.3 eV，为混合阴离子宽带隙半导体的研究开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合阴离子半导体为超越传统多阳离子合金的带隙工程提供了机会，但竞争相与无序阻碍了其合成和电子性质的确定。GaON有望连接重要的GaN与Ga₂O₃体系，然而其晶体结构、组分与带隙之间的明确关系一直难以建立。

Method: 采用氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积（PLD）直接生长结晶γ-GaON薄膜；通过调节氮/氧供应获得单斜β-Ga₂O₃、以无序为主的薄膜以及结晶尖晶石GaON；利用互补的衍射、原子分辨率显微术与元素分析确认尖晶石结构和氮的掺入；采用变温光电流谱研究光电响应。

Result: 结晶γ-GaON薄膜的光电流响应呈现明显红移，从而支持其在室温下带边跃迁约为4.3 eV的归属；并系统揭示了结构与组分随氮/氧供给的变化规律。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究混合阴离子宽带隙半导体物理以及发展相关异质结构提供了新的机会。

Abstract: Mixed-anion semiconductors offer opportunities for bandgap engineering beyond conventional multication alloys, but competing phases and disorder complicate their synthesis and the identification of their electronic properties. Gallium oxynitride (GaON) could connect the technologically important GaN and Ga2O3 systems, yet establishing a clear relation between its crystal structure, composition and bandgap remains challenging. Here we report the direct growth of crystalline spinel gamma-GaON films by nitrogen-plasma-assisted pulsed-laser deposition. Varying the nitrogen and oxygen supply yields monoclinic beta-Ga2O3, predominantly disordered films and crystalline spinel GaON. Complementary diffraction, atomic-resolution microscopy and elemental analysis establish the spinel structure and nitrogen incorporation. Temperature-dependent photocurrent spectroscopy reveals a clear redshift of the crystalline gamma-GaON response, supporting assignment of a band-edge transition near 4.3 eV at room temperature. These results open opportunities to investigate mixed-anion wide-bandgap semiconductor physics and develop heterostructures.

</details>


### [142] [Time-Resolved Phonon Dispersions of Thermally Excited Graphite at the Nanoscale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18561)
*Mairi McCauley,Christoph T. Koch,Benedikt Haas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过将动量分辨的振动电子能量损失谱（EELS）与脉冲激光加热及同步门控电子探测相结合，首次在微秒时间尺度上实现了对石墨加热和冷却过程中声子色散的时间分辨测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格振动决定材料中的热量传播，通过时间分辨声子色散测量研究其动力学有助于理解声子激发及其相互作用。然而，此前尚无在纳米尺度直接分辨声子色散的时间分辨测量方法，需要一种能同时获得能量和动量分辨并具备纳米空间分辨与时间分辨能力的探测手段。

Method: 将动量分辨的振动EELS与脉冲激光加热相结合，并采用同步门控电子探测，在激光激发后的特定时刻同时记录增益谱和损耗谱；由二者确定各时间延迟下的温度，进而追踪声子支随温度的变化。该技术兼容低至50 ns的探测器门控。

Result: 随着温度升高，石墨的光学声子支能量下降且峰宽增大，这一变化与温度依赖的声子重整化及由非谐相互作用引起的散射一致。

Conclusion: 该工作演示了基于振动EELS的时间分辨声子色散谱学，为在纳米尺度下同时以能量和动量分辨探测温度依赖的声子动力学提供了途径，并可进一步扩展到更快的时间动力学。

Abstract: Lattice vibrations govern the propagation of heat in materials, and investigating their dynamics through time-resolved phonon dispersion measurements would provide insight into these excitations and their interactions. However, time-resolved measurements that directly resolve the phonon dispersion at the nanoscale have not been previously demonstrated. Momentum-resolved vibrational electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) provides access to phonon energies and momenta with nanoscale spatial resolution. Here, we combine momentum-resolved vibrational EELS with pulsed laser heating and synchronised gated electron detection to observe the phonon dispersion of graphite during controlled heating and cooling on the microsecond timescale. Recording both the gain and loss spectra at defined times relative to laser excitation, we determine the temperature at each time delay and follow changes to the phonon branches as a function of temperature. The momentum-resolved spectra reveal a decrease in energy of the optical phonon branch with increasing temperature accompanied by an increase in peak width. These changes are consistent with temperature-dependent phonon renormalisation and scattering associated with anharmonic interactions. The approach is compatible with detector gating on timescales down to 50 ns, providing a route towards extending these measurements to faster dynamics. This work demonstrates time-resolved phonon dispersion spectroscopy using vibrational EELS and provides a route towards probing temperature-dependent phonon dynamics at the nanoscale with simultaneous energy and momentum resolution.

</details>


### [143] [Composition-controlled optical fingerprints and anisotropy of self-trapped excitons in double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18665)
*Simone Grillo,Offir Zachs Maishlos,Liya Koren,Rotem Strassberg,Rachel Lifer,Edi Maizenberg,Umberto De Giovannini,Angel Rubio,Yehonadav Bekenstein,Ofer Neufeld*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合光学光谱与第一性原理计算，研究了无铅Sb掺杂Cs2BInCl6双钙钛矿纳米晶（B位从Na向K合金化）的光致发光机理。发现合金化使PL能量从约2.48调至2.70 eV，而约3.8 eV的吸收几乎不变；Sb掺杂开辟了亮辐射复合通道并使激子波函数离域化；自陷激子（STE）导致了大的PL红移，Na→K取代选择性地改变STE晶格畸变；理论预测并实验证实了PL偏振各向异性，可作为STE波函数空间结构的指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双钙钛矿（DPs）为光催化和光电子学提供了组分可调的平台，但其软晶格易导致强激子-声子耦合与自陷激子（STE），产生宽化、红移的光致发光（PL），限制了理解和应用。作者希望厘清掺杂与合金化在无铅DPs中各自的作用，并区分PL与吸收对组分变化响应不同的根源。

Method: 采用光学光谱（含时间分辨偏振各向异性测量）与从头算（ab initio）计算相结合的方法，研究无铅Sb掺杂Cs2BInCl6纳米晶（B位Na→K合金化）。通过对比PL能量、吸收特征，并结合理论计算揭示Sb掺杂对辐射复合通道与激子波函数离域化的影响；识别STE及其晶格畸变，分析Na→K取代对STE畸变的选择性改变；理论预测PL偏振各向异性并用时间分辨各向异性实验验证。

Result: 合金化将PL能量从约2.48调至2.70 eV，而约3.8 eV处的吸收特征几乎不变；Sb掺杂产生亮辐射复合通道并使激子波函数离域化；STE（伴随掺杂杂化带隙内态向上移动）导致大的PL红移；Na→K取代选择性地改变STE晶格畸变，解释了PL对组分敏感而吸收不敏感的机理；理论预测PL偏振各向异性反映STE波函数空间结构且依赖B位组分，时间分辨各向异性测量与预测高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究厘清了无铅双钙钛矿中掺杂与合金化的不同作用，并确立了偏振分辨光谱作为探测STE波函数及其随合金化演化的实验手段，为理解和调控无铅DPs的发光性质提供了新途径。

Abstract: Double perovskites (DPs) offer a compositionally versatile platform for photocatalysis and optoelectronics, but their soft lattices favor strong exciton-phonon coupling and self-trapped excitons (STEs), which produce broad, red-shifted photoluminescence (PL). Here, we combine optical spectroscopy and ab initio calculations to study lead-free, Sb-doped Cs2BInCl6 nanocrystals with B-site alloying from Na to K. Alloying tunes the PL energy from about 2.48 to 2.70 eV, while absorption features near 3.8 eV remain nearly unchanged. Calculations reveal that Sb doping enables bright radiative recombination channels and delocalizes the excitonic wavefunction. We identify STEs responsible for the large PL red shift, associated with an upward shift of dopant-hybridized in-gap states. Na-to-K substitution selectively changes the STE lattice distortion, explaining why PL varies strongly with composition while absorption does not. Theory further predicts pronounced PL polarization anisotropy that reflects the STE wavefunction's spatial structure and depends on B-site composition. Time-resolved anisotropy measurements closely agree with these predictions, providing experimental fingerprints of the excitonic wavefunction and its evolution with alloying. Together, these results disentangle the roles of doping and alloying in lead-free DPs and establish polarization-resolved spectroscopy as a probe of STE wavefunctions.

</details>

Result: 建立电磁无源性与闭系统能量衰减，得到零供给边界条件下的唯一性；分数极化功率分解为非负历史储能与非负本构耗散率；第二种扩散表示的储能不超过历史储能，二者之差为仅在零极化历史时为零的非负加权方差；分数阶数展宽介质损耗频带、重新分布波衰减、增强可积初始功率层并延长阶跃响应尾部；精确电阶跃响应验证了两种能量表示的一致性。

Conclusion: 该公式将分数初始层、遗传储能、无源性、扩散弛豫、色散、吸收与衰减统一于一个局部本构模型中，为多分数通道各向异性 Cole–Cole 介质的能量分析提供了严格且自洽的理论基础。

Abstract: This study develops an energy formulation for linear anisotropic Maxwell--Cole--Cole media with multiple fractional polarization channels, passive conductivity, prescribed currents, and finite-terminal Caputo memory with zero prehistory. A weighted solution class captures the integrable fractional initial layer generated by quiescent polarization. Fractional polarization power then separates into a nonnegative history storage and a nonnegative constitutive dissipation rate. Although the instantaneous rate may diverge at initialization, it remains integrable, while stored history energy and accumulated loss vanish initially. Combined with the Poynting theorem, this decomposition yields a global field-plus-memory identity that establishes electromagnetic passivity, closed-system energy decay, and conditional uniqueness under zero-supply boundary conditions. The framework also recovers the Debye, instantaneous-dielectric, vanishing-susceptibility, and vacuum limits under convergence assumptions. A positive diffusive representation provides a second decomposition whose storage never exceeds the history storage. Their difference is an exact nonnegative weighted variance that vanishes only for a zero polarization history. In agreement with these time-domain results, the finite-time harmonic response approaches the anisotropic multichannel Cole--Cole permittivity, whose Hermitian imaginary part ensures nonnegative cycle-averaged absorption. A reproducible single-channel study shows that fractional order broadens the dielectric-loss band, redistributes wave attenuation, strengthens the integrable initial power layer, and prolongs the step-response tail. An exact electric-step response verifies both energy representations. The formulation connects fractional initial layers, hereditary storage, passivity, diffusive relaxation, dispersion, absorption, and attenuation in a local constitutive model.

</details>


### [145] [Comment on "Landau Levels and Riemann Zeros"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14108)
*Mi-Ra Hwang,Eylee Jung,MuSeong Kim,DaeKil Park*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Sierra-Townsend模型中任意朗道能级的本征函数及其边界渐近行为，证明边界条件在任意固定能级n下仅差一个与能量无关的相位因子，从而不产生ln E项，并澄清ST文中半经典公式中ln E项的来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sierra和Townsend提出Connes的"吸收谱"模型可视为带电粒子在正交电磁场中的最低朗道能级极限，并推测更高朗道能级贡献黎曼零点计数函数N(E)的涨落部分。本文旨在检验该推测，厘清在ST所用近似下各朗道能级的边界条件与渐进行为，特别是ln E项是否出现。

Method: 在Sierra-Townsend采用的近似下，构造任意朗道能级n的本征函数，并推导其在ST所识别的两个"盒子"边界处的渐近形式；比较不同能级n下边界条件的能量依赖关系，并与半经典计数公式对照。

Result: 任意固定能级n的边界条件与n=0情形相同，仅相差一个与能量E无关的相位因子(-i)^n，因此对任意固定n均不产生ln E项；ST式(29)中半经典的ln E项实为光滑LLL计数函数在平移E→E+γz²下的一阶移动。

Conclusion: 在ST近似下，所有朗道能级的边界条件能量无关（仅带相位(-i)^n），故不贡献ln E项；ST半经典公式中的ln E项并非来自高朗道能级，而是光滑最低朗道能级计数函数的一阶能量平移效应。

Abstract: Sierra and Townsend (ST) [1] showed that the ``absorption spectrum'' model of Connes [2] arises as the lowest-Landau-level (LLL) limit of a charged particle in crossed electric and magnetic fields, and suggested that the higher Landau levels supply the fluctuation part of the Riemann-zero counting function $N(E)$. Within the approximation used by ST we construct the eigenfunctions of an arbitrary Landau level $n$ and derive their asymptotics at the two box boundaries that ST identify. The boundary condition is the same as for $n=0$ up to an $E$-independent phase $(-\ii)^n$, so no $\ln E$ term arises for any fixed $n$. We also note that the $\ln E$ term in the semiclassical Eq.~(29) of ST is the first-order shift of the smooth LLL counting function, $E\to E+γz^2$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解量子自旋系统的局域结构，回答 Haah 提出的问题：将 (3+1) 维量子自旋系统的局域保持对称群（即量子元胞自动机模去稳定化、有限深度量子电路和平移）与辫融合范畴的 Witt 群联系起来。

Method: 作者发展了一个基于拓扑 n-网的同伦框架：拓扑 n-网将结合幺半代数指派给 n 维空间中的局部区域，以捕捉底层格点理论的局域结构。该网论视角自然支持范畴化，使得拓扑 n-网的范畴带有一个高阶 Morita 范畴结构。此外，最有物理意义的网形成一个 Karoubi 完备子范畴，其结构特征与有限 TQFT 的范畴相似。

Result: 主要结果是将 (3+1) 维量子自旋系统的局域保持对称群（即量子元胞自动机模去稳定化、有限深度量子电路和平移）等同于非退化辫融合范畴的完全 Witt 群，从而回答了 Haah 提出的问题。

Conclusion: 拓扑 n-网的同伦框架成功地将量子自旋系统的局域对称结构与高阶范畴论及辫融合范畴的 Witt 理论联系起来，为回答 Haah 的问题提供了严格的数学证明。

Abstract: We develop a homotopical framework for understanding the space of quantum spin systems based on topological $n$-nets, which assign associative unital algebras to local regions in an $n$-dimensional space in a manner that captures the locality structure of the underlying lattice theories. The net-theoretic perspective admits a natural categorification, in which the category of topological $n$-nets carries the structure of a higher Morita category. Moreover, the nets of greatest physical interest form a Karoubi-complete subcategory that shares many of the characteristic structural features of the category of finite TQFTs. Our main result identifies the group of locality-preserving symmetries of (3+1)-dimensional quantum spin systems (i.e. quantum cellular automata modulo stabilization, finite-depth quantum circuits, and translations) with the full Witt group of nondegenerate braided fusion categories, thereby answering a question posed by Haah.

</details>

Result: 成功构造了旋量记号下的微分 1-形式和 2-形式，给出狄拉克算子的外微积分表述，并实现了向非单演函数的推广。结果表明旋量外微积分具有内部自洽性，显示出其在相对论场方程与量子理论几何表述中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: This article presents an introductory study of $p$-differential forms in spinor variables defined on the Minkowskian (or Lorentzian) manifold. Starting from the algebraic and geometric foundations of spinor spaces and Clifford algebras, we establish the correspondence between Minkowski spacetime and Hermitian matrices in spinor coordinates. Within this framework, we construct differential 1-forms and 2-forms expressed in spinor notation, and formulate the Dirac operator in terms of exterior calculus. Furthermore, we extend this approach to the case of non-monogenic functions, following the method proposed in~\cite{Jaramillo2024}. The results illustrate the consistency of the spinorial exterior calculus and its potential applications in the study of relativistic field equations and geometric formulations of quantum theory.

</details>


### [148] [Inverse Resonance Problem for the Half-Line Schrödinger Operator with a Complex Robin Boundary Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14323)
*Roman Shterenberg*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究半直线上具有紧支撑复值位势和复 Robin 边界条件的一维 Schrödinger 算子的逆共振问题，将 Dirichlet 情形的结果推广到 Robin 情形，证明固定 Robin 参数下完整的特征值与共振（计多重度）唯一确定位势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marletta、Shterenberg 与 Weikard 已对 Dirichlet 问题建立逆共振理论，但 Robin 边界条件（u'(0)-a u(0)=0, a 为复数）下的对应问题尚未解决。作者旨在将该策略推广，处理 Robin 特征函数 F_a(z;q)=ψ_x(z,0;q)-aψ(z,0;q) 取代 Dirichlet Jost 函数 ψ(z,0) 后带来的新困难。

Method: 沿用 MSW 的变换算子、整函数与 Fourier 分析策略。核心改动是以 Robin 特征函数 F_a(z;q)=ψ_x(z,0;q)-aψ(z,0;q) 替换 Dirichlet Jost 函数。关键新特征是渐近行为 F_a(z;q)=iz-a+O(1)（而非 1+O(z^{-1})），变换算子论证中相应的边界量变为 B_x(0,t)-aB(0,t) 以及标量项 B(0,0)。据此建立大能量估计、Hadamard 因子分解、有限数据稳定性与唯一性结果。

Result: 对固定 a∈C，以及 a 在 C 的有界子集内一致地，所得逆共振估计与 MSW 对 Dirichlet 问题证明的结果具有相同的定性形式。特别地，对固定 Robin 参数 a，完整的 Robin 特征值与共振集合（计多重度）唯一确定位势。

Conclusion: 作者成功将 Dirichlet 逆共振理论推广到一般复 Robin 边界条件情形，尽管 Robin 特征函数与 Dirichlet Jost 函数有不同的渐近行为，仍能建立同类的稳定性与唯一性定理，表明完整的 Robin 谱数据唯一确定位势。

Abstract: We study the inverse resonance problem for the one-dimensional Schrödinger operator on the half-line with a compactly supported complex-valued potential and a complex Robin boundary condition \[ u'(0)-a u(0)=0,\qquad a\in\mathbb C. \] The proof follows the transformation-operator, entire-function, and Fourier analytic strategy developed by Marletta, Shterenberg and Weikard for the Dirichlet problem \cite{MSW}. The Dirichlet Jost function $ψ(z,0)$ is replaced by the Robin characteristic function \[ F_a(z;q)=ψ_x(z,0;q)-aψ(z,0;q). \] We establish the corresponding large-energy estimates, Hadamard factorization, finite-data stability, and uniqueness results. The principal new feature is that $F_a(z;q)=iz-a+O(1)$ rather than $1+O(z^{-1})$. In the transformation-operator argument the relevant boundary quantity is $B_x(0,t)-aB(0,t)$, together with the scalar term $B(0,0)$. For fixed $a\in\mathbb C$, and uniformly for $a$ in bounded subsets of $\mathbb C$, the resulting inverse resonance estimates have the same qualitative form as those proved in \cite{MSW} for the Dirichlet problem. In particular, for a fixed Robin parameter $a$, the complete set of Robin eigenvalues and resonances, counted with multiplicity, determines the potential uniquely.
  The text is produced with the help of AI ChatGPT for Academy.

</details>


### [149] [Yang-Mills Theory in the Multisymplectic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14418)
*David Villa-Blanco*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本硕士论文将多辛场论框架系统性地构建并应用于纯杨-米尔斯理论，涵盖拉格朗日/哈密顿多辛形式、奇异拉格朗日约束算法、瞬时预辛公式、对称性与诺特定理，最后专门化到主丛规范理论并复现杨-米尔斯方程与守恒流/荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典杨-米尔斯理论通常以时空协变的张量形式或瞬时的辛几何形式处理，两者之间的协变联系与几何结构（尤其是规范对称性、约束与守恒律）需要统一的框架加以整合；多辛场论正好为把一阶场论的协变表述、哈密顿表述、奇异拉格朗日约束以及诺特定理纳入同一几何语言提供了动机。

Method: 论文从射流丛与庞加莱-嘉当形式的几何基础出发，构建一阶场论的拉格朗日多辛形式并导出欧拉-拉格朗日方程，将辛力学的泊松括号推广为分级李代数；随后建立与多辛哈密顿形式的勒让德对应（含奇异拉格朗日的约束算法）；通过柯西面切片打破洛伦兹协变性，得到瞬时预辛（无穷维）表述及其德唐德方程与预辛括号；再系统处理对称性，建立多辛与瞬时动量映射及多种诺特定理版本，最后专门化到主丛规范理论，以联络丛为多辛位形空间、把规范对称视为对称，构建杨-米尔斯拉格朗日量并作局域平凡化计算。

Result: 成功复现了标准张量形式杨-米尔斯方程与比安基恒等式，得到杨-米尔斯守恒流与守恒荷，导出了一类哈密顿形式的分级代数；同时恢复了欧拉-拉格朗日方程、哈密顿方程、瞬时德唐德方程与预辛括号，并给出多辛与瞬时动量映射及诺特定理的多个版本。

Conclusion: 多辛场论框架能统一且协变地处理杨-米尔斯理论，把其位形空间、规范对称性、运动方程、守恒律与代数结构纳入同一几何语言，验证了该框架对规范场论的有效性与一致性。

Abstract: This Master's thesis develops and applies the multisymplectic field-theory framework to (pure) Yang-Mills theory. Building from the geometric foundations of jet bundles and the Poincaré-Cartan forms, the work constructs the Lagrangian multisymplectic formalism for first-order field theories, recovering the usual Euler-Lagrange field equations and extending the Poisson brackets of symplectic mechanics to a full graded Lie algebra. It then establishes the Legendre correspondence with the multisymplectic Hamiltonian formalism (in which Hamilton equations are retrieved) including the case of singular Lagrangians (where a constraint algorithm is reproduced). Breaking Lorentz covariance via a Cauchy surface slicing, the thesis then relates the covariant multisymplectic picture to an instantaneous presymplectic (infinite-dimensional) formulation in which the instantaneous De Donder equations and presymplectic brackets are obtained in a compatible way. A comprehensive treatment of symmetries then leads to multisymplectic and instantaneous momentum maps generalizations and to several versions of Noether's theorems.
  Specializing the formalism to principal-bundle gauge theory, the thesis identifies the multisymplectic configuration space with the bundle of connections, gauge symmetries with symmetries in the multisymplectic language and builds the Yang-Mills Lagrangian. Lastly, among many computations, the standard tensorial Yang-Mills equations and the Bianchi identity in local trivializations are retrieved, Yang-Mills conserved currents and charges are obtained and the graded algebra is derived for a special class of Hamiltonian forms.

</details>


### [150] [Non-Intrusive Balancing for Data-Driven Model Reduction with Oblique Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15219)
*Alberto Padovan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非侵入式平衡降阶建模框架，利用前向仿真数据计算非线性经验能达性格拉姆矩阵与能观性格拉姆矩阵的平衡斜投影，结合算子推断学习潜空间动力学，在多个算例（含8千万自由度的翼型绕流）中将预报误差降低最高达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非侵入式降阶方法依赖正交投影到由最小化静态重构误差识别的高能线性或非线性流形上，会截断低能量但动力学上重要的状态；而平衡截断等侵入式方法虽能通过伴随信息给出动力学感知的斜投影，却需要访问求解器内部。因此亟需一种仅用前向仿真数据即可计算平衡斜投影的非侵入式方法。

Method: 作者提出非侵入式计算非线性经验能达性格拉姆矩阵，并将其与投影到合适限制子空间上的能观性对偶格拉姆矩阵进行平衡；当动力学为线性且任一格拉姆矩阵的值域包含于限制子空间时，该过程可证明地恢复平衡截断的斜投影。在线性且无伴随信息情形下，证明minimax最优限制子空间由能达性格拉姆矩阵的主特征空间张成，从而得到计算成本与标准SVD相当的平衡算法。得到斜投影后，在其值域上使用算子推断学习潜空间动力学。

Result: 该方法在多个复杂度递增的算例上验证，包括具有8×10^7自由度的三维不可压缩翼型绕流；所有算例中，平衡算子推断模型均显著优于基于POD的对比模型，预报误差最多降低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文证明了面向非线性系统的平衡斜投影可完全通过前向仿真数据非侵入式地计算，无需伴随信息，且在线性情形下与平衡截断一致。该方法有效弥补了传统非侵入式降阶模型在大幅瞬态能量增长动力学下精度不足的缺陷，具有与标准SVD相当的计算效率和广泛的工程适用性。

Abstract: Non-intrusive reduced-order modeling is essential for systems simulated with black- or gray-box solvers, yet computing accurate data-driven models remains challenging when the dynamics exhibit large-amplitude transient energy growth. This is because state-of-the-art non-intrusive methods typically rely on orthogonal projection onto high-energy linear or nonlinear manifolds identified by minimizing static reconstruction losses, which truncates low-energy, yet dynamically important, states. Intrusive methods such as Balanced Truncation and its extensions address this issue by using adjoint information to define dynamics-aware oblique projections. Here, we show that balancing-based oblique projections for nonlinear systems can instead be computed non-intrusively from forward simulation data. Specifically, we develop a framework to compute the nonlinear empirical reachability Gramian and balance it against its observability counterpart, projected onto a suitable restriction subspace. This procedure provably recovers the oblique projection of Balanced Truncation when the dynamics are linear and the range of either Gramian is contained in the restriction subspace. Moreover, in the linear case and in the absence of adjoint information, we show that the minimax-optimal restriction subspace spans the dominant eigenspaces of the reachability Gramian, which informs an efficient balancing algorithm with cost comparable to a standard SVD. Once the oblique projection is computed, we use Operator Inference to learn the latent-space dynamics on its range. The method is demonstrated on several examples of increasing complexity, including the three-dimensional incompressible flow past a wing with $8\times 10^7$ degrees of freedom. In all examples, the Balanced Operator Inference models significantly outperform their POD-based counterparts, reducing forecasting error by up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [151] [From isolated singular wells to exact Bloch bands: the trigonometric Pöschl-Teller lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16072)
*Ulysses Camara da Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有周期逆平方奇点的精确可解晶格模型，揭示了能谱隙闭合的两种不同物理机制，并展示了常规二次载流子、类 Dirac 色散与可调无质量接触在同一奇异晶格中的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究具有周期逆平方奇点的可解模型中能隙闭合的物理机制，理解能带结构在对称与非对称极限下的行为差异，以及有限隙如何充当有效 Dirac 质量。

Method: 从广义三角 Pöschl-Teller 势出发，在奇点处两个局域解均平方可积的耦合范围内构造模型；采用保流、传播的自伴匹配条件连接相邻单胞，生成非平凡 Bloch 谱；分析具有两个不等价奇点子晶格的晶格，利用能带曲率定义有效电子与空穴质量，并通过精确 Bloch 本征函数研究概率密度。

Result: 谱包含两族交替的隙，带边复现相关有限奇异系统的标度不变谱；一簇隙在两子晶格等价时闭合，但这源于原胞周期减半导致的布里渊区折叠；另一簇隙在临界但真正非对称的组态下闭合，此时原胞周期保持不变，相邻能带在原布里渊区中心以真正的无质量 Dirac 锥相接；在任一临界极限附近，小有限隙充当有效 Dirac 质量，色散在带边附近保持二次型，越过窄区后过渡为有质量 Dirac 形式；等谱晶格可展现不同的单粒子概率密度。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个解析可控的一维框架，使常规二次载流子、有质量类 Dirac 带色散与可调无质量接触共存于同一奇异晶格中，为研究谱隙闭合机制与 Dirac 类物理提供了理想平台。

Abstract: An exactly solvable lattice with periodic inverse-square singularities exhibits two physically distinct mechanisms of spectral-gap closure. The model arises from the generalized trigonometric Pöschl-Teller potential in the coupling range where both local solutions at each singularity are square-integrable. A transmitting, current-preserving self-adjoint matching condition connects neighboring cells and generates a nontrivial Bloch spectrum. For a generic lattice with two inequivalent singular sublattices, the spectrum contains two alternating families of gaps, and the band edges reproduce the scale-invariant spectra of the associated finite singular system. One gap family closes when the two sublattices become equivalent. In this symmetric limit, however, the primitive period halves, so the apparent band crossing results from Brillouin-zone folding. A different family closes at a critical but genuinely asymmetric configuration. Because the primitive period is preserved, adjacent bands then meet at the center of the original Brillouin zone in a genuine massless Dirac-like cone. Near either critical limit, a small finite gap acts as an effective Dirac mass: the dispersion remains quadratic near a band edge but crosses over to a massive Dirac-like form beyond that narrow region. The band curvatures define effective electron and hole masses. Exact Bloch eigenfunctions further reveal that isospectral lattices can display different single-particle probability densities. This model therefore provides an analytically controlled one-dimensional setting in which conventional quadratic carriers, massive Dirac-like band dispersions, and a tunable massless contact coexist within the same singular lattice.

</details>


### [152] [The Fröhlich Polaron at large total momentum in d=3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16081)
*Tobias Schmidt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维Fröhlich极化子在固定耦合强度α>0与总动量P下的谱性质，证明谱底在有限的|P|处达到本质谱，从而在大总动量时纤维哈密顿量不存在基态，将已知的弱耦合结果推广到任意耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果仅在弱耦合情形下证明了Fröhlich极化子在足够大的总动量下不存在基态，本文旨在将该结论推广到任意固定耦合强度，揭示大动量下谱底与本质谱的关系。

Method: 作者研究三维Fröhlich极化子的纤维哈密顿量，通过将谱阈值附近的态描述为一个具有小剩余动量的全相互作用极化子加上一个携带几乎全部总动量的附加声子，从而分析谱底与本质谱的接触点。

Result: 证明在固定耦合强度α>0下，谱底在总动量|P|的某个有限值处到达本质谱，导致该纤维哈密顿量在足够大的总动量下没有基态。

Conclusion: 本文将弱耦合下Fröhlich极化子无基态的结论推广到任意耦合强度，表明大动量时谱底与本质谱相交，纤维哈密顿量失去基态，深化了对极化子谱结构随动量变化的理解。

Abstract: We consider the three-dimensional Fröhlich polaron at fixed coupling strength $α>0$ and total momentum $P \in \bbR^3$. We show that the bottom of the spectrum reaches the essential spectrum at a finite value of $|P|$, implying that the fiber Hamiltonian has no ground state at sufficiently large total momentum. This extends the known weak-coupling result to arbitrary coupling. Our approach describes states near the spectral threshold by a fully interacting polaron with small residual momentum and one additional phonon carrying almost all of the total momentum.

</details>


### [153] [Using the Renormalized Energy from Ginzburg-Landau Theory to Compute Solution Landscapes in the Reduced Landau-de Gennes Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16083)
*Yucen Han,Apala Majumdar*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维有界域上研究向列液晶的简约Landau-de Gennes（rLdG）模型，将Ginzburg-Landau理论中的重正化能量推广到带尖角的多边形，并利用其临界点构造求解器的初值，高效发现稳定解、鞍点及此前未报道的rLdG解支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对rLdG偏微分方程求解器进行计算代价高昂，且难以全面探索解空间；而现有的Ginzburg-Landau重正化能量理论仅适用于无尖角的光滑域，无法处理多边形（如正六边形、矩形）等具有尖角的实际几何区域。作者希望将重正化能量框架推广到带尖角的多边形，从而高效、系统地构造初值并揭示rLdG解的完整分支结构。

Method: 在有限维重正化能量框架下，将重正化能量推广至带锐角的多边形；该能量完全由三元组(a,c,d)刻画：d为内部缺陷的拓扑度，c为多边形顶点处的绕数贡献，a为内部缺陷位置，并由Dirichlet边界条件下的求和规则约束。利用重正化能量的临界点作为rLdG求解器的初始条件，在圆盘、正六边形和矩形上求解，从而恢复已知的能量极小解与非极小鞍点，并发现新的解支。

Result: 在圆盘、正六边形和矩形上成功恢复了已知的能量极小化及非极小化鞍点解，并发现了此前未报道的rLdG解支；相比直接求解偏微分方程，重正化能量方法计算效率显著更高。

Conclusion: 重正化能量方法为rLdG求解器提供了一种高效的系统性初值构造方式，能够恢复并扩展已知解的分支结构；但其局限在于会遗漏含有线缺陷及有限尺寸效应的解支。

Abstract: We study nematic liquid crystal phases confined to bounded, two-dimensional (2D) domains, in the reduced Landau-de Gennes (rLdG) theoretical framework. The rLdG framework is equivalent to the celebrated Ginzburg-Landau (GL) theoretical setting, under certain assumptions. In the asymptotic limit of large domains (or low temperatures), we recall the concept of the finite-dimensional renormalized energy in the GL theory [Bethuel et al., GL Vortices, Prog. Nonlinear Differ. Equ. Appl. 13, Birkhäuser, 1994.] and generalize it to polygons with sharp corners. The renormalized energy is fully defined by the triplet, $(\mathbf{a},\mathbf{c}, \mathbf{d}$), of topological degrees of the interior defects ($\mathbf{d}$) and the winding number contribution of polygon vertices ($\mathbf{c}$) and interior defect locations ($\mathbf{a}$), subject to sum rules dictated by the imposed Dirichlet boundary condition. We use the critical points of the renormalized energy to construct initial conditions for the rLdG solver, recover known energy-minimizing and non-energy minimizing saddle points of the rLdG energy on a disc, regular hexagon and rectangle along with the discovery of previously unreported rLdG solution branches. The renormalized energy approach is computationally substantially more efficient than a direct PDE-solver in the rLdG setting but misses solution branches with line defects and finite-size effects.

</details>


### [154] [Superintegrable systems in curved spaces via coalgebras and deformed Racah algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16154)
*Samuel Jimenez-Swain,Ian Marquette,Yao-Zhong Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在抽象 Poisson 余代数层面确定并构造了由部分 Casimir 生成的 Poisson 代数，分别对应未形变与非标准 q-形变的 sl(2) 余代数，并证明相关结果不依赖具体辛实现；对三、四位点情形分别得到一阶、二阶 Racah Poisson 代数（未形变）与有限二次 Poisson 代数（q-形变），并利用一个精确的两块恒等式将 Stäckel q-形变 Smorodinsky-Winternitz 模型推广到最大超可积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望从一个普适的、与具体辛实现无关的抽象代数框架出发，统一刻画由部分 Casimir 生成的（可积/超可积）Poisson 代数，并将 Stäckel q-形变 Smorodinsky-Winternitz 模型提升为最大超可积系统。

Method: 在 Poisson 余代数层次上，分别对未形变和非标准 q-形变的 sl(2) 余代数定义部分 Casimir 及相连部分 Casimir，构造其生成的 Poisson 代数并确定闭合系数的显式形式；对三、四位点分别求闭合结构；给出一个直接在局域 Poisson 代数中证明的精确两块恒等式，用于控制额外的 Stäckel 积分，并在三维、四维乃至任意 N 下组织为（嵌套的）二次 Poisson 块。

Result: 三、四位点情形：未形变 sl(2) 分别给出秩一、秩二 Racah Poisson 代数；非标准形变给出由相连部分 Casimir 生成的有限二次 Poisson 代数。三点与四点的最终闭合系数均不显式依赖形变参数。Stäckel q-形变 Smorodinsky-Winternitz 模型被显式完成到最大超可积：三维情形生成一个带自身 Casimir 关系的二次 Poisson 块；四维情形其与全体左/右余代数族的耦合组织成三个嵌套二次块；同一恒等式把该嵌套耦合推广到任意 N。

Conclusion: 所构造的 Poisson 代数结构在余代数层次上成立，因而适用于一切保持定义括号与余积的实现；辛实现可给出非恒曲率空间中的 Hamilton 量。精确两块恒等式是完成 Stäckel q-形变 SW 模型最大超可积的关键，并为任意位点数 N 的推广提供了统一机制。

Abstract: We determine the Poisson algebras generated by partial Casimirs of undeformed and non-standard $q$-deformed $\mathfrak{sl}(2)$ coalgebras, respectively. The derivation is performed on the abstract algebraic level and does not depend on any particular symplectic realization. For the 3- and 4-site cases the constructions associated with the undeformed $\mathfrak{sl}(2)$ produce the rank-one and rank-two Racah Poisson algebras, respectively, while those with the non-standard deformed $\mathfrak{sl}$ yield finite quadratic Poisson algebras generated by connected partial Casimirs. At both the three and four sites, the final closure coefficients do not explicitly depend on the deformation parameter. The results hold at the Poisson coalgebra level and hence apply to any realizations which preserve the defining brackets and coproducts. Canonical symplectic realizations give rise to Hamiltonians in spaces of non-constant curvature. For the Stäckel $q$-deformed Smorodinsky-Winternitz model we show explicitly how the universal coalgebra sector is completed to maximal superintegrability. An exact two-block identity, proved directly in the localized Poisson algebra without appealing to a symplectic realization, controls the additional Stäckel integrals. In three dimensions, it generates a quadratic Poisson block with its own Casimir relation; in four dimensions, its coupling to the full left/right coalgebra family is organized into three nested quadratic blocks, and the same identity extends this nested coupling to arbitrary $N$. Explicit higher-site partial Casimirs and factorized bracket generators are collected in the appendices.

</details>


### [155] [Almost invariant subspaces in the topologically non-trivial setting: a pedestrian approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16393)
*Horia Cornean,Gabriele Peluso,Mikkel Hviid Thorn*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了一种自洽的算法化构造，用于为 Hofstadter 类格点哈密顿量 $H_b$ 构造近似不变子空间：从一个与 $H_{b_0}$ 交换的“局部有隙”投影 $P_0$ 出发，迭代地得到与 $H_{b_0+\varepsilon}$ 交换（误差为 $\varepsilon^\infty$ 阶）的投影 $P_\varepsilon$，并保证陈数不变、积分态密度满足 Středa 公式，且局部 Parseval 框架可转移到 $P_\varepsilon$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁场微扰下 Hofstadter 类哈密顿量的谱子空间（特别是拓扑非平凡的能带投影）在 $\varepsilon$ 阶扰动下的稳定性与可构造性，希望在离散模型中给出相对初等、无需高级赝微分算子的自洽方法，从而避免连续模型中的繁重技术负担。

Method: 作者采用离散格点模型 $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z}^2,\mathbb{C}^n)$ 上的 Hofstadter 类哈密顿量 $H_b$，对有理 $b_0/(2\pi)$，从与 $H_{b_0}$ 交换且“局部有隙”的投影 $P_0$ 出发，设计一种迭代算法逐步构造与 $H_{b_0+\varepsilon}$ 近似交换的投影 $P_\varepsilon$，交换误差达到 $\varepsilon^\infty$ 阶。文中还证明在 $P_\varepsilon$ 存在期间其陈数等于 $P_0$ 的陈数，积分态密度随 $\varepsilon$ 线性变化，并将 $P_0$ 的空间局域 Parseval 框架映射为 $P_\varepsilon$ 的局域 Parseval 框架。

Result: 成功构造了与微扰后哈密顿量近似交换（误差 $\varepsilon^\infty$ 阶）的投影 $P_\varepsilon$，并证明：（1）$P_\varepsilon$ 存在时其陈数与 $P_0$ 相同；（2）积分态密度随 $\varepsilon$ 线性变化，满足 Středa 公式；（3）$P_0$ 的空间局域 Parseval 框架可映射为 $P_\varepsilon$ 的局域框架。整个构造在离散模型下自洽完成，无需高级赝微分演算。

Conclusion: 作者相信该离散模型下的大部分结果可以推广到（无界）磁（赝）微分算子，但技术负担将重得多，留作未来工作。离散框架的选用有效降低了技术难度，同时保持了拓扑不变量（陈数）与态密度等核心物理量的控制。

Abstract: We present a self-contained, algorithmic construction of almost invariant subspaces [Panati et al. (2003), De Nittis and Panati (2010), Freund and Teufel (2016)] for Hofstadter-like Hamiltonians $H_b $ acting on $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z}^2,\mathbb{C}^n)$. More precisely, for rational values of $b_0/(2π)$ we describe an iterative procedure that takes a ``locally gapped" projection $P_{0}$ (possibly topologically non-trivial) which commutes with $H_{b_0}$, into a projection $P_{\varepsilon}$ that commutes with $H_{b_0+\varepsilon}$ up to an error of order $\varepsilon^\infty$.
  Our construction insures that the Chern number of $P_{\varepsilon}$ equals the Chern number of $P_0$ as long as $P_\varepsilon$ exists. Moreover, its integrated density of states varies linearly with $\varepsilon$ (the ``Středa formula"). We also show that any spatially localized Parseval frame of $P_{0}$ can be mapped into to a localized Parseval frame of $P_{\varepsilon}$.
  We choose to work with a discrete model in order to keep the technicalities to a minimum level, with no need for advanced pseudo-differential calculus. Nevertheless, we are confident that most of our results can be extended to (unbounded) magnetic (pseudo)differential operators, but the technical burden would be much heavier. This is left for future work.

</details>


### [156] [Geometric bulk conductances in even dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16208)
*Alexis Drouot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出Martin-Stoiber近期结果的一个初等证明：偶数维空间中，几何Kubo公式等于一个仅依赖开集相对位置的拓扑整数与体电导的乘积，证明仅用Stokes定理与Poincaré引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Martin-Stoiber将Drouot-Zhu二维结果推广到所有偶数维的结论提供一个更初等、更简洁的证明，避免复杂技术工具。

Method: 将几何体电导写成投影子P与Z^{2n}上一个cochain的配对（源自Kitaev的想法），并通过对该cochain进行形变而非对开集族(U_1,...,U_{2n})形变来证明；整个证明只使用Stokes定理和Poincaré引理。

Result: 证明了几何Kubo公式 Tr(P[P,1_{U_1}]∧⋯∧[P,1_{U_{2n}}]) 等于拓扑整数X_U（仅依赖U_1,...,U_{2n}的相对位置）与P的体电导的乘积。

Conclusion: 通过形变cochain的方式给出了偶数维几何Kubo公式的初等证明，该证明由Claude Opus 5.5完成，将结果归约为拓扑整数与体电导的乘积。

Abstract: Let $P$ be a short-range projector on $\ell^2(\mathbb{Z}^{2n},\mathbb{C}^m)$ and $U=(U_1,\dots,U_{2n})$ be a transverse family of open subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{2n}$. We give an elementary proof of a recent result of Martinian-Stoiber, which extends a two-dimensional result of Drouot-Zhu to all even dimensions. Specifically, using only Stokes' theorem and the Poincaré lemma, we show that the geometric Kubo formula $\operatorname{Tr}\big(P[P,\mathbf{1}_{U_1}]\wedge\cdots\wedge[P,\mathbf{1}_{U_{2n}}]\big)$ is the product of a topological integer $\mathcal{X}_U$, which depends only on the relative positions of $U_1, \dots, U_{2n}$, and of the bulk conductance of $P$. Our proof writes the geometric bulk conductance as the pairing of $P$ with a cochain on $\mathbb{Z}^{2n}$ - an idea originally due to Kitaev - and deforms the cochain rather than the sets $(U_1, \dots, U_{2n})$. The proof presented in this paper is due to Claude Opus 5.5.

</details>


### [157] [Nested polynomial algebras and the exact spectrum of a three-dimensional two-spin oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16317)
*Ian Marquette,O. Ogulcan Tuncer,I. Yurdusen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为带两个自旋1/2自由度的三维谐振子构造了对称代数的表示，并精确求解其能谱。作者确定了哈密顿量及其一阶标量和赝标量积分在完备谱基上的作用，揭示了角动量固定时的二次代数结构与径向su(1,1)正离散级数表示，从而给出完整能量壳层多重度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前分类工作已得到该三维谐振子（含两个自旋1/2自由度）的哈密顿量及其一阶标量和赝标量积分，但这些积分如何在完备谱基上作用、系统的精确能谱如何确定尚待解决。作者希望借助对称代数表示方法，彻底确定系统的谱结构、角动量-自旋多重度以及径向能量量子化。

Method: 作者在完备谱基上确定哈密顿量及一阶积分的作用。在固定总角动量下，二次代数作用于三个三重态轨道道，形成一个三维变形谐振子表示；同时一个重叠的二态交换代数连接中间三重态与单态。这些有限模给出角-自旋多重度；径向部分由共同径向哈密顿量的su(1,1)正离散级数表示生成能量序列。文中给出有限矩阵、Casimir算符与结构函数、归一化本征函数、径向阶梯作用、j=0例外扇区及粒子交换效应。

Result: 得到系统完整的精确能谱：有限代数模确定了角-自旋多重度，su(1,1)正离散级数表示对径向能量进行量子化，两者结合给出完整能量壳层多重度。构造了归一化本征函数、有限矩阵、Casimir与结构函数，并处理了例外j=0扇区和粒子交换对称性。

Conclusion: 通过对称代数表示，完整解决了带两个自旋1/2自由度三维谐振子的谱问题：有限二次代数与交换代数描写角-自旋结构，su(1,1)表示描写径向能量，从而严格确定整个谱与多重度。

Abstract: We construct representations of the symmetry algebra and derive the exact spectrum of a three-dimensional oscillator with two spin-$\tfrac12$ degrees of freedom. The Hamiltonian and its first-order scalar and pseudo-scalar integrals were obtained in earlier classifications. Here we determine their action on the complete spectral basis. At fixed total angular momentum, a quadratic algebra acts on the three triplet orbital channels and admits a three-dimensional deformed-oscillator representation, while an overlapping two-state exchange algebra joins the middle triplet to the singlet. These finite modules determine angular--spin multiplicities but do not quantize the radial energy. A separate positive-discrete-series $su(1,1)$ representation of the common radial Hamiltonian generates the energy sequence. We give the finite matrices, Casimir and structure functions, normalized eigenfunctions, radial ladder actions, the exceptional $j=0$ sector, complete energy-shell multiplicities, and the effect of particle interchange.

</details>


### [158] [Willis-type thermal homogenization for particulate composites under harmonic heat transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17245)
*Chunlin Wu,Ning Ding,Huiming Yin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种二维颗粒复合材料在谐波传热下的Willis型热均质化框架，利用基于对偶互易的包含体边界元法引入两个本征场模拟热导率与热容失配，直接由本征场与广义宏观响应关系构造完整本构算子，无需引入残余场，并通过一维解析解与扩展傅里叶级数均质化方案验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热均质化方法通常需通过残余场生成若干独立宏观解来提取有效核，过程繁琐且缺乏统一的宏观本构表征；针对二维颗粒复合材料在谐波（非稳态）传热下的动态/非局域等效行为，需要一种无需残余场、可直接构造完整Willis型本构算子的通用计算方法。

Method: 采用基于对偶互易（dual-reciprocity）的包含体边界元法求解局部温度场；引入两个本征场分别模拟导热系数与热容的失配；直接利用本征场与广义宏观响应（温度梯度和热流相关量）之间的关系构造完整本构算子。有效性通过与一维解析解和扩展傅里叶级数均质化方案进行对比验证，并利用傅里叶公式建立耦合张量间的反向波数互易关系及反射对称性奇偶关系，从而解释横向耦合分量消失条件及反射下非对角电导分量符号反转；数值上进一步分析Bloch条件下的去相位平均对提取物理一致等效参数的必要性，揭示其非局域的频率/波数依赖行为。

Result: 建立了无需残余场的唯一有效本构关系；有效本构关系与一维解析解和扩展傅里叶级数均质化方案吻合良好；基于傅里叶公式确立了两个耦合张量的反向波数互易关系与反射对称性奇偶关系，解释了横向耦合分量的消隐条件和反射下非对角电导分量的符号反转；数值表明需采用去相位平均才能在Bloch条件下提取物理一致的等效热物性，且等效参数呈现非局域的频率/波数依赖特征。

Conclusion: 所提框架为颗粒复合材料的Willis型热均质化提供了通用计算方法，可扩展至三维及多物理场问题；其不依赖残余场直接构造完整本构算子的思路简化了谐波传热下动态均质化的实现，并揭示了等效热属性的非局域频率/波数依赖性。

Abstract: This paper develops a Willis-type thermal homogenization framework for two-dimensional particulate composites under harmonic heat transfer and establishes a unique effective constitutive relation without introducing residual fields. The local thermal fields are obtained using the dual-reciprocity inclusion-based boundary element method, in which two eigen-fields are introduced to simulate the mismatches in thermal conductivity and heat capacity. Instead of retrieving effective kernels from several independent macroscopic solutions generated by residual fields, the complete constitutive operator is constructed upon relations between eigen-fields and generalized macroscopic responses. The effective constitutive relations are validated against a one-dimensional analytical solution and an extended Fourier-series homogenization scheme. The Fourier formulae further establish the opposite-wavenumber reciprocity relations between two coupling tensors and reflection-symmetry parity relations, which explain when transverse coupling components vanish and the sign reversal of off-diagonal conductivity components under reflection. Numerical results further demonstrate that the de-phased average is required to extract physically consistent effective thermal properties under the Bloch conditions, and reveal their nonlocal frequency/wavenumber-dependent behavior. The framework provides a general computational method for Willis-type homogenization of particulate composites and is extendable to three-dimensional, multiphysical problems.

</details>


### [159] [Norm properties of quantum uniformity measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17454)
*Berke Ünal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文完全解决了 Bu、Gu 与 Jaffe 提出的一个开放问题：证明 k=4 时量子均匀性度量构成范数，而 k≥5 时三角不等式不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应 Bu、Gu 与 Jaffe 关于量子均匀性度量在何种参数 k 下成为范数的开放问题，厘清量子均匀性度量的范数性质。

Method: k=4 情形：引入量子均匀性配对 ⟨·,·⟩_{Q^4} 并利用其 Cauchy–Schwarz 不等式，结合适当有限维 Bochner L^8 空间中的三角不等式予以证明。k≥5 情形：构造有限域上的线性方程组，并借助赋范线性系统的结构理论推出矛盾。

Result: 证明了 k=4 时量子均匀性度量是范数；对任意 k≥5，三角不等式失效。

Conclusion: 该结果对 Bu、Gu 与 Jaffe 的开放问题给出完整解答：量子均匀性度量恰在 k=4 时为范数，k≥5 时不再满足三角不等式。

Abstract: We prove that for $k=4$ the quantum uniformity measures are norms, whereas for every $k\geq5$ the triangle inequality fails. This gives a complete answer to an open question of Bu, Gu, and Jaffe. The $k=4$ case is proved using the Cauchy--Schwarz inequality for the quantum uniformity pairing $\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{Q^4}$ introduced here, together with the triangle inequality in a suitable finite-dimensional Bochner $L^8$ space. For $k\geq5$, we construct systems of linear equations over a finite field and derive a contradiction from the structure theory of norming linear systems.

</details>


### [160] [$L^2$ convergence rates for singular stochastic wave dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17815)
*Nikolay Barashkov,Giacomo Di Gesù,Petri Laarne*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为奇异随机阻尼波动方程（源自随机量子化）建立了定量hypocoercivity理论，提出维度无关判据，并用奇异随机薛定谔算子最低特征值估计验证了二维Φ^4、sine-Gordon及三维Φ^4模型的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机量子化中，随机阻尼波动方程是构造与采样欧氏量子场论测度的重要动力学工具。其收敛到平衡态的速度（hypocoercivity）需要定量刻画，但此前的有限维论证依赖一致半凸性假设，对Φ^4等奇异交互模型不再适用，因此需要在奇异情形下建立可用、且能做连续极限的收敛判据。

Method: 基于Cao、Lu、Wang（Arch. Rat. Mech. Anal. 2023）的有限维论证，作者推导出一个维度无关的判据：以对数Sobolev不等式加上Hessian谱底部的指数型下尾界替代一致半凸性。随后通过对关联的奇异随机Schrödinger算子最低特征值的估计，在Φ^4_2、sine-Gordon及固定体积下的Φ^4_3模型上验证该判据，并结合伴随论文中的连续极限得到相应结论。

Result: 结合连续极限，得到Φ^4_2动力学及β^2<2π的sine-Gordon的指数L^2收敛到平衡态。对Φ^4_2，在弱耦合、小质量区域通过优化阻尼得到至少m阶的动力学松弛率，相比过阻尼动力学的m^2阶松弛率有真正的加速。格点估计对sine-Gordon在全β^2<4π范围内、对固定体积的Φ^4_3均关于离散化一致，其中包含超出当前连续动力学所能构造的区域。

Conclusion: 作者成功建立了奇异随机阻尼波动方程的定量hypocoercivity，并用对数Sobolev不等式加谱下尾界的方法绕开了一致半凸性限制，在多个奇异模型中验证了判据，还揭示了欠阻尼动力学相对过阻尼在Φ^4_2中的加速效应，为奇异随机量子化动力学的收敛理论提供了新工具。

Abstract: We establish quantitative hypocoercivity for singular stochastic damped wave equations arising in stochastic quantization. Building on the finite-dimensional argument of Cao, Lu, and Wang [Arch. Rat. Mech. Anal. 2023], we derive a dimension-free criterion that replaces uniform semiconvexity by a log-Sobolev inequality together with exponential lower-tail bounds for the bottom of the Hessian spectrum. We verify this criterion for the two-dimensional $Φ^4_2$ and sine-Gordon models and for the three-dimensional $Φ^4_3$ model, through estimates on the lowest eigenvalue of associated singular random Schrödinger operators. Combined with the continuum limit established in a companion paper, this yields exponential $L^2$ convergence to equilibrium for the $Φ^4_2$ dynamics and for sine-Gordon with $β^2<2π$. For $Φ^4_2$, we verify that in a weak-coupling, small-mass regime, optimizing the damping yields a kinetic relaxation rate of at least order $m$. This yields a genuine acceleration over the order-$m^2$ relaxation rate of the overdamped dynamics. The lattice estimates are uniform in the discretization for sine-Gordon throughout $β^2<4π$, and for $Φ^4_3$ at fixed volume, including regimes beyond those for which the continuum dynamics is vpresently constructed.

</details>


### [161] [How many switches does two-Hamiltonian universality require?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17826)
*Matas Garbaravičius,Michael M. Wolf*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究用两段哈密顿量交替演化合成任意酉矩阵所需的切换次数，证明其期望随维数 d 线性增长，并在谱足够通用时给出依赖于基变换矩阵的构造性上界（如复 Hadamard 矩阵情形下 9d 次切换即可覆盖整个酉群）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子控制中的一个核心问题是：给定一对哈密顿量，通过交替演化能否生成整个酉群 U(d)，以及最少需要多少次切换。已知最少切换数至少为 d，且一般情况下无界，这带来控制成本的高不确定性，因此需要刻画其在"典型/通用"情形下的期望规模并给出可构造的保证。

Method: 作者构造任意酉矩阵作为在两个给定本征基下交替对角的酉矩阵之积的精确表示（短精确表示），并利用该表示推导出最小切换数的上界。该上界是两哈密顿量本征基之间基变换矩阵的函数，并假定两个谱足够通用；随后将所有界扩展到非通用谱和有限演化时间的情形。

Result: 证明了最小切换数的期望随维数 d 线性增长。若两个谱足够通用，则得到基于基变换矩阵的构造性上界；特别地，当基变换矩阵含有一个各元素模长均相等的列（如复 Hadamard 矩阵）时，仅需 9d 次切换即可覆盖 U(d) 中任意酉矩阵。所有上界均可推广到非通用谱及有界演化时间。

Conclusion: 尽管最坏情况下最小切换数无界，但在通用谱假设下其期望仅线性依赖于维数 d，且通过短精确表示能给出显式、可构造的切换次数上界，显著降低了对控制成本不确定性的顾虑，并将结果推广至更一般的谱与时间约束情形。

Abstract: Any pair of sufficiently generic Hamiltonians on $\mathbb{C}^d$ generates the full unitary group, i.e., any $W\in U(d)$ can be synthesized to arbitrary precision by alternating between the two evolutions for suitably chosen durations. For any given pair of Hamiltonians, the minimal number of switches required to cover the entire group is at least $d$ and unbounded in general. Despite this, we show that it scales linearly in $d$ in expectation. Assuming that the two spectra are sufficiently generic, we derive constructive upper bounds on the minimal switching number as a function of the change-of-basis matrix between the two eigenbases. In particular, if this matrix has a column whose entries all have equal moduli, as for a complex Hadamard matrix, then $9d$ switches suffice to reach every unitary. To obtain these results, we construct a short exact representation of any unitary as a product of unitaries that are alternately diagonal in two given bases. All bounds are extended to non-generic spectra and bounded evolution times.

</details>


### [162] [Dynamics of low energy wave packets in the one-dimensional quantum Heisenberg ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17883)
*Mateusz Kwaśnicki,Marcin Napiórkowski,Sören Petrat,Michał Pietruszka*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子自旋波理论框架下,首次从严格数学角度研究了单链(一维)量子铁磁海森堡模型中自旋波态的动力学演化,证明了双磁振子波包在适当时间尺度下可由自由磁振子演化良好近似,并论证了该时间尺度的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波理论在分析量子铁磁海森堡模型低能性质时处于核心地位,但此前缺乏严格的数学论证。作者旨在为量子动力学背景下自旋波近似的合理性提供严格证明的第一步,即证明自旋波态的时间演化可被自由磁振子演化很好近似。

Method: 作者研究一维自旋-1/2量子铁磁海森堡模型,针对由两个磁振子组成的自旋波包,利用严格数学分析(量子动力学)证明其时间演化与具有适当自由准粒子(磁振子)能量的自由演化之间的近似关系,并给出在何种时间尺度内近似成立。

Result: 作者严格证明了双磁振子自旋波包的演化在特定时间尺度内可被其自由演化(带正确磁振子能量)良好近似,并论证了该结果关于时间尺度是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对一维量子动力学背景下自旋波近似进行严格论证的第一步,针对双磁振子波包给出了严格演化近似结果,且时间尺度最优,为后续更一般情形的严格处理奠定基础。

Abstract: Spin wave theory plays a central role in the analysis of low-energy properties of the quantum ferromagnetic Heisenberg model. In this work, we present a first step towards a rigorous justification of this approximation in the context of quantum dynamics in one dimension. According to spin wave theory, the evolution of a spin wave state should be well approximated by its free evolution with the appropriate energy of free quasiparticles called magnons. Our main result establishes this idea rigorously for a class of spin wave packets. We focus on spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ systems, and consider wave packets composed of two magnons. We argue our result is optimal with respect to the timescales.

</details>


### [163] [Dynamical BEC formation and vanishing entropy for trapped bosons in non-relativistic QED via nonlinear resonance cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17923)
*Thomas Chen,Ali Mezher*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明，与量子化辐射场耦合的玻色子在动力学演化中会形成完整玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚：其宏观单粒子密度矩阵通过非线性共振级联耗散掉所有激发态，最终以迹范数收敛到纯的基态凝聚体，且 von Neumann 熵渐近趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明被捕获玻色子系统在耦合量子化辐射场后，即使初态为真正的混合态（宏观态 κ₀ 熵为正），也能通过辐射耗散实现渐近纯化，并回答宏观单粒子态为何可以不依赖全局幺正性而收敛到纯凝聚体的问题。

Method: 作者通过分析宏观单粒子密度矩阵 κ_T（对 de Finetti 测度 μ₀ 下的平均场粒子-场轨道取平均）在非线性共振级联中的演化，刻画辐射弛豫如何不可逆地耗尽所有激发模式。在初态基态分量非零且向下 Fermi-Golden-Rule 速率严格为正的假设下，证明迹范数强收敛。技术路线为：从有限 N 的 BBGKY 层级推导玻色子-光子耦合系统的 Hartree 层级，先取 N→∞ 的均场极限，再取弱耦合极限，最后取 T→∞（三个极限依次进行）。

Result: 证明了 lim_{T→∞} ‖ κ_T − |χ₀⟩⟨χ₀| ‖_{𝔖₁(𝔥)} = 0，即宏观单粒子密度矩阵强收敛到基态纯态；同时 von Neumann 熵渐近消失，lim_{T→∞} S[κ_T] = 0（不要求熵随 T 单调）。因此任何初态混合、熵正的态（0 < S[κ₀] < ∞）都被纯化到基态 |χ₀⟩⟨χ₀|。

Conclusion: 尽管全局粒子-场演化保持幺正，能量与关联持续辐射进入色散场，宏观单粒子态仍收敛到纯态，完成动力学凝聚形成与渐近纯化。这揭示了开放量子系统中耗散驱动的宏观态纯化机制。

Abstract: The dynamical formation of a complete Bose-Einstein condensate and the asymptotic purification of trapped bosons coupled to a quantized radiation field are rigorously established. By tracking the macroscopic one-particle density matrix $κ_T$ through a nonlinear resonance cascade, it is demonstrated that radiative relaxation irreversibly depletes all excited trap modes, even when the initial macroscopic state $κ_0$ is genuinely mixed. Here $κ_T$ is an average, with respect to a de Finetti measure $μ_0$, over mean-field particle-field orbits, so that the mixedness of $κ_0$ reflects a statistical ensemble of orbits. Under the assumptions of a non-vanishing initial ground state component and strictly positive downward Fermi-Golden-Rule rates, strong trace-norm convergence to a pure condensate is proven, $$
  \lim_{T\to\infty} \| κ_T-|χ_0\rangle\langleχ_0| \|_{\mathfrak{S}_1(\mathfrak{h})} = 0 \, . $$ Furthermore, the von Neumann entropy of the macroscopic one-particle density matrix is shown to vanish asymptotically, $$
  \lim_{T\to\infty}S[κ_T]=0 \, , $$ without any claim of monotonicity in $T$. Thus, any initially mixed, positive-entropy state ($0<S[κ_0]<\infty$) is purified to the ground state $|χ_0\rangle\langleχ_0|$. For the proof, we derive the Hartree hierarchy of the coupled system of bosons and photons from the finite-$N$ BBGKY hierarchy of the microscopic quantum many-body system, establishing trace-norm convergence in the mean-field limit as $N\to\infty$. The limits are taken successively; first $N\to\infty$, then the weak-coupling limit, and finally $T\to\infty$. Although the global particle-field evolution remains unitary, the macroscopic one-particle state converges to a pure state as energy and correlations are continuously radiated into the dispersive field.

</details>


### [164] [Spectral Theory of an Exactly Solvable Quantum-Optical Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18421)
*Sam Johar,Joseph Kraisler*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维单激发模型：标量光子场与二次密度分布的两能级原子连续谱耦合。所得矩阵哈密顿量具有无界的空间变化耦合且非半有界，作者证明了其本质自伴性并显式确定了谱结构：连续谱为 (-∞, Ω]，Ω 以上为严格递增的简单本征值序列，其渐近行为由 Airy 函数及其导数的交错零点刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光与物质相互作用中具有空间非均匀、无界耦合的模型，此类矩阵哈密顿量非半有界，超出标准自伴算子理论框架，需要建立新的数学方法来严格刻画其谱性质。

Method: 采用非线性谱约化（nonlinear spectral reduction）方法，将原问题转化为两个等效振子问题：在 Ω 上方得到束缚的（confining）无质量相对论振子，在 Ω 下方得到倒置（inverted）振子；进而利用 Airy 函数及其导数的零点交错性质刻画本征值。

Result: 证明了该非半有界矩阵哈密顿量本质自伴；连续谱为 (-∞, Ω]；Ω 以上谱由严格递增的简单本征值序列构成，且渐近展开为 ω_n = (3πg n/4)^{1/2} + Ω/4 + O(n^{-1/2})。

Conclusion: 该模型展示了无界耦合下光-物质系统的自伴性与可精确求解的谱结构，非线性谱约化方法为处理非半有界、耦合无界的量子系统提供了有效工具，本征值的 Airy 零点刻画与渐近公式具有明确的解析意义。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional single-excitation model of a scalar photon field coupled to a continuum of two-level atoms with quadratic density. The resulting matrix Hamiltonian has an unbounded spatially varying coupling and is not semibounded. We prove that it is essentially self-adjoint and determine its spectrum explicitly. The continuous spectrum is the half-line $(-\infty,Ω]$, where $Ω$ is the atomic resonance frequency, while the spectrum above $Ω$ consists of a strictly increasing sequence of simple eigenvalues. These eigenvalues are characterized by the interlaced zeros of the Airy function and its derivative and satisfy \begin{align*}
  ω_n = \left(\frac{3πg n}{4}\right)^{1/2} + \fracΩ{4} + O(n^{-1/2}). \end{align*} The analysis is based on a nonlinear spectral reduction that yields a confining massless relativistic oscillator above $Ω$ and an inverted oscillator below $Ω$.

</details>


### [165] [Haagerup-Izumi modular data from hyperbolic words: S-fold TQFTs, central charge, torsion and the Bethe vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18512)
*David E Evans*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 Haagerup–Izumi 范畴的二重（doubles）的模数据，将其刻画为两个有限度量群的嫁接；作者利用双曲单穿孔环面单值词，给出了 Bethe 真空、hand-gluing/fibering 算符取值及水平矩阵的闭式表达，并据此系统地重现并修正了 Evans–Gannon 的 Q-system 表与 Gannon–Schopieray–Yadav 的模数据列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 Haagerup–Izumi 范畴二重模数据的理解主要基于 Evans–Gannon 的猜想与 Gannon–Schopieray–Yadav 对未扭转情形的证明，而 Gang–Kim 在秩 0 S-折叠理论的拓扑扭转中通过阿贝尔化规范理论的 Bethe 真空发现了该形状的非幺正模数据。作者希望严格理解 hand-gluing 与 fibering 算符的意义，并构造相应的 Bethe 真空，从而为模数据提供统一的几何/规范理论解释。

Method: 对每个双曲单穿孔环面单值 φ（L 与 R 中的一个词），取两个群 A_∓ = coker(φ∓1)，并配以 φ 与 −φ 的映射环面的挠链接形式（torsion linking forms）。对所有迹为 m²+2 的词，这些形式重现 Q-system 表与模数据列表。Bethe 数据由词直接闭式给出：水平矩阵、正真空、有效中心荷 c_eff=#L，以及单位类处的 hand-gluing 值 2^{#L} λ^{±1} |A_-||A_+|（λ 为伸缩因子）。φ∓1 的每个核向量，模 |A_∓| 处若显式三角公式非零，则给出 Bethe 方程的闭式解；并通过半指标字典确定中心荷同余 c≡#L−6#R (mod 24)。

Result: 对迹为 m²+2 的所有词，所得挠链接形式完整重现 Evans–Gannon 的 Q-system 表（并修正一处印刷错误），且与 Gannon–Schopieray–Yadav 列表中的不同模数据一一对应。给出了水平矩阵、真空、c_eff、hand-gluing 值及 Bethe 方程解的全闭式公式，并推导出中心荷的同余条件 c≡#L−6#R (mod 24)。

Conclusion: Haagerup–Izumi 范畴二重的模数据可完全由双曲单穿孔环面单值词所定义的两个有限度量群嫁接给出；该词同时通过闭式公式决定 Bethe 数据、hand-gluing/fibering 算符的取值与水平矩阵，从而将拓扑扭转的 S-折叠理论、有限度量群与 Evans–Gannon 分类结果统一起来。

Abstract: The modular data of the doubles of the Haagerup--Izumi categories is a grafting of two finite metric groups (conjectured by Evans--Gannon, proved for untwisted categories by Gannon--Schopieray--Yadav). Gang and Kim found non-unitary modular data of this shape in the topological twist of rank-$0$ $S$-fold theories, from the Bethe vacua of an abelianised gauge theory. The aim is to understand rigorously the significance of the handle-gluing and fibering operators, and to construct the Bethe vacua.
  For every hyperbolic once-punctured-torus monodromy $\varphi$, a word in $L$ and $R$, the two groups are $A_\mp=\coker(\varphi\mp1)$, with the torsion linking forms of the mapping tori of $\varphi$ and $-\varphi$. Over all words of trace $m^2+2$ these forms reproduce the Evans--Gannon table of Q-systems, one printed entry being corrected, and the distinct modular data in the list of Gannon--Schopieray--Yadav. The Bethe data are given in closed form by the word: the level matrix, the positive vacuum, $c_{\eff}=\#L$, and the handle-gluing values $2^{\#L}λ^{\pm1}|A_-||A_+|$ at the identity classes, $λ$ the stretch factor. Each kernel vector of $\varphi\mp1$ modulo $|A_\mp|$ at which an explicit trigonometric formula does not vanish gives a solution of the Bethe equations in closed form. Under the half-index dictionary, with the unit in the class of $σV^*$, $c\equiv\#L-6\#R\pmod{24}$.

</details>


### [166] [Inverse eigenvalue problem for discrete three-diagonal Schroedinger operator and the continuum limit in Tikhonov regularization approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18734)
*Vladimir M. Chabanov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Sturm-Liouville算子的离散近似及其向连续坐标极限的过渡，自洽地推导了逆特征值问题的主要公式；采用Tikhonov概念将离散近似视为不适定算子问题的正则化，并证明连续极限给出的是势函数的某种组合而非势矩阵本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Sturm-Liouville算子逆特征值问题提供自洽的公式推导，克服三对角矩阵哈密顿量直接取连续极限不可能实现的困难。

Method: 对Sturm-Liouville算子作离散近似，将其视作不适定算子问题的Tikhonov正则化，再取三对角矩阵哈密顿量向连续坐标的极限；此时极限的对象是矩阵元的某种特定组合，该组合恰好给出连续情形下的势。

Result: 得到逆特征值问题的主要公式，并证明连续极限所恢复的量正是连续情形下的势函数。

Conclusion: 通过Tikhonov正则化框架下的离散近似与极限过渡，可以自洽地导出Sturm-Liouville算子逆特征值问题的核心公式，实现了从离散谱数据到连续势的恢复。

Abstract: In the present article the self-contained derivation of main formulas of the inverse eigenvalue problem for the Sturm-Liouville operator is given on the base of its discrete approximation and the subsequent transition to the limit of the continuum coordinate. In doing so, we adhere to Tikhonov concept, where the discrete approximation is performed as a regularization of the ill-posed operator problem. While the continuum limit for the three-diagonal matrix Hamiltonian is impossible as such, following the Tikhonov procedure leads not to a limit for the potential matrix itself, but rather for a certain combination of the matrix elements, and this limit is precisely the desired potential in the continuum case.

</details>


### [167] [Finite temperature Fredholm determinants for dynamical correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18750)
*Oleksandr Gamayun*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限温度动力学 Fredholm 行列式在长时间、大距离下的渐近行为，通过有效形状因子方法将其表为有效相移的泛函，并确定二次稳定点两侧的两个分支及其过渡剖面（用抛物柱面函数表达），结果与独立 Riemann–Hilbert 分析一致，且足以恢复硬棒动力学结构因子的广义流体力学 Euler 尺度预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度量子/经典可积系统的动力学关联函数常归结为 Fredholm 行列式，其长时间、大距离渐近在稳定点附近往往失效于常规鞍点法；作者需要一种能刻画稳定点过渡区域的方法，并检验其是否能重现已有的唯象理论（如广义流体力学）结果。

Method: 采用有效形状因子（effective form factors）方法，将 Fredholm 行列式的渐近表示为有效相移的泛函；在二次稳定点两侧分别确定两个分支，并用抛物柱面函数构造连接两个分支的过渡剖面；通过与高温高斯 β 系综的关联固定有限归一化，并辅以独立的 Riemann–Hilbert 分析加以验证。

Result: 得到了有限温度动力学 Fredholm 行列式在长时间、大距离下的完整渐近公式，明确给出稳定点两侧分支及过渡剖面；固定后的有限归一化与 Riemann–Hilbert 分析结果一致；并利用主导指数渐近成功恢复硬棒动力学结构因子在 Euler 尺度下广义流体力学（GHD）的预言。

Conclusion: 有效形状因子方法能够系统地给出跨越二次稳定点的有限温度动力学 Fredholm 行列式渐近，过渡剖面由抛物柱面函数描述，且与独立 Riemann–Hilbert 分析吻合；该方法在 Euler 尺度上可复现广义流体力学结果，展示了对非线性可积模型动力学描述的普适性。

Abstract: We study the long-time, large-distance asymptotics of finite-temperature dynamical Fredholm determinants using effective form factors. The method expresses these asymptotics as a functional of an effective phase shift. We determine its two branches on either side of a quadratic stationary point and the transition profile joining them. The profile is expressed through parabolic-cylinder functions. Its connection to the high-temperature Gaussian $β$ ensemble fixes the finite normalization, in agreement with an independent Riemann--Hilbert analysis. We also show that the leading exponential asymptotics suffice to recover the Euler-scale prediction of generalized hydrodynamics for the dynamical structure factor of hard rods.

</details>

### [168] [Exact SU($N$) symmetry resolution of bosonic Hubbard models: general boson-fermion mappings and flat-band singlet-cluster states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14183)
*Loïc Herviou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用 U(L) 与 U(N) 之间的 Howe 对偶，将 SU(N) 对称 Bose-Hubbard 模型在每个不可约表示中分别进行精确对角化，从而得到谱的普适性、玻色-费米配分函数恒等式及基态色铁磁结构等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SU(N) 对称 Bose-Hubbard 模型的 Hilbert 空间随粒子数和颜色数快速增长，传统对角化困难；作者希望借助 U(L)-U(N) Howe 对偶将模型按不可约表示分解，从而降低计算复杂度并揭示与 N 和统计无关的普适结构。

Method: 利用 Howe 对偶对 SU(N) 对称 Bose-Hubbard 模型按 SU(N) 不可约表示分块精确对角化；针对由 U(L) 生成元构成的任意哈密顿量，在给定轨道扇区中进行分析，并提出颜色分辨（color-resolved）方法，用于激发态、阻挫晶格和有限温度研究。

Result: 得到三个结构性结论：(i) 给定轨道扇区的谱与 N 及粒子统计无关；(ii) 玻色与费米正则配分函数满足精确恒等式 Z_b(-N)=(-1)^{N_b} Z_f(N)；(iii) 无阻挫跃迁时基态总是色铁磁体。应用上：推导出大 N 下少格点环上吸引性 SU(N) 玻色子的有限温度相图；在锯齿与 kagome 晶格上展示了由阻挫跃迁产生的单态团簇态（费米铁磁性的对应物）。

Conclusion: 该颜色分辨的精确对角化方法充分利用 Howe 对偶，将 SU(N) 玻色-哈伯德模型按不可约表示高效分解，揭示了谱与 N、统计无关等普适性质，并为研究阻挫晶格、激发态和有限温度相图提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We show how to perform exact diagonalization of SU($N$)-symmetric Bose-Hubbard models separately in each irreducible representation of SU($N$), by exploiting the Howe duality between U($L$) and U($N$). Three structural consequences follow for any lattice and any Hamiltonian built from the U($L$) generators: (i) the spectrum in a given orbital sector is independent of $N$ and of the particle statistics, (ii) the canonical partition functions for bosons and fermions obey the exact identity $Z_{\mathrm{b}}(-N)=(-1)^{N_\mathrm{b}}Z_{\mathrm{f}}(N)$, (iii) with unfrustrated hopping the groundstate is always a color ferromagnet, so that the color-resolved method we introduce is useful to study excited sectors, frustrated lattices and finite temperatures. As applications, we first derive the finite-temperature phase diagram of attractive SU($N$) bosons on few-site rings at large $N$ for arbitrary filling. We then show the emergence of singlet-cluster states from frustrated hopping, the counterpart of fermionic ferromagnetism, with the example of the sawtooth and kagome lattice.

</details>


### [169] [Exotic metallic phase in a heterotic Kondo lattice: violation of Luttinger's theorem and absence of Kondo gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14556)
*Hanting Zhong,Shuxiang Yang,Chao Cao,Xiao-Yong Feng,Jianhui Dai,Hai-Qing Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究具有异质层状结构的Kondo晶格系统，发现其顺磁相中强Kondo耦合区在电子半填充时仍保持金属性，Fermi面发生转变，Luttinger定理跨越临界点被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fermi面转变伴随量子相变在重费米子和范德瓦尔斯量子异质结构等强关联电子系统中广泛存在，而统一的Anderson晶格描述暗示半填充时强Kondo耦合区总是失稳金属相。本文旨在检验这一论断是否在异质层状结构的Kondo晶格系统中成立。

Method: 作者在具有异质层状结构的Kondo晶格系统顺磁相内进行分析：弱Kondo耦合区表现为分数量子Dirac液体，包含无质量或有质量Dirac费米子及电荷中性的自旋子Fermi面；强Kondo耦合区自旋子与Dirac费米子杂化，形成一个近扁平杂化带并伴随导带与价带重费米子带，从而产生大的电子Fermi面。

Result: 研究发现，根据层状结构的堆叠构型不同，这些带之间不出现Kondo能隙；在强耦合区即使精确半填充时系统仍保持金属性，且Luttinger定理在量子临界点两侧被违反。

Conclusion: 该结果修正了Anderson晶格描述中"半填充强Kondo耦合必然失稳金属相"的普遍预期，并对范德瓦尔斯量子异质结构的相关现象具有启示意义。

Abstract: Fermi surface transformation accompanied by quantum phase transition is widely observed in correlated electron systems including heavy fermion materials and van der Waals quantum heterostructures.
