Result: 1）Sr等掺杂原子倾向形成二聚体而非孤立分布；2）二聚体使两个掺杂空穴（或电子）束缚成单重态，且该单重态具有极化特性；3）掺杂使现场库仑排斥U不再是常数，在掺杂位点附近U可发生不连续下降，稳定了Z-对；4）Z-对是一种p波状的局域态，破坏CuO2面对称性，并沿垂直于极化方向相互吸引。

Conclusion: 作者提出所有氧化物高温超导体中空穴（电子）在制备阶段就预先配对成Z-对；Z-对与库珀对、张-莱斯单重态在概念上均不同，其吸引力源于强反铁磁交换能。Z-对可能普遍存在于所有非常规超导体中，其周期性排列可导致相干态和超导电性的出现。

Abstract: Based on first principles calculations, we show in hole-doped La2-xSrxCuO4, the Sr dopants prefer to form dimers, rather than be isolated as generally believed. Same trend applies to other superconductors such as Na-doped Ca2CuO2Cl2, and electron type Nd2-xCexCuO4. The dimer structure makes the two doped holes (electrons) bound up and form a singlet which is also polarized. Another type of dopant in cuprates is oxygen which behaves similarly. So, we propose that, for all oxide high TC superconductors, the holes (electrons) are pre-paired (Z-pairs) during the preparation of the superconductor. The spontaneously formed Z-pairs are conceptually different from not only the Cooper pairs, but also the Zhang-Rice singlets. The attraction force in a Z-pair originates basically from the strong antiferromagnetic exchange energy. We also found that the on-site Coulomb repulsion (U) is no longer a constant due to doping. Around a doping site, U can reduce its value discontinuously, which plays a key role in stabilizing the Z-pairs against isolated holes (electrons). A Z-pair is a p-wave like localized state which breaks the symmetry of the CuO2 plain and attracts each other along the direction perpendicular to the polarization. The Z-pairs may be popular in all of the unconventional superconductors and the periodic assembly can result in the emergence of coherence states and superconductivity.

</details>


### [207] [Quantum Geometry and Pair Breaking in Intra- and Intervalley Superconductivity of Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14480)
*Louis Primeau,Yang Zhang,Shi-Zeng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究电子掺杂菱形四层石墨烯的超导机制，发现布洛赫波函数形状因子（量子几何）强烈促进谷内手性 p 波配对并提高 Tc，但三角翘曲导致的能量失配切断 Cooper 对数发散，将谷内超导限制在相图中更窄的区域（主要在成对 Lifshitz 转变附近的环形或三瓣费米面区域）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 菱形石墨烯中从自旋-谷极化正常态涌现的超导引发一个基本困惑：由于三角翘曲，谷内 Cooper 对涉及的状态通常并不简并，缺乏传统 Cooper 不稳定性所依赖的粒子-粒子嵌套。近期模型研究提示非平凡能带几何可强烈促进手性配对，但这种几何增强如何与真实能带结构导致的拆对效应竞争仍不清楚。

Method: 采用 DFT 拟合的多轨道紧束缚模型研究电子掺杂菱形四层石墨烯的 Kohn-Luttinger 超导，在 RPA 屏蔽和投影 Cooper 相互作用中都保留完整的布洛赫波函数形状因子。将超导不稳定性分解为三项贡献：对称性分辨的形状因子修饰相互作用、由对称化配对色散决定的 Cooper 对相空间，以及三角翘曲产生的能量失配。

Result: 布洛赫形状因子强烈偏好谷内配对，主导不稳定性主要为 p_x - i p_y（手性）配对，且最大 Tc 显著高于谷间参考态；同时三角翘曲引起的失配切断 Cooper 对数，将谷内超导限制在相图中更窄区域。主导不稳定性出现在成对 Lifshitz 转变附近，主要在重构的环形或三瓣费米面区域。

Conclusion: 菱形石墨烯的超导由量子几何修饰的相互作用、成对费米学（pair fermiology）与三角翘曲诱导的拆对效应三者共同控制。该结论调和了手性配对增强与真实能带拆对之间的竞争，为菱形堆叠多层石墨烯中超导的实验相图提供了微观解释。

Abstract: The observation of superconductivity emerging from a spin- and valley-polarized normal state in rhombohedral graphene raises a basic puzzle: intravalley Cooper pairs involve states that are not generically degenerate because of trigonal warping, and therefore lack the particle--particle nesting underlying the conventional Cooper instability. While recent model studies suggest that nontrivial band geometry can strongly promote chiral pairing, how this geometric enhancement competes with realistic band-structure-induced pair breaking remains unclear. Here we study Kohn--Luttinger superconductivity in electron-doped rhombohedral tetralayer graphene using a DFT-fitted multiorbital tight-binding model, retaining the full Bloch-wave-function form factors in both RPA screening and the projected Cooper interaction. We separate the superconducting instability into three contributions: the symmetry-resolved form-factor-dressed interaction, the Cooper-pair phase space determined by the symmetrized pair dispersion, and the energy mismatch generated by trigonal warping. We find that the Bloch form factors strongly favor intravalley pairing, with a leading predominantly $p_x-ip_y$ instability and a substantially larger maximum $T_c$ than the intervalley reference state. At the same time, trigonal-warping-induced mismatch cuts off the Cooper logarithm and confines intravalley superconductivity to a much narrower region of the phase diagram. The dominant instability occurs near pair-Lifshitz transitions and primarily in reconstructed annular or three-lobed Fermi-surface regimes. Our results show that superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene is controlled by the combined effects of quantum-geometry-dressed interactions, pair fermiology, and trigonal-warping-induced pair breaking.

</details>


### [208] [Chemical-Potential-Dependent Superconducting Spectroscopy on Bi2Te3/NbSe2 Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14627)
*Pengyu Hu,Chun Yu Wan,Qiaoyan Yu,Chen Qian,Yisheng Gu,Xian Du,Xuhui Ning,Tingwen Miao,Shi Chen,Zihao Ma,Hao Yang,Hemian Yi,Weijiong Chen,Xiaoxue Liu,Liang Liu,Dandan Guan,Yaoyi Li,Shiyong Wang,Canhua Liu,Sangen Zhao,Fulin Wang,Junwei Liu,Hao Zheng,Jinfeng Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用稀释制冷机温度下的高分辨扫描隧道谱，研究拓扑绝缘体/超导体（Bi2Te3/NbSe2）异质结，发现5QL和7QL薄膜中相干峰呈现内扭结/内峰等偏离常规单一s波谱形的特征，并将其归因于Bi2Te3体态与表面态被选择性近邻耦合到NbSe2的单一超导能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究拓扑绝缘体/超导体异质结中超导近邻效应的来源与本质，辨析谱学特征究竟源于NbSe2复杂的序参量继承，还是Bi2Te3中体态与表面态对单一能隙的选择性近邻耦合。

Method: 在稀释制冷机温度下进行高分辨扫描隧道谱（STS）测量，并结合化学势依赖的光谱测量与模型模拟，对5QL和7QL Bi2Te3/NbSe2异质结进行分析。

Result: 在5QL和7QL Bi2Te3薄膜中均观测到具有内扭结或内峰的相干峰，偏离常规单一s波谱线形状，这些特征源于Bi2Te3体态与表面态被选择性近邻耦合到NbSe2的单一超导能隙。

Conclusion: 光谱特征并非继承NbSe2复杂的超导序参量，而是来自Bi2Te3体态和表面态被选择性近邻耦合到NbSe2单一超导能隙的贡献。

Abstract: High-resolution scanning tunneling spectroscopy performed at dilution refrigerator temperatures was employed to investigate the superconducting properties of topological insulator/superconductor (Bi2Te3/NbSe2) heterostructures. Coherence peaks with inner kinks or inner peaks, deviating from the conventional single s-wave spectral line shape, were observed in both 5 quintuple-layer (5QL) and 7QL Bi2Te3 thin films. Based on the combination of chemical-potential-dependent spectroscopic measurements and model simulations, we conclude that these spectral features arise from contributions of bulk and surface states in Bi2Te3 by selectively proximity-coupled to a single superconducting gap from NbSe2, rather than inheriting the complicated superconductor's order parameter of NbSe2.

</details>


### [209] [Kink boundary-induced polarity revealed by nonreciprocal charge transport in FeTe0.7Se0.3 superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14728)
*Shengyao Li,Ya Deng,Han Yan,Dianyi Hu,Zherui Yang,Xueyan Wang,Ming Yue,Qiong Wu,Zheng Liu,X. Renshaw Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文展示在中心对称超导体FeTe0.7Se0.3中，通过扭折边界诱导的应变工程实现非互易电荷输运，打破了传统上由本征晶体对称性限制的材料适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易电荷输运通常受本征晶体对称性支配，导致适用材料范围受限；研究者希望突破这一限制，在中心对称体系中实现方向依赖的电响应。

Method: 利用FeTe0.7Se0.3生长过程中的应力弛豫，生成具有不对称曲率的尖点状扭折带边界，产生空间非均匀应变场和有效面内极性，从而打破反演对称性；并在面外磁场下测量其输运性质。

Result: 在面外磁场下观察到显著的非互易输运，其对电流和温度（跨越超导转变）有强烈依赖；相比之下，平坦纳米片中响应可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 扭折边界诱导的对称性破缺为在中心对称体系中工程化非互易电荷输运提供了一条通用途径。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal charge transport, characterized by direction-dependent electrical response, provides a powerful probe of electronic structure and a platform for rectification. However, its realization is typically governed by intrinsic crystal symmetry, restricting the range of suitable materials. Here, we demonstrate nonreciprocal charge transport in an otherwise centrosymmetric superconductor, FeTe0.7Se0.3 (FTS), through kink boundary-induced strain engineering. The stress relaxation during growth generates a cusp-like kink-band boundary with asymmetric curvature, producing a spatially nonuniform strain field and an effective in-plane polarity that breaks inversion symmetry. Pronounced nonreciprocal transport is observed under out-of-plane magnetic fields, with strong dependence on current and temperature across the superconducting transition, in contrast to the negligible response in flat nanosheets. Our results identify kink boundary-induced symmetry breaking as a general route to engineer nonreciprocal charge transport in centrosymmetric systems.

</details>


### [210] [Driven Vortex Motion in Periodic Channel Potential Embedded in Superconducting Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14775)
*A. Al Luhaibi,A. Glatz,Michael Vogl,Hocine Bahlouli,J. B. Ketterson*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过数值求解含时Ginzburg-Landau方程，研究周期性宽度调制的超导通道中涡旋动力学，发现通道几何可有效控制所产生电场的谐波含量，适当设计的通道能强抑制高次谐波，使电场响应接近纯正弦波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超导器件中实现低谐波（接近纯正弦）的微波信号，为可扩展的超导量子比特架构提供低温片上波形发生器。

Method: 数值求解含时Ginzburg-Landau（TDGL）方程，研究宽度按正弦周期调制的超导通道（以平均宽度和调制幅度表征）中由涡旋运动驱动的动力学行为，并分析所产生电场的基频 $f_0$ 及其高次谐波。

Result: 与圆形孔周期钉扎势中涡旋格点动力学的结果（Al Luhaibi 等 [1]）相比，本工作表明电场谐波含量可通过通道几何有效调控；适当工程的通道轮廓能显著抑制高次谐波，使电场波形逼近纯正弦。

Conclusion: 几何调制为超导器件中低谐波微波信号工程提供了一条可行路径，有望实现控制超导量子比特的低温片上波形发生器，为可扩展的 transmon 量子计算架构提供有用的基础组件。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate vortex dynamics in periodically width-modulated superconducting channels by numerically solving the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau (TDGL) equations. The channels are designed with sinusoidally varying widths characterized by their average width and modulation amplitude. The motion of driven vortices generates an oscillatory electric field with a fundamental frequency, $f_0$, set by the channel periodicity, together with higher-order harmonics. In contrast to the situation of vortex lattice dynamics in a periodic pinning landscape of circular holes considered by Al Luhaibi et al. [1], we show that the harmonic content of the generated electric field can be effectively controlled through the channel geometry. In particular, appropriately engineered channel profiles strongly suppress higher-order harmonics, yielding an electric-field response that closely approaches a pure sinusoidal waveform. These results demonstrate that geometric modulation offers a viable route to engineering low-harmonicity microwave signals in superconducting devices and may enable cryogenic on-chip waveform generators for controlling superconducting qubits. Such devices could provide a useful building block for scalable transmon-based quantum computing architectures.

</details>


### [211] [Local Suppression of Superconductivity in Niobium Films by Focused Silicon Ion Implantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15200)
*Rochelle Y. Qu,Shunsuke Nishimura,Max Proepper,Joseph J. Barrera,Alexander Brooks,Joshua Vande Berg,Achim Nadzeyka,Aravind R. Sreeja,Jay C. LeFebvre,Roger K. Lake,Shane A. Cybart*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出用聚焦离子束直接写入的硅离子注入方式，在铌（Nb）薄膜中局部抑制超导性来制作“护城河”（moat），以替代传统的刻蚀工艺，用于捕获磁通、保护超导电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路中磁通会被俘获在磁通量子中，改变附近约瑟夫森结的临界电流并干扰电路工作。传统解决方法是在接地平面上刻蚀出护城河以存储磁通，但刻蚀工艺存在局限。作者希望找到一种无需刻蚀薄膜即可形成护城河的方法。

Method: 采用聚焦离子束直接写入硅离子注入，在三种厚度的铌薄膜电极上跨接写入单条注入线，离子注量跨越两个数量级。测量了各条线的转变温度、电阻和临界电流密度，并用含时Ginzburg-Landau模拟验证注入区能否捕获并保持涡旋。

Result: 注入线的转变温度随注量增大而下降，而周围铌膜保持不变；在4.2 K下，临界电流密度下降约两个数量级后，线才变为电阻性。线的临界电流与电阻的乘积在约化温度下遵循单一幂律，据此可由转变温度和电阻估算任意温度下的临界电流。模拟表明注入区能够俘获并保持涡旋。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果，作者给出了在4.2 K、100 nm铌接地平面上制作注入护城河的起始离子能量和注量建议，并估算了等效的宽束注量。

Abstract: Magnetic flux trapped in a superconducting circuit shifts the critical currents of nearby Josephson junctions and disturbs the operation of the circuit. The usual remedy is a moat etched through the ground plane to hold the flux away from the logic. We report direct write silicon ion implantation with a focused ion beam as a way to make moats without etching the film, by locally suppressing superconductivity in niobium (Nb). Single implanted lines were written across Nb electrodes in films of three thicknesses over two orders of magnitude in ion fluence, and the transition temperature, resistance, and critical current density of each line were measured. The transition temperature of the line falls with fluence while the surrounding Nb is unchanged, and the critical current density falls by about two orders of magnitude before the line becomes resistive at 4.2 K. The product of the critical current and the resistance of the lines follows a single power law in reduced temperature. This gives an estimate of the critical current of a line at any temperature from its transition temperature and resistance. A time dependent Ginzburg-Landau simulation indicates that an implanted region can capture and hold a vortex. From these results we suggest a starting ion energy and fluence for implanted moats in a 100 nm Nb ground plane at 4.2 K and estimate the equivalent broad beam fluence.

</details>


### [212] [Spatial interference changes the symmetry class of a Josephson element](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15746)
*Takayuki Kubo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间干涉如何通过多重谐波破坏约瑟夫森势的反演对称性，提出基于复数空间形状因子的判据，证明单一谐波恒为平移余弦而多谐波可产生反演不对称势，从而支持三次相互作用与三波混频；并证明相位反射在固定充电能下反转奇阶非线性而不改变电容分流结的完整量子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使局域能量具有反演对称性，横向均匀的约瑟夫森结反演对称性仍可能被破坏，作者旨在为这种新型非线性来源建立一般性判据，并探索其量子谱可逆性，以服务于三波混频等无法用标准约瑟夫森非线性实现的量子光学应用。

Method: 作者推导基于谐波相关的复数空间形状因子（spatial form factors）的一般判据：单一局域谐波保持为平移余弦；多个谐波则可获得与任何公共反演中心不相容的相位，从而允许三次相互作用。进一步分析相位反射操作（phase reflection）对所有展开系数的影响，并在固定充电能和任意偏置电荷条件下考察电容分流结的完整量子谱行为。

Result: 结果显示：单一局域谐波仍是平移余弦；多重谐波可产生反演不对称有效势，允许立方项与三波混频。相位反射反转所有奇阶展开系数（相对各自极小值）而保持偶阶系数不变，且在固定充电能下保持完整量子谱不变，从而能在无需重新调谐频谱的情况下反转奇阶非线性扇区。中心交叉结是多重局域谐波的具体实例；在外加场模型中，三分量磁场可生成反演不对称势，反转奇数个场分量即可精确反射该势且不改变电容分流结的量子谱。

Conclusion: 多重谐波的空间干涉可普遍地破坏约瑟夫森势的反演对称性并实现三次相互作用，且通过相位反射可在量子谱不变的前提下反转奇阶非线性，为三波混频和可调非线性量子器件提供了新的物理机制与设计判据。

Abstract: Spatial interference can break the inversion symmetry of an effective Josephson potential even in a laterally homogeneous junction with inversion-symmetric local energy. We derive a general criterion based on harmonic-dependent complex spatial form factors: a single local harmonic remains a shifted cosine, whereas multiple harmonics can acquire phases incompatible with any common inversion center, permitting cubic interactions and three-wave mixing. Phase reflection reverses all odd-order expansion coefficients about corresponding minima while preserving the even-order ones. For a capacitively shunted junction at fixed charging energy and arbitrary offset charge, it also preserves the complete quantum spectrum, enabling reversal of the odd nonlinear sector without spectral retuning. A centered cross junction with multiple local harmonics is one concrete example. Within the imposed-field model, a three-component magnetic field can generate an inversion-asymmetric effective potential, and reversing an odd number of field components exactly reflects it without changing the quantum spectrum of the capacitively shunted junction.

</details>


### [213] [Pressure Evolution of the Superconducting Order Parameter of Kagome Superconductor CsV3Sb5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16253)
*Haiyan Zuo,Wangju Yang,Zhifan Wu,Yuqing Zhao,Qiuxia Chen,Weiming Lao,Yangpeng Qi,Hechang Lei,Haizi Cong,Cong Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用软定向点接触谱（SDPCS）与超导电阻测量，系统研究了CsV3Sb5在静水压下超导序参量的演化，揭示了c轴两个s波能隙及ab面内强各向异性能隙随压力的变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CsV3Sb5在中等静水压下呈现双穹顶超导相图并伴随CDW被单调抑制，暗示SC与CDW之间非常规耦合；然而压力调制的超导态微观本质及其与CDW的关系尚不清楚。

Method: 采用软定向点接触谱（SDPCS）结合超导电阻测量，在静水压缩下进行各向异性点接触安德烈夫反射（PCAR）测量，并用双能带Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk（BTK）模型拟合c轴谱，分析ab面结的超导能隙各向异性与峰-谷-肩结构。

Result: c轴PCAR分辨出两个s波能隙：大能隙满足2Δ₁/kBTc>3.52，小能隙2Δₛ/kBTc<3.52；谱权重平均耦合比2Δ_avg/kBTc在P1与P2之间的压力窗口内增强；ab面结的超导能隙各向异性在P1以上被逐步抑制，并出现显著的峰-谷-肩结构，暗示超导准粒子与某玻色激发耦合增强。

Conclusion: 研究表明压力诱导的低能电子结构改变可能促进超导演化，且玻色模式耦合增强，为理解kagome超导体中CDW与SC的复杂纠缠提供了新见解。

Abstract: Kagome superconductors AV3Sb5 (A = K, Rb, Cs) offer an outstanding platform for exploring the interplay among charge-density-wave (CDW) order, superconductivity (SC), and nontrivial topological electronic states. For CsV3Sb5, moderate hydrostatic pressure induces a double-dome superconducting phase diagram while monotonically suppressing the CDW phase, which signals an unconventional coupling between SC and CDW. Nevertheless, the microscopic nature of the pressure-modulated superconducting state and its relationship with CDW order remain poorly understood. Here we employ soft directional point-contact spectroscopy (SDPCS), combined with superconducting resistive measurements, to probe the superconducting order parameter of CsV3Sb5 under hydrostatic compression. Our anisotropic PCAR measurements resolve two distinct s-wave superconducting gaps along the c-axis and a strongly anisotropic gap within the ab plane. Two-band Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk (BTK) fits to the c-axis spectra yield a large gap satisfying 2Delta1/kBTc > 3.52 and a small gap with 2Deltas/kBTc < 3.52. The spectral-weight-averaged coupling ratio 2Deltaavg/kBTc is enhanced in the pressure window between P1 and P2, suggesting that pressure-induced modifications of the low-energy electronic structure may contribute to the evolution of superconductivity. For ab-plane junctions, the superconducting gap anisotropy is gradually suppressed above P1, accompanied by the emergence of a pronounced peak-dip-hump structure suggestive of enhanced coupling between superconducting quasiparticles and a bosonic excitation. These results shed new light on the intricate entanglement between CDW and SC in kagome superconductivity.

</details>


### [214] [Photon echo from Maki-Thompson interference in a fluctuating s-wave superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16462)
*Hui Zhou,Yuan Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上研究了涨落s波超导体在双太赫兹脉冲驱动下的非线性光学响应，发现Maki-Thompson过程中时间反演电子-空穴轨迹的量子干涉会产生光子回波信号，而Aslamazov-Larkin过程的Cooper对输运不产生回波，说明太赫兹二维相干光谱可区分涨落电导机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分超导体涨落电导的不同微观机制（Aslamazov-Larkin与Maki-Thompson过程）在传统线性输运测量中较为困难，作者希望利用非线性光学手段——光子回波——提供一种可区分的实验探针。

Method: 从理论上研究涨落s波超导体在连续两个太赫兹脉冲驱动下的非线性光学响应，分析Maki-Thompson过程中时间反演电子与空穴轨迹之间的量子干涉，并与Aslamazov-Larkin过程（涨落Cooper对输运）进行对比；结合太赫兹二维相干光谱的探测方案。

Result: 响应在Maki-Thompson过程中表现出光子回波信号，源于时间反演电子-空穴轨迹的量子干涉；而Aslamazov-Larkin导电过程不产生回波信号。

Conclusion: 光子回波信号可通过太赫兹二维相干光谱直接测量，具有区分不同涨落电导机制（Maki-Thompson与Aslamazov-Larkin）的潜力。

Abstract: We study theoretically the nonlinear optical response of a fluctuating s-wave superconductor driven by two successive terahertz pulses. We show that the response exhibits a photon echo signal that arises from the quantum interference between the time-reversed electron and hole trajectories in the Maki-Thompson process. By contrast, the transport of the fluctuating Cooper pairs responsible for the Aslamazov-Larkin conductivity does not generate an echo. The photon echo signal can be readily measured using terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy, highlighting its potential to distinguish different fluctuation conductivity mechanisms.

</details>


### [215] [Molecule-intercalated van der Waals Josephson junctions uncover complex multiband superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16483)
*Daniel Margineda,David Caldevilla-Asenjo,Covadonga Álvarez-García,Daniel Tezze,Raul Arenal,Maxim Ilyn,Celia Rogero,Luis E. Hueso,Marco Gobbi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文在TaS2/NbSe2异质结上实现混合分子约瑟夫森结，通过选择性分子插层诱导类单层Ising超导，并观测到非对称Fraunhofer干涉、场致与自发约瑟夫森二极管效应，证明混合电极中存在时间反演对称性破缺的多带非常规超导态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子插层为调控范德华超导体的电子结构提供了途径，但插层化合物极少被集成到相位敏感量子器件中；作者旨在将分子工程超导体引入量子器件平台，以相位敏感方式探测和调控非常规超导相。

Method: 构建基于TaS2/NbSe2异质结构的混合分子约瑟夫森结，对TaS2电极进行选择性插层以诱导类单层Ising超导，并通过改变电极堆叠顺序和对下层NbSe2电极插层来对比验证；利用Fraunhofer干涉和约瑟夫森二极管效应进行相位敏感测量。

Result: 插层后的结表现出非对称Fraunhofer干涉，以及场致和自发（零场）约瑟夫森二极管效应；零场二极管效应在反转电极堆叠后消失，而在对下层NbSe2插层后重新出现，表明混合电极中的非常规超导态是导致非互易输运的根源。

Conclusion: 该行为与具有非平庸超导凝聚体相对相位的时间反演对称性破缺多带超导态一致。混合分子约瑟夫森结可作为相位敏感研究和集成分子工程超导体的量子器件平台，为发现和工程化非常规超导相提供了新机遇。

Abstract: Intercalation of molecular species provides a route to tailor the electronic structure in van der Waals superconductors, yet intercalated compounds have rarely been integrated into phase-sensitive quantum devices. Here, we realize hybrid molecular Josephson junctions based on TaS2/NbSe2 heterostructures. Selective intercalation of the TaS2 electrode induces monolayer-like Ising superconductivity, while the junctions exhibit asymmetric Fraunhofer interference, field-induced and spontaneous Josephson diode effects. The zero-field diode disappears upon reversing the electrode stacking and re-emerges after intercalating the lower NbSe2 electrode. These findings indicate that the hybrid superconducting electrode hosts unconventional superconductivity underlying the nonreciprocal transport, while molecular intercalation acts as a tuning knob for its internal phase structure. This behavior is consistent with a time-reversal-symmetry-breaking multiband superconducting state characterized by a nontrivial relative phase between superconducting condensates. Our results establish hybrid molecular Josephson junctions as a quantum-device platform for phase-sensitive investigation and integration of molecularly engineered superconductors, opening opportunities to uncover and engineer unconventional superconducting phases.

</details>


### [216] [Superconductivity from altermagnetic fluctuations: the crucial role of sublattice polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16507)
*Mats Barkman,Andreas Kreisel,Brian M. Andersen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究在具有交错磁（altermagnet）电子结构但并未发生磁有序的关联体系中，由交错磁涨落诱导的超导电性，并揭示了亚晶格极化（SLP）对超导配对对称性的决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规超导常出现在磁有序态附近，交错磁体因具有非相对论性自旋劈裂的能带结构而成为相关候选。但许多候选交错磁材料实际上从未穿越磁失稳，其无磁有序相中由交错磁涨落介导的超导机制尚不清楚。

Method: 作者研究无磁序体系中由交错磁涨落驱动的超导电性，重点分析正常态能带的亚晶格极化（SLP）如何约束库珀配对。SLP 是交错磁能带结构的固有属性，即使在无磁序时依然存在，会阻止不同亚晶格间形成库珀对。由此抑制由最近邻反铁磁交换介导的、倾向于自旋单态的超导配对，使更长程的同亚晶格键占主导。当次近邻交换为铁磁性（无阻挫的 Néel 序所需）时，体系进入自旋三重态超导。

Result: SLP 抑制最近邻反铁磁交换介导的自旋单态配对；在次近邻铁磁交换条件下，产生自旋三重态超导。该机制与具有长程 Néel 序的交错磁体截然不同——后者依靠自旋劈裂费米面倾向于等自旋配对。当无序相中更长程相互作用反而有利于单态配对时，SLP 导致具有与最近邻配对态特征明显区别的单态超导态。

Conclusion: 亚晶格极化是决定无磁序交错磁体系中超导态本质的核心因素，它阻断了跨亚晶格配对、改变有效配对通道，从而催生自旋三重态超导或特征独特的自旋单态，为理解交错磁涨落介导超导提供了新机制。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity in correlated systems often appears in the vicinity of a magnetically ordered state. This situation is also relevant for altermagnets, characterized by a nonrelativistic spin-split electronic structure in the presence of Néel order. Many proposed altermagnetic candidates, however, never cross the magnetic instability. We study superconductivity arising from altermagnetic fluctuations in such systems and show that the sublattice polarization (SLP) of the normal-state bands plays a pivotal role in determining the superconducting state. SLP, an inherent property of altermagnetic band structures that persists even without magnetic order, prevents Cooper pairs from forming between different sublattices. This suppresses pairing mediated by the nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange, which favors spin-singlet superconductivity, and leaves longer-range same-sublattice bonds dominant. When the next-nearest-neighbor exchange is ferromagnetic, as expected for frustration-free Néel order, this results in spin-triplet superconductivity. This mechanism is fundamentally different from that in altermagnets with long-range Néel order, where the spin-split Fermi surfaces favor equal-spin pairing. Finally, when longer-range interactions in the disordered phase instead favor singlet pairing, SLP leads to singlet states with characteristic properties distinct from nearest-neighbor pairing states.

</details>


### [217] [Tailored VxOy Metal--Insulator-Transition Oxides for Temperature-Triggered Smart Insulation in Superconducting Coils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16581)
*Mattia Ortino,Davide Uglietti,Andrea Testino,Nicola Casati,Alexander Steppke,Wiktoria Wnetkowska,Andrea Masi,Kamil Sedlak*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于REBCO磁体智能绝缘层的钒氧化物体系，旨在将金属-绝缘体转变温度从V2O3的150-170 K降低到实用的30-70 K窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能绝缘层可在正常运行时绝缘、仅在热点附近导电，从而兼顾绝缘线圈的快速励磁与无绝缘线圈的被动泄能优势。金属-绝缘体转变(MIT)氧化物是天然候选材料，但此前唯一在磁体中测试过的V2O3转变温度为150-170 K，远高于REBCO磁体实用的30-70 K温区，因此需要探索钒氧体系以降低转变温度。

Method: 通过TGA氧化还原处理制备多相钒氧化物粉末，并用水热法制备V3O7(含水和退火两种形态)；制作V2O3-环氧树脂试样作为参考与验收标准；开展低温测量研究热滞后现象，测量V2O3的比热容，并建立首个电-热模型模拟电流分流行为。

Result: TGA氧化还原处理得到的多相粉末有效转变温度接近127 K，比V2O3低约25 K；水热V3O7(无论含水或退火)电阻过高且无可用的转变；低温测量显示可重复的热滞后，V2O3实测比热容对早期模型输入修正了近两个数量级；电-热模型成功再现了通过智能绝缘层的电流分流。

Conclusion: 钒氧体系可通过多相TGA处理将转变温度降至约127 K，但尚未达到30-70 K目标；掺杂V2O3和V8O15被确定为下一步的研究目标。

Abstract: Smart insulation is a turn-to-turn layer that insulates in normal operation and conducts only near a hot spot. It could combine the fast ramping of insulated coils with the passive energy dump of non-insulated coils. Metal-insulator transition (MIT) oxides are natural candidates, but the only one tested in a coil so far, V2O3, switches at 150-170 K, far above the 30-70 K window useful for REBCO magnets. We explore the vanadium-oxygen system to lower the transition. TGA redox processing produced multi-phase powders with an effective transition near 127 K, about 25 K below V2O3. Hydrothermal V3O7, hydrated or annealed, was far too resistive and showed no usable transition. V2O3-epoxy specimens provided a robust reference and an acceptance criterion. Cryogenic measurements revealed a reproducible thermal hysteresis, and the measured specific heat of V2O3 corrects earlier model inputs by close to two orders of magnitude. A first electro-thermal model reproduces current sharing through the smart-insulation layer. Doped V2O3 and V8O15 are identified as the next targets.

</details>


### [218] [Microscopic Modeling of Interlayer Tunneling in Twisted Bilayer Cuprates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16930)
*Siddhant Panda,Andreas Kreisel,Laura Fanfarillo,P. J. Hirschfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文为扭曲双层铜氧化物超导系统建立了更真实的微观模型，通过轨道重叠计算层间隧穿振幅，发现与唯象模型相比，时间反演对称破缺的 d+id' 态的稳定区域被明显缩小，且接近 45° 扭转时约瑟夫森临界电流被强烈抑制，与实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲双层铜氧化物系统中实验结果的巨大变异性与理论预言之间的分歧，表明需要更准确的微观模型，特别是对层间隧穿进行更真实的描述。

Method: 拟合面内跃迁，并通过计算扭曲正方晶格模型每个格点上 d_{x²-y²} 波函数之间的轨道重叠来确定层间隧穿振幅；随后研究能带结构、费米面、谱函数，并与唯象模型结果对比；最后确定以扭转角、填充和直接隧穿强度（由层间 c 轴距离控制）为变量的相图，并计算相图上的约瑟夫森临界电流。

Result: 观察到符号交变的隧穿振幅、直接隧穿项对扭转角的强烈依赖，以及往往主导直接隧穿的长程隧穿振幅。相较于唯象模型，谱权重发生显著重新分布，改变了不同超导基态之间的竞争，导致时间反演对称破缺 d+id' 态的区域缩小。接近 45° 扭转时约瑟夫森临界电流被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 更真实的轨道重叠隧穿模型揭示了层间隧穿对谱权重和超导基态竞争的重要影响，并预言了接近 45° 扭转时约瑟夫森临界电流的抑制，与实验观测一致，为理解扭曲双层铜氧化物超导态提供了关键的微观基础。

Abstract: The field of twistronics, recently extended to twisted bilayer cuprate systems, has generated considerable interest due to the prediction of $d+id'$ states close to a $45^\circ$ twist, as suggested by multiple experimental probes. Theoretical studies of these systems using a variety of models and many-body theoretical techniques have revealed multiple competing order parameters. The large variability in experimental results and theoretical predictions demands an accurate microscopic model for these systems, in particular a more realistic description of the interlayer tunneling. In this study, we fit the in-plane hopping and compute the interlayer tunneling amplitudes by calculating the orbital overlap between $d_{x^2-y^2}$ wave functions at each site of the twisted square-lattice model. We observe sign-changing tunneling amplitudes, a strong twist-angle dependence of the direct tunneling term, and long-range tunneling amplitudes that often dominate over direct tunneling. We investigate the band structure, Fermi surfaces, and spectral functions in comparison with those obtained from the phenomenological model and find a substantial redistribution of spectral weight that modifies the competition between different superconducting ground states. We determine the phase diagram as a function of twist angle, filling, and direct tunneling strength, which is controlled by the $c$-axis distance between the monolayers, and observe a reduction in the region of time-reversal-symmetry-breaking (TRSB) $d+id'$ states compared with the phenomenological model. Finally, we investigate the Josephson critical current across the phase diagram and find strongly suppressed critical currents close to a $45^\circ$ twist, consistent with experimental observations.

</details>


### [219] [High-Efficiency Field-Free Superconducting Diodes in Mo$_2$N/FePt Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16972)
*Juan S. Cruz,Emilio De Biasi,Juan C. Zapata,Yeonkyu Lee,Jeehoon Kim,Nestor Haberkorn*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在室温生长的Mo₂N/FePt双层膜中实现了近理想、无外场的超导二极管行为，源于FePt条带畴的剩余杂散场导致极性依赖的通量进入与涡旋运动阈值，实现了高达约85%的二极管效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种能实现高整流效率、无需外部磁场即可工作的超导二极管，以满足无耗散电子学应用对非互易输运的需求。

Method: 在室温生长的Mo₂N/FePt双层膜中，利用FePt层的剩余条带畴产生的周期性杂散场，结合微磁学模拟分析条带间的杂散场分布及桥边缘处的非对称场增强，并通过改变面内磁化准备角和反向磁场扫描实现对非互易性的连续调控与可逆切换。

Result: 一个电流极性保持近似无耗散，直至接近估算的拆对电流的较大比例，而相反极性在低得多的电流下即产生耗散，在较薄双层膜中二极管效率最高约85%；非互易性可被连续调谐并可逆切换。

Conclusion: 边缘控制的通量进入与涡旋运动是实现高效率超导二极管的有效途径，基于剩磁条带畴的无场超导二极管有望应用于无耗散电子器件。

Abstract: Near-ideal, field-free superconducting diode behavior is demonstrated in room-temperature-grown Mo$_2$N/FePt bilayers. The response is consistent with remanent FePt stripe domains producing polarity-dependent thresholds for flux entry and vortex motion. Micromagnetic simulations reveal a periodic stray-field modulation across the stripes and asymmetric field enhancement near their ends at the bridge edges. One current polarity remains nearly dissipationless up to a sizable fraction of the estimated depairing current, whereas the opposite polarity develops dissipation at much lower current, yielding diode efficiencies up to approximately 85% in the thinner bilayer. The nonreciprocity can be continuously tuned by the in-plane magnetic preparation angle and reversibly switched by reverse-field excursions through the hysteretic reversal of the FePt layer. These results support edge-controlled flux entry and vortex motion as a route to high-efficiency superconducting diodes.

</details>


### [220] [H$_2$-Like Units and Pressure-Tunable High-$T_\mathrm{c}$ Superconductivity in NaH$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17148)
*Tianran Chen,Taner Yildirim*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理研究氢化物NaH₆，发现其R-3m和Pm-3m两种相分别在低压和高压下占主导，在中等压力（10–50 GPa）下即可实现34–230 K的超导转变温度，并揭示拉曼活性氢振动模式是超导的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富氢材料是实现高温常规超导的重要途径，但通常需要极高压力。作者旨在寻找在较温和压力下即可稳定存在并具有高Tc的氢化物，并理解其超导机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理方法，结合大规模氢化物结构搜索与高通量研究；对多个竞争相（重点为R-3m与Pm-3m）进行动力学稳定性、电子-声子耦合（EPC）及Tc计算；并通过分子动力学与声子重整化评估非谐效应；进一步利用模式分辨的EPC分析及基于对称性的氢子晶格二阶矩张量最小模型解释超导趋势。

Result: R-3m相在低至10 GPa仍动力学稳定，Tc=34 K，50 GPa时升至99 K；立方Pm-3m相在更高压力下热力学占优，50 GPa时Tc达230 K且随加压仅缓慢下降。两相虽含通常被视为不利高Tc的拉长H₂类单元，但分子动力学与非谐计算支持结构在采样时间内持续存在、非谐效应较小。模式分辨EPC表明拉曼活性氢振动主导超导；在R-3m中，随压力诱导的H₂单元相对三重轴倾斜增大，这些模式线宽增加，与Tc上升同步，对称性模型显示氢子晶格垂直于三重轴的响应分量贡献显著。

Conclusion: NaH₆是一种不寻常的类分子氢化物，其高Tc超导性可能源于氢子晶格的重组，从而在相对温和的压力下实现高温超导。

Abstract: Hydrogen-rich materials offer a route to high-temperature conventional superconductivity, but often require extreme pressures. Here we report a first-principles study of NaH$_6$, identified in our large-scale hydride search and independently predicted in a subsequent high-throughput study. Several phases compete, with $R\bar{3}m$ and $Pm\bar{3}m$ dominant over broad pressure ranges. The $R\bar{3}m$ phase remains dynamically stable down to 10 GPa, where its calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ is 34 K, rising to 99 K at 50 GPa. Cubic $Pm\bar{3}m$ becomes thermodynamically competitive at higher pressures and reaches a calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ of 230 K at 50 GPa, decreasing only weakly upon further compression. Molecular dynamics and phonon-renormalization calculations support structural persistence over the sampled timescale and indicate relatively small anharmonic effects. Despite their high $T_\mathrm{c}$, both phases contain elongated H$_2$-like units, often considered unfavorable for high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ hydrides. Mode-resolved electron-phonon coupling links Raman-active hydrogen vibrations to superconductivity. In $R\bar{3}m$, the linewidths of these modes increase with the pressure-induced tilt of H$_2$ units relative to the threefold axis, alongside the rise in $T_\mathrm{c}$. A minimal symmetry-based model of the vibration-induced second-moment tensor of the hydrogen sublattice shows that these trends are consistent with a significant contribution from response components perpendicular to the threefold axis. NaH$_6$ thus represents an unusual molecular-like hydride in which hydrogen-sublattice reorganization may enable high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ superconductivity at comparatively moderate pressures.

</details>


### [221] [Observation of stacked vortex structure with number controllable Majorana zero mode in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe heterojunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17701)
*Xinru Han,Zhaokun Luo,Quanxin Hu,Xingjian Zhu,Xiaohong Pan,Dan Mu,Xinyu Liang,Zulin Xu,Qiheng Wang,Ryan L. Kong,Mingyuan Li,Zhengliang Zhang,Fazhi Yang,Mojun Pan,Xin Liu,Qiangqiang Gu,Baiqing Lv,Gan Wang,Hong Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文报道在化学计量比的一层五层（1QL）Bi₂Te₃/FeTe 异质结中，通过扫描隧道显微谱学观测到数量可控的马约拉纳零模（MZMs）及堆叠涡旋结构，为拓扑量子计算提供了可控平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固态器件中创建并控制马约拉纳零模是实现容错拓扑量子计算的关键挑战。作者旨在寻找能够产生并调控 MZMs 数量的超导界面体系。

Method: 利用低温扫描隧道显微镜（STM）和扫描隧道谱（STS），对化学计量比的 1 个五层厚 Bi₂Te₃/FeTe 异质结进行表征，观察长周期莫尔超晶格、超导能隙的纳米级调制以及磁涡旋的堆叠核心结构，并分析涡旋束缚态的色散分支与量子极限分支。

Result: 发现了由晶格失配引起的长周期莫尔条纹，其诱导超导能隙的纳米尺度调制；磁涡旋呈现堆叠核心结构，具有两支束缚态分支——远离涡旋中心演化为 X 形图案的色散分支，以及以空间未劈裂的 MZMs 和离散整数量子化 Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon 态为特征的量子极限分支。所有测得的涡旋中均观察到 MZMs，其数量可由磁场和 Bi₂Te₃ 岛尺寸调控。

Conclusion: 化学计量比的 1QL-Bi₂Te₃/FeTe 是一种图案化超导界面，可实现孤立及少涡旋的马约拉纳构型，为马约拉纳零模的融合（编织）提供了可控平台。

Abstract: Creation and control of Majorana zero modes (MZMs) in solid devices is the key challenge in realizing fault tolerant topological quantum computation. Here we report spectroscopic evidence for number controllable MZMs in stoichiometric one quintuple layer Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe heterostructures, together with the observation of stacked vortices in superconducting islands. Low temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy and spectroscopy reveal a long period moire superlattice arising from lattice mismatch, which induces nanoscale modulation of superconducting gaps. Magnetic vortices exhibit a stacked core structure with two layer derived bound state branches: a dispersive branch evolving into an X-shaped pattern away from the vortex center, and a quantum-limit branch characterized by a spatially unsplit MZMs together with discrete integer quantized Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon states. Notably, MZMs are observed in all measured vortices, and their number can be tuned by magnetic field and the size of Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$ islands. These results establish stoichiometric 1QL-Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe as a patterned superconducting interface that enables isolated and few-vortex Majorana configurations, providing a controlled platform for fuse MZMs.

</details>


### [222] [Imaging atomic-scale surface structure and superconducting gap of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17879)
*Dongming Zhao,Shamarita Deb,Lichen Ji,Souvik Das,Paul Simon,Raúl Cardoso-Gil,Steffen Wirth,Walter Schnelle,Sushmita Chandra,Jian-Feng Ge,Claudia Felser*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过X射线衍射、截面扫描透射电子显微镜和低温扫描隧道显微镜等手段，研究了天然异质错配层状化合物(SnS)1.17NbS2的表面原子结构与局域超导电性，发现其表面原子排布与体相错配结构不同，但两种终止面上的超导能隙几乎一致，且能隙空间分布不随错配晶格发生可见调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德华耦合二维材料超晶格可调控电子能带结构并诱发新奇量子态，而错配层状化合物作为天然组装的超晶格，其中共存共格与非共格晶格方向所引入的结构调制可能改变超导等实空间电子序，因此需要弄清其表面结构与局域超导行为之间的关系。

Method: 结合X射线衍射（XRD）、截面扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）进行结构表征，并利用电输运测量和2 K下的低温扫描隧道显微镜/隧道谱（STM/STS）研究局域超导电性。

Result: XRD和截面STEM确认了体相错配结构；STM分辨出大面积以SnS和NbS2终止的台阶，其表面原子排布与体相错配结构不一致；2 K下的隧道谱显示两种终止面上的超导能隙几乎相同，且能隙的空间变化未出现与错配晶格相对应的可见调制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了这一天然组装错配异质结表面结构与超导态的原子尺度图像，表明表面重构并未显著影响其局域超导能隙，为理解错配层状化合物中的超导电性提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Superlattices of van der Waals-coupled two-dimensional materials offer a platform for engineering electronic band structure and fostering emergent quantum states. Misfit layered compounds provide naturally assembled superlattices, in which crystallographically distinct two-dimensional subsystems coexist within a single bulk crystal. Therein, the presence of both commensurate and incommensurate lattice directions introduces structural modulation, which might alter real-space electronic orders such as superconductivity. Here, we investigate the atomic-scale surface structure and local superconductivity of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2 using structural characterization, electrical transport, and low-temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM). While X-ray diffraction and cross-sectional scanning transmission electron microscopy confirm the bulk misfit structure, STM resolves large SnS- and NbS2-terminated terraces, where the surface atomic registry differs from the bulk misfit structure. Furthermore, tunnelling spectroscopy at 2 K reveals nearly identical superconducting gaps on both terminations, and the spatial variation of the gap shows no visible modulation that corresponds to the misfit lattice. These results establish an atomic-scale picture of the surface structure and superconducting states of this naturally assembled misfit heterostructure.

</details>


### [223] [Effects of the choice of the exchange-correlation functional on high-temperature superconductivity predictions in covalent-cage M(BN)5 family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18171)
*Zhongyu Wan,Guo-Hua Zhong,Cheng-wei Ju,Ruiqin Zhang,Hai-Qing Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过高通量第一性原理计算，系统量化了 M(BN)5 共价笼状超导体中电子-声子耦合性质对不同交换关联泛函的响应。研究发现，PBE 泛函系统性高估晶格体积，导致声子软化、EPC 增强并引发 Peierls 不稳定性；而 PBEsol 泛函消除了 PBE 下 Rb(BN)5 出现的虚频，预测其常压 Tc 达 113 K、2 GPa 下达 138 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共价笼状超导体的实验合成激发了常压高温超导的研究希望，但准确估算超导性质的计算策略面临挑战。具体而言，超导性质（尤其是电子-声子耦合）对交换关联泛函选择的高度敏感性尚不明确，亟需厘清不同 XC 效应对 M(BN)5 体系 EPC 性质的影响机制。

Method: 采用高通量第一性原理计算，对 M(BN)5 超导体在不同交换关联泛函（PBE 与 PBEsol）下的电子-声子耦合性质进行定量比较，分析晶格体积、声子谱、EPC 强度、Peierls 不稳定性、电子局域化与能带结构等随泛函选择的变化，并结合压力依赖分析考察压缩对声子与电子性质差异的调制作用。

Result: 1. 超导相关的金属共价键对泛函选择的不确定性表现出异常高的敏感性，区别于传统金属键介导的超导体。2. PBE 系统性高估晶格体积，导致声子软化、EPC 增强及 Peierls 不稳定性。3. PBEsol 通过强化短程相互作用处理更准确，消除了 PBE 下 Rb(BN)5 的虚频，预测常压 Tc=113 K、2 GPa 下 Tc=138 K。4. 共价键中增强的电子局域化与短程关联通过双重机制（晶格尺度的声子硬化与电子尺度的能带展宽）协同抑制 Peierls 畸变并离域反键态。5. 压力依赖分析表明压缩会减弱不同 XC 泛函间的声子差异，而电子性质对费米能级附近精细能带的 XC 扰动仍显著敏感。

Conclusion: 交换关联泛函的选择对 M(BN)5 共价笼状超导体的 EPC 性质具有决定性影响：PBE 因高估体积而低估超导稳定性，PBEsol 则给出更可靠且显著提升的 Tc 预测。研究揭示了共价键体系中电子局域化与短程关联调控超导的双重物理机制，为共价笼状超导体的计算筛选与实验探索提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The recent experimental synthesis of covalent cage superconductors has ignited hopes for ambient-pressure high-temperature superconductivity, while the computational strategy for discovering new materials poses challenges for accurately estimating superconducting properties. Here, we quantified the response of electron-phonon coupling (EPC) properties in M(BN)5 superconductors to different exchange-correlation (XC) effects using high-throughput first-principles calculations. We found that the metallic covalent bonds responsible for superconductivity exhibit anomalously high sensitivity to functional selection uncertainty, distinguishing them from conventional superconductors mediated by metallic bonding. The widely used PBE functional systematically overestimates lattice volumes, inducing phonon softening, enhanced EPC, and consequent Peierls instability. In contrast, PBEsol, which is more accurate through enhanced treatment of short-range interactions, eliminates imaginary frequencies in Rb(BN)5 that appear at the PBE level, yielding an outstanding superconducting critical temperature (Tc) of 113 K at ambient pressure and 138 K at 2 GPa. Enhanced electron localization and short-range correlation in covalent bonds manifest through dual mechanisms: phonon hardening at the lattice scale and band widening at the electronic scale, which collectively suppress Peierls distortion and delocalize antibonding states. Pressure-dependent analysis reveals that compression attenuates phonon discrepancies between XC functionals, whereas electronic properties remain notably sensitive to XC-induced perturbations of fine electronic bands near the Fermi level.

</details>


### [224] [Quantitative Impact of Occupation Smearing Methods on Conventional Superconductivity: from Electronic Structure through Lattice Dynamics to Electron-Phonon Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18185)
*Zhongyu Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本论文以六氢化物 CsH6 为基准，系统研究布里渊区电子网格展宽函数及其宽度对声子介导超导计算的误差影响，发现长尾展宽削弱电声耦合、短尾展宽导致 CsH6 被误判为动力学不稳定，而无参数的最优化四面体方法表明 CsH6 在常压下动力学稳定且超导转变温度高达 121 K；并基于自由电子气模型推导出适用于不同展宽函数的展宽宽度标度关系，为高通量筛选提供客观、精确、高效的参数选取协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在第一性原理计算金属体系时，布里渊区离散电子网格需引入展宽函数及其宽度（如广泛使用的 0.02 Ry Methfessel-Paxton 方案）以保证费米面处能量与占据数的连续性，但这类经验参数对声子介导超导的定量影响长期被忽视，参数选择往往仅凭惯例，缺乏客观依据，可能导致对高温超导体的误判。

Method: 以六氢化物 CsH6 为基准体系，系统对比长尾展宽、短尾展宽与无参数的最优化四面体方法（optimized tetrahedron method）在费米能级位移、态密度、声子谱及电声耦合等方面的表现，定量追踪展宽诱导误差的传播机制；并从自由电子气模型出发推导展宽宽度的标度关系，构建适用于不同展宽函数的参数选取协议。

Result: 长尾展宽使占据窗口离域化，引起显著的费米能级偏移、态密度平坦化和声子模式硬化，从而削弱电声耦合；短尾展宽则采样不足，导致态密度振荡和增强的费米面嵌套，通过放大的电子屏蔽和 Peierls 不稳定性软化声学模式并产生虚频，使 CsH6 被误判为动力学不稳定而被舍弃。而最优化四面体方法显示 CsH6 在常压下动力学稳定，具有 121 K 的超导转变温度。所推导的展宽宽度标度关系可客观、精确、高效地指导不同展宽函数的参数选择。

Conclusion: 经验性展宽参数会通过费米能级偏移、态密度形变、声子硬化和虚频产生等机制系统性地干扰声子介导超导的预测，甚至导致有前景的高温超导体被错误舍弃；采用无参数的最优化四面体方法或依据自由电子气模型推导的展宽宽度标度关系进行参数选取，可避免主观经验参数掩盖理论上优异的高温超导体，提升高通量筛选的可靠性。

Abstract: In first-principles calculations of metallic systems, the discrete electronic mesh in the Brillouin zone necessitates a smearing function and its width (e.g., the widely used 0.02 Ry Methfessel-Paxton scheme) to enforce continuity of energy and occupation at the Fermi surface. However, the quantitative impact of such empirical parameters on phonon-mediated superconductivity has long been overlooked, leading to their habitual selection by mere convention. Here, using hexahydride CsH6 as a benchmark, we systematically investigate the magnitude and propagation mechanism of smearing-induced errors against the parameter-free optimized tetrahedron method. Long-tailed smearing delocalizes the occupation window, causing substantial Fermi-level shifts, flattening of the density of states, and hardening phonon modes, thereby weakening electron-phonon coupling. Conversely, short-tailed smearing suffers from insufficient sampling, producing density-of-states oscillations and enhanced Fermi-surface nesting; via amplified electronic screening and Peierls instabilities, this softens acoustic modes and generates imaginary frequencies, causing CsH6 to be dynamically unstable and discarded. However, the optimized tetrahedron method reveals that CsH6 is dynamically stable under ambient pressure with a remarkable superconducting transition temperature of 121 K. To prevent subjective empirical parameters from obscuring theoretically promising high-temperature superconductors, we derive a simple scaling relation for the smearing width, applicable to different smearing functions, from the free-electron-gas model, offering an objective, precise, and efficient protocol for parameter selection in high-throughput screening.

</details>


### [225] [Phonon-Mediated Superconductivity near Phase Boundaries in Rhombohedral Multilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18286)
*Konstantinos Sourounis,Peter M. Oppeneer*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过求解自洽 Migdal-Eliashberg 方程，结合第一性原理输入并纳入电子基态的相互作用驱动对称性破缺，证明菱方（rhombohedral）石墨烯中多个超导区域可由声子介导配对机制解释，并预测了尚未发现的超导相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非扭转多层石墨烯中观测到多个超导区域，其随位移场和载流子浓度变化呈现不同的温度与磁场响应，被归因于不同电子相，可能对应不同微观机制。此前纯电子配对机制已被广泛研究，但缺乏对声子介导超导的系统深入考察。

Method: 采用耦合的自洽 Migdal-Eliashberg 方程，输入来自第一性原理计算的结果，并计入由相互作用驱动的电子基态对称性破缺；通过改变载流子浓度与位移场，系统计算超导性质。

Result: 再现了实验观测到的多个超导区域，其能隙和临界温度 T_c 等超导性质与实验相当；同时预测了额外的、尚未被发现的超导相。

Conclusion: 菱方石墨烯中的多个超导区域可以满意地由声子介导配对机制解释，无需诉诸不同的电子配对机制。

Abstract: The recent observation of superconductivity in untwisted multilayer graphene has sparked intense interest in its microscopic origin. As the displacement field and carrier density are varied, superconductivity emerges in several distinct regions with different temperature and magnetic-field responses. This behavior has been attributed to the presence of distinct electronic phases, raising the possibility that different superconducting regions originate from different microscopic mechanisms. While purely electronic pairing mechanisms have been extensively explored, an in-depth examination of phonon-mediated superconductivity is still missing. Here, we solve the coupled selfconsistent Migdal-Eliashberg equations using input from first-principles calculations and incorporating interaction-driven symmetry breaking of the electronic ground state. Varying the carrier density and displacement field, we reproduce the experimentally observed superconducting regions with comparable superconducting properties such as gap and $T_c$ and predict additional, as-yet undiscovered superconducting phases. Our findings exemplify that several superconductivity regions in rhombohedral graphene can be satisfactorily explained by phonon-mediated pairing.

</details>


### [226] [The role of dependency range in thermally coupled superconducting networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18418)
*Nahala Yadid,Bnaya Gross,Shlomo Havlin,Nadav M. Shnerb*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究热耦合非晶超导网络中由热输运产生的依赖关系所导致的级联失效，发现系统复现了三种经典相变机制，但热驱动依赖随距离同时展宽并减弱，使长程热耦合在升温时反而稳定超导相，与几何模型相反，且级联呈各向异性的一维传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相互依赖网络理论大多依赖几何网络模型，其中依赖范围由外部人为设定且耦合强度与距离无关；这难以刻画真实材料中由物理输运自然产生的依赖关系。作者希望填补这一空白，理解实际系统中涌现的依赖如何影响网络脆弱性与相变行为。

Method: 作者采用经过实验标定的阻性分流约瑟夫森结（RSJ）模型，模拟热耦合的非晶超导网络。该模型中依赖关系由电流重分配和受热扩散率（thermal diffusivity）控制的热介导相互作用自然涌现，通过数值模拟考察相变与级联失效行为。

Result: 系统复现了三种典型相变区间：连续、成核主导和混合阶（mixed-order）相变。但热驱动依赖同时随距离展宽并减弱，因此长程热耦合在升温时可稳定超导相，这与几何模型中的失稳作用相反。级联失效呈现各向异性，表现为一维的前沿传播。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可由实验验证的视角，补充并修正了以往几何框架的结论，表明物理输运涌现的依赖关系会显著改变网络脆弱性与级联传播的机制。

Abstract: Interdependent networks can be highly fragile because dependency links amplify local failures into cascades that trigger global collapse. Much of the theoretical understanding comes from geometric network models, where dependency range is imposed externally and coupling strength is distance independent, and it is unclear how these insights apply to real materials, where interdependence arises from physical transport. Here we study thermally coupled amorphous superconducting networks, in which dependencies emerge from current redistribution and heat-mediated interactions governed by thermal diffusivity. Using an experimentally calibrated model of resistively shunted Josephson junctions, we show the system reproduces the three canonical transition regimes: continuous, nucleation-dominated, and mixed-order. However, because heat-driven dependency simultaneously broadens and weakens with distance, long-range thermal coupling can stabilize the superconducting phase upon heating, in contrast with its destabilizing role in geometric models. Cascades are anisotropic, with one-dimensional front propagation. These results offer an experimentally testable perspective complementing the geometric framework.

</details>


### [227] [Twofold Symmetry Observed in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe Interfacial Superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.05904)
*Xinru Han,Hailang Qin,Tianluo Pan,Bin Guo,Kaige Shi,Zijin Huang,Jie Jiang,Hangyu Yin,Hongtao He,Fei Ye,Wei-Qiang Chen,Jia-Wei Mei,Gan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文在Bi₂Te₃/FeTe界面超导体中，通过准粒子干涉(QPI)与宏观磁阻测量，观察到二重对称的超导能隙，且对称方向与FeTe中Te-Te方向一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导配对对称性对于理解超导体的微观超导机制至关重要，而Bi₂Te₃/FeTe界面超导体系的配对机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用扫描隧道显微镜中的准粒子干涉(QPI)技术，并结合宏观磁阻测量（面内角度依赖磁阻与面内上临界场）进行研究。

Result: 观察到二重的超导能隙对称性：能隙内外能量对应的QPI图样显示出明显的各向异性超导能隙；面内角度依赖磁阻和面内上临界场均呈现清晰的二重对称。该二重对称方向与FeTe中Te-Te方向一致，从而削弱了由双共线反铁磁序产生该对称性的可能性。

Conclusion: 这一发现为进一步理解Bi₂Te₃/FeTe超导体系的拓扑性质提供了关键信息，并进一步推动了该体系配对机制的理论研究兴趣。

Abstract: Superconducting pairing symmetry are crucial in understanding the microscopic superconducting mechanism of a superconductor. Here we report the observation of a twofold superconducting gap symmetry in an interfacial superconductor Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe, by employing quasiparticle interference (QPI) technique in scanning tunneling microscopy and macroscopic magnetoresistance measurements. The QPI patterns corresponding to energies inside and outside the gap reveal a clear anisotropic superconducting gap. Furthermore, both the in-plane angle-dependent magnetoresistance and in-plane upper critical field exhibit a clear twofold symmetry. This twofold symmetry align with the Te-Te direction in FeTe, which weakens the possible generation by bi-collinear antiferromagnetism order. Our finding provides key information in further understanding of the topological properties in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe superconducting system and propels further theoretical interests in the paring mechanism in the system.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.other'></div>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Revealing New Leakage Paths in GAAFETs using Predictive First-Principles Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14298)
*Denis Mamaluy,Juan P. Mendez,Coleman Cariker,Michael Titze,Alex A. Belianinov*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文利用电荷自洽的非平衡格林函数（NEGF）量子输运模拟，空间解析了 GAAFET 中由漏致介电势垒降低（DIDBL）驱动的栅-漏直接隧穿泄漏路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GAAFET 是 FinFET 的继任者，是先进半导体节点持续微缩的核心。尽管其静电控制改善，但实测显示其在负栅压下漏电流严重偏离热电子行为，且随栅压更负而增大。该泄漏被归因于经高-k 介质的栅-漏直接隧穿，并被 DIDBL 增强。现有研究多依赖唯象泄漏模型，缺乏从第一性原理出发、空间分辨的机理图景。

Method: 采用基于 Contact Block Reduction（CBR）方法的预测性、电荷自洽 NEGF 模拟，构建多端量子输运框架，将源、漏、栅均视为载流子输运的量子力学接触，并在整个器件内解析局部电流密度。

Result: 空间分辨的电流密度表明，栅注入的载流子优先在栅靠近漏极一侧隧穿高-k 介质（该处介电势垒被漏极偏压最强地降低），随后被漏极收集。

Conclusion: 该结果提供了 DIDBL 驱动栅-漏泄漏机制的直接实空间图像，揭示了泄漏主要发生在栅靠近漏侧的区域。

Abstract: Gate-All-Around Field-Effect Transistors (GAAFETs) are emerging as successors to Fin Field-Effect Transistors (FinFETs) and are central to continued scaling at advanced semiconductor nodes. Despite their improved electrostatic control, recent measurements show that the drain current in GAAFETs deviates strongly from thermionic behavior at negative gate voltages, increasing as the gate voltage becomes more negative. This leakage has been attributed to direct gate-drain tunneling through the high-$k$ dielectric, enhanced by drain-induced dielectric barrier lowering (DIDBL). Here, we use predictive, charge-self-consistent Non-Equilibrium Green Function (NEGF) simulations based on the Contact Block Reduction (CBR) method to spatially resolve this leakage pathway in GAAFETs. Unlike approaches based primarily on phenomenological leakage models, our first-principles, multi-terminal quantum-transport framework treats the source, drain, and gate as current-carrying quantum-mechanical contacts and resolves the local current density throughout the device. The spatially resolved current density shows that gate-injected carriers tunnel through the high-$k$ dielectric preferentially near the drain side of the gate, where the dielectric barrier is most strongly lowered by the drain bias, and are collected by the drain. This provides a direct real-space picture of the DIDBL-driven gate-drain leakage mechanism.

</details>


### [229] [Non-Hermitian Two-Kink Control of Corner States in Twisted Chern Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15470)
*Xiao-Ming Zhao,Su-Peng Kou*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究由相反陈数的陈绝缘体组成的扭转双层，提出非厄米层间质量携带的质量相位扭结与独立的虚动量扭结，可将质量缺陷角态的谱、面内及层间控制解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角态通常受边界质量缺陷的几何锚定束缚，这种几何锚定同时制约了角态的空间分布与内部自由度控制，难以独立调控其能量、空间位置及层极化等性质。

Method: 以相反陈数陈绝缘体组装的扭转双层为平台，引入两种解耦的扭结机制：非厄米层间质量携带的质量相位扭结产生母角极点并固定其能量与本征衰减，定向层间隧穿编码层极化；独立的虚动量纹理则重塑闭合边界包络并在各角间重新分配稳态右态权重。通过扭转的齐—吴—张（QWZ）双层在任意（含凹）边界上验证该分工，并用30°扭转Haldane十二边形构造十二个有限能量母态。

Result: QWZ双层验证了谱、面内与层间控制在任意（含凹）边界上的解耦分工；30°扭转Haldane十二边形实现十二个有限能量母态，其带内谱流形在边界图样改变时持续存在，同时归一化右子空间权重被重新分配至六、三、二或一个目标角。

Conclusion: 两个扭结可协同或竞争，同时保持谱谱系、空间权重和层特征彼此独立，从而为跨不同边界几何与质量纹理控制质量缺陷角态建立了统一框架。

Abstract: Corner states are bound by boundary-mass defects, but this geometric anchoring restricts their spatial and internal control. Focusing on twisted bilayers assembled from Chern insulators with opposite Chern numbers, we show that a mass-phase kink carried by a non-Hermitian interlayer mass and an independent imaginary-momentum kink separate the spectral, in-plane, and interlayer control of mass-defect corner states. The mass-phase kink creates the parent corner pole and fixes its energy and intrinsic decay, while directed interlayer tunneling encodes its layer polarization. The imaginary-momentum texture instead reshapes the closed-boundary envelope and redistributes stationary right-state weight between corners. A twisted Qi--Wu--Zhang bilayer verifies this division on arbitrary, including concave, boundaries. A $30^\circ$ twisted Haldane dodecagon realizes twelve finite-energy parent states whose in-gap spectral manifold persists while boundary patterns redistribute its normalized right-subspace weight toward six, three, two, or one target corners. The two kinks therefore cooperate or compete while keeping spectral ancestry, spatial weight, and layer character distinct, thereby establishing a unified framework for controlling mass-defect corner states across disparate boundary geometries and mass textures.

</details>


### [230] [Coexistence of Multiple Distinct Corner-State Classes via Nearest-Neighbor Couplings Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15727)
*Luhong Su,Dongyi Wang,Shenglong Guo,Xinjiang Chen,Lei Zhou,Shaojie Ma,Mengyao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文在仅最近邻耦合（无长程耦合）条件下实现了多类局域态的共存，并在拓扑电路实验中验证了 I 型、II 型角态、角局域连续谱束缚态（BIC）、平带态和边缘态的共存，证明通过仅调节最近邻耦合即可替代长程相互作用，实现丰富的局域角态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类局域态（尤其是不同类型拓扑角态）的共存通常依赖足够强的长程耦合，这给跨系统实验实现带来挑战。作者希望找到无需长程耦合（甚至无次近邻耦合）也能实现多类角态共存的途径。

Method: 作者在仅含最近邻耦合的条件下设计晶格，通过调节最近邻耦合来替代长程耦合的作用，使单一晶格同时支持 I 型与 II 型角态、角局域束缚态 BIC、平带态和边缘态。实验上采用拓扑电路系统实现该晶格，利用电路设计严格排除长程耦合，直接表征能带谱和局域本征模的空间分布。

Result: 实验确认在单一晶格中多类局域角模共存，包括 I 型与 II 型角态、角局域 BIC、平带态和边缘态，并直接得到能带谱与局域本征模的空间形貌。

Conclusion: 仅修改最近邻耦合即可替代长程相互作用，实现多类角态和丰富局域态，提供了一条确定性且广泛可用的路径，对模分复用和多模拓扑器件具有直接应用前景。

Abstract: The coexistence of multiple classes of localized states, including different types of topological corner states, is highly desired; yet its realization typically requires sufficiently strong long-range couplings, bringing challenges to experimental realizations across a wide range of systems. Here, we demonstrate the emergence of multiple localized corner modes evolution under purely nearest-neighbor coupling conditions without long-range couplings, not even next-nearest-neighbour couplings, and experimentally confirm their coexistence in a single lattice. Our platform simultaneously hosts type-I and type-II corner states, corner localized bound states in the continuum, flat-band states, and edge states. We experimentally realize such lattice using topolectrical circuits, a system that can strictly exclude long-range coupling by design, enabling direct characterizations of both the energy-band spectrum and the spatial profiles of the localized eigenmode. This work demonstrates that solely modifying nearest-neighbor couplings can replace the role of long-range interactions in realizing multiple types of corner states, providing a deterministic and widely accessible route to multiple corner-state classes and rich localized states, with immediate potential for mode-division multiplexing and multimode topological devices.

</details>


### [231] [Deterministic positioning accuracy of B centers in hBN by electron irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16468)
*A. Núñez Marcos,C. Arnold,J. Barjon,S. Buil,J. -P. Hermier,A. Delteil*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文在扫描电子显微镜（SEM）中利用原位阴极发光（CL）监测，生成单个B中心（436 nm单光子发射体）阵列，量化了发射体定位偏差的来源，并将定位标准差归因于束-发射体相互作用（27 nm）及碳二聚体前驱体密度等附加因素（总计40 nm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方氮化硼（hBN）是量子技术中有前景的材料平台，可承载高质量单光子发射体（SPE）。其中B中心可在预定义位置通过电子束辐照逐个生成，这对自上而下的量子光子学至关重要。然而，发射体创建的空间精度强烈制约其潜在应用，因此需要识别并量化限制确定性定位的偏差来源。

Method: 在扫描电子显微镜（SEM）中配合原位阴极发光（CL）监测，生成单个B中心阵列。通过CL映射测量发射体生成瞬间电子束与发射体之间的距离，从而直接估计束-发射体相互作用的空间范围，并表征偏离目标位置的随机误差来源。

Result: 束-发射体相互作用是发射体制造精度的主要制约因素，其贡献的标准差为27 nm。附加贡献（主要来自UV发光碳二聚体前驱体的有限密度）使总体定位标准差增加至40 nm。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了B中心创建技术可能的改进方向，有望实现亚10 nm的定位精度，从而提升hBN量子光子器件的确定性制造能力。

Abstract: Hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) is a promising material platform for quantum technologies, enabling both the realization of nanophotonic devices and the hosting of point defects that act as high-quality single-photon emitters (SPEs). Among these defects, B centers, which reproducibly emit at 436 nm, can be generated individually at predefined positions using electron beam irradiation, a crucial capability for top-down quantum photonics. Yet, the spatial accuracy of emitter creation strongly conditions the potential applications. Here, we generate arrays of individual B centers in a scanning electron microscope (SEM) with in-situ cathodoluminescence (CL) monitoring. We characterize the sources of random deviations from the target positions to identify the limitations to their deterministic positioning. In particular, we measure the distance between the electron beam and the emitter at the moment the emitter is created based on CL mapping. This approach provides a direct estimation of the spatial extent of the beam-emitter interaction. We find this contribution to be the main constraint to the emitter fabrication accuracy, yielding a standard deviation of 27 nm. Additional contributions, primarily from the finite density of UV-emitting carbon dimer precursors, increase the overall positional standard deviation to 40 nm. Our results highlight the possible improvements to the B center creation technique to reach sub-10 nm accuracy.

</details>


### [232] [Optically driven ultrafast lattice contraction in a topological crystalline insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16513)
*M. H. Berntsen,W. Chen,D. Phuyal,J. Wu,A. Grubišić-Čabo,M. Dendzik,Q. Guo,A. Orthaber,C. Li,R. M. Geilhufe,J. Weissenrieder,O. Tjernberg*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文利用时间-角度分辨光电子能谱(tr-ARPES)、X射线衍射(XRD)和第一性原理计算，研究了拓扑晶体绝缘体Pb₁₋ₓSnₓSe对超快光激发的晶格与电子响应。研究发现光激发虽使电子温度远超拓扑-普通转变温度，却未诱发拓扑相变，反而驱动小于1 ps内的超快晶格收缩(Δa≈-0.45 pm)，使体系更深地进入拓扑相。总能量计算表明该行为源于拓扑区带反转改变了光生电荷转移方向、瞬时增强了原子键合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态调控拓扑序是量子材料探索的核心挑战。作者旨在厘清拓扑晶体绝缘体在超快光激发下是否发生拓扑相变，以及晶格与电子如何耦合响应，从而揭示带反转对非平衡晶格响应的根本影响。

Method: 结合时间-角度分辨光电子能谱(tr-ARPES)探测电子结构动力学、X射线衍射(XRD)测定静态与瞬态晶格常数，并辅以第一性原理总能量计算解析光生电荷转移与键合机制。通过静态XRD确定晶格常数为拓扑转变的关键控制参数，临界值a_c=6.073 Å。

Result: 静态XRD确立晶格常数a_c=6.073 Å为区分普通绝缘相与拓扑相的临界值。尽管光激发使电子温度远高于拓扑-普通转变温度T_c，拓扑相中并未发生相变；相反，光激发在<1 ps内驱动Δa≈-0.45 pm的超快晶格收缩，抵消热膨胀并阻止体系达到a_c，从而将体系更深推入拓扑相。总能量计算表明该现象源于带反转反转了光生电荷转移方向并瞬时增强原子键合。

Conclusion: 带反转作为一种机制从根本上改变了非平衡晶格响应，光学激发不是驱动拓扑相→普通相转变，而是使拓扑相更稳定。这一发现对拓扑材料的超快光学调控具有普遍意义，为利用带反转实现拓扑序的主动控制提供了新视角。

Abstract: The ability to dynamically control topological order remains a central challenge in the exploration of quantum materials. Here, we investigate the lattice and electronic response of the topological crystalline insulator (TCI) Pb$_{1-x}$Sn$_x$Se to ultrafast optical excitation using time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (tr-ARPES) in combination with X-ray diffraction (XRD) and first-principles calculations. Static XRD measurements establish the lattice constant as the key control parameter for the topological transition, with a critical value $a_\mathrm{c}=6.073$ Åseparating the normal-insulator and topological phases. Despite generating electronic temperatures far above the topological-to-normal transition temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$, optical excitation in the TCI phase does not induce a phase transition. Instead, it drives an ultrafast lattice contraction of $Δa \approx -0.45$ pm within less than 1 ps that counteracts thermal expansion and prevents the system from reaching $a_\mathrm{c}$. The optical excitation therefore pushes the system deeper into the topological phase, providing a direct explanation for the absence of an optically driven transition. Total-energy calculations reveal that this behavior originates from the band inversion in the topological regime, which reverses the character of the photoinduced charge transfer and transiently strengthens atomic bonding. These findings identify band inversion as a mechanism that fundamentally alters the nonequilibrium lattice response, with general implications for ultrafast optical control of topological materials.

</details>


### [233] [Momentum conservation for ab initio calculations with pseudopotentials via a phase space electronic structure formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18631)
*Guy Ohad,Nadine C. Bradbury,Joseph E. Subotnik*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种在离子赝势存在下构建的稳健相空间电子哈密顿量，证明必须引入非局域单电子算符 Γ̂^nl 以同时保证赝势非局域分量与线/角动量守恒，并通过推广的 Nafie 精确等式在双原子分子体系上验证了方法的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的电子结构方法基于 Born-Oppenheimer 近似，仅依赖核位置 R，无法正确描述核动量 P 及电子动量等超越 Born-Oppenheimer 的物理。引入赝势后，其非局域分量与动量守恒之间的矛盾使得直接构造依赖 (R,P) 的相空间哈密顿量变得困难。因此，作者旨在建立一种既能纳入离子赝势非局域部分、又能同时守恒线动量和角动量的相空间电子哈密顿量。

Method: 作者构造了依赖核位置 R 与核动量 P 的相空间电子哈密顿量 Ĥ_PS(R,P)。关键点在于：为正确处理赝势的非局域分量同时守恒线/角动量，方法强制引入一个耦合电子与核动量的非局域单电子算符 Γ̂^nl。为验证该 ansatz，作者将 Nafie 精确等式（联系电子动量与电子位置的关系）推广到包含赝势的情形，得到广义 Nafie 等式，并在涵盖多种元素的一系列双原子分子上，将相空间电子结构理论的结果与该广义等式进行基准对比。

Result: 在一组覆盖广泛元素的多样双原子分子测试集上，所提出的相空间电子结构理论与广义 Nafie 等式结果取得了极好的一致性，表明该方法能有效捕捉超越 Born-Oppenheimer 框架的有意义电子动量信息。

Conclusion: 该方法成功构建了在离子赝势存在下稳健、且满足线/角动量守恒的相空间电子哈密顿量，并通过引入 Γ̂^nl 算符解决了非局域赝势带来的困难。数值验证证实在 ab initio 测试集上具有出色精度，证明了相空间电子结构理论在刻画超越 Born-Oppenheimer 近似的电子动量方面的能力。

Abstract: We demonstrate how to build a robust phase space electronic Hamiltonian $\hat{H}_\text{PS}(\boldsymbol{R},\boldsymbol{P})$ that depends on nuclear position $\boldsymbol{R}$ and nuclear momentum $\boldsymbol{P}$ in the presence of ionic pseudopotentials. In particular, we show that in order to account for the non-local component of a pseudopotential while also conserving linear and angular momentum, one is forced to introduce a non-local one-electron operator $\hat{\boldsymbolΓ}^\text{nl}$ that couples electronic and nuclear momenta together. To test the validity of our ansatz, we derive a generalized form of Nafie's exact equality (which relates electronic momentum and electronic position) so as to include the presence of pseudopotentials. We then benchmark our proposed phase space electronic structure theory against this generalized Nafie-like result for a diverse set of diatomic molecules spanning a wide range of elements. We find excellent agreement across this ab initio test set, highlighting the strength of our method in capturing meaningful electronic momentum beyond the Born-Oppenheimer framework.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [On physical implementability of reinforcement-learning-driven reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14131)
*Eiji Konishi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文给出一个关于强化学习驱动推理过程在物理神经网络硬件实现中的否定性结果：在给定奖励的外部势能存在时，动力学中出现思维链（chain of thought）与系统的Lyapunov稳定性相矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2025年前后，机器学习领域对强化学习驱动的推理过程进行了大量研究。本文旨在考察该推理过程在物理神经网络模型中的硬件可实现性，特别是在给定奖励所对应的外部势能条件下，探讨推理动力学能否稳定实现。

Method: 将一个强化学习驱动的推理过程嵌入物理神经网络模型的硬件实现中，引入与给定奖励对应的外部势能，并利用Lyapunov稳定性理论分析该系统的动力学行为，考察思维链在动力学演化中是否存在。

Result: 在硬件实现中，当存在给定奖励对应的外部势能时，动力学中思维链的存在与系统的Lyapunov稳定性相互矛盾，即二者不可兼得，构成一个否定性结果。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在物理神经网络硬件实现中，强化学习驱动的思维链式推理过程在外部奖励势能条件下无法满足Lyapunov稳定性，提示此类推理过程在物理层面的实现存在内在困难。

Abstract: In the machine learning community, the reinforcement-learning-driven reasoning process has been intensively studied since around 2025. In this article, we present a negative result on the hardware implementation of this reinforcement-learning-driven reasoning process in a physical neural network model in the presence of a reward external potential energy for a given reward. In this hardware implementation, the existence of a chain of thought in dynamics contradicts the Lyapunov stability of the system.

</details>


### [235] [Weyl's theorem and the theoretical origin of Boson peak in two-dimensional scalar spring network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15318)
*Cunyuan Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文以零温标量弹簧网络为模型，仅借助厄米矩阵的Weyl定理，从数学上证明无序（由网络拓扑连接定义）可将振动态密度（VDOS）推向低频，从而在那里积累形成反常低频VDOS，揭示了非晶固体中该反常现象的普适、干净机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶固体中普遍观测到的反常低频振动态密度（VDOS）的起源已被争论数十年，作者希望剥离复杂因素，从数学原理层面揭示其干净而深刻的产生机制。

Method: 采用零温标量弹簧网络模型（既能刻画非晶结构，又能重现反常VDOS特征的最简模型），以网络拓扑连接性定义无序，利用厄米矩阵的Weyl定理进行数学推导，证明无序会导致VDOS向低频移动并积累反常部分；该方法不依赖微扰论和唯象假设。

Result: 数学上证明了网络拓扑连接意义上的无序能够使振动态密度向低频方向转移，从而在低频区域积累形成反常VDOS；该理论框架仅基于Weyl定理，具有普适性与通用性。

Conclusion: 本文在数学原理层面揭示了一个深刻而干净的非晶固体反常低频VDOS产生机制，为真实世界场景提供了普适且有价值的理论框架。

Abstract: The origin of the anomalous low-frequency vibrational density of states (VDOS) universally observed in amorphous solids has been debated for decades. In this work, we model amorphous solids as a zero-temperature scalar spring network, the simplest model that captures both the amorphous structure and the features of the anomalous VDOS. Using this scalar network, we mathematically demonstrate that disorder can lead to a shift of the VDOS toward low frequencies, thereby accumulating the anomalous part there. Disorder is defined through the topological connectivity of the network. The validity and universality of the theoretical framework are general and valuable for real-world scenarios because it rests only on Weyl's theorem for Hermitian matrices and is free of perturbation and phenomenological assumptions. This work strips away complex factors and reveals, at the level of mathematical principles, a profound and clean mechanism for the origin of the anomalous VDOS in amorphous solids.

</details>


### [236] [Eigenstate transitions and critical transport in 3D Aubry-André model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15842)
*Pragna Das,Lev Vidmar,Miroslav Hopjan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过数值研究三维Aubry-André模型在有限尺寸可公度近似下的谱性质、波函数性质与时间演化，确认存在两个临界点：一个类比三维Anderson模型的实空间局域-退局域转变，另一个是无Anderson模型对应物的对偶动量空间转变。两个临界点均表现出量子遍历与局域之间的临界行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三维Aubry-André模型中是否存在不同于三维Anderson模型的临界行为，并揭示其实空间与对偶动量空间两类局域-退局域转变的普适性质及其临界动力学特征。

Method: 在有限尺寸可公度近似下，对三维Aubry-André模型进行数值标度分析，综合运用谱测度（能隙比、谱形状因子）和波函数测度（逆参与比、参与熵）来估计临界点位置；并结合本征谱压缩系数χ与本征态分形维数d^(1)的普适关系，以及由谱分形维数和晶格维数表征的临界动力学指数z来分析临界动力学。

Result: 明确证实存在两个临界点：一个是类比三维Anderson模型的实空间局域-退局域转变，另一个是无Anderson对应物的对偶动量空间转变。两个临界点处，本征态与本征谱满足谱压缩系数与本征态分形维数间的普适关系，波包动力学由临界动力学指数z支配，且与三维Anderson模型类似，临界动力学指数为亚扩散，z=3。

Conclusion: 三维Aubry-André模型展现出更丰富的临界结构，同时具有实空间与对偶动量空间两类局域-退局域转变，二者都呈现量子遍历与局域临界点的标志性特征，临界动力学为亚扩散且z=3，与三维Anderson模型一致。

Abstract: We numerically study spectral and wave-function properties, as well as time evolution, in finite-size commensurate approximations of the 3D Aubry-André model. We explicitly show, through scaling analysis, the existence of two critical points. One of the critical points is a localization-delocalization transition in real-space, similar to the critical point in the 3D Anderson model. The other is a localization-delocalization transition in a dual momentum space, which does not have any analog in the 3D Anderson model. We estimate their values from spectral measures, such as the gap ratio and the spectral form factor, as well as from wave-function measures, such as the inverse participation ratio and the participation entropy. Additionally, we demonstrate that both critical points exhibit hallmarks of critical points between quantum ergodicity and localization. First, the eigenstates and the eigenspectrum at the critical point fulfill the mutual universal relation between the eigenspectrum compressibility $χ$ and the eigenstate fractal dimension $d^{(1)}$ [S. Jiricek et al., Proc. Nat. Acad. Sci. 123, e2518027123 (2026)]. Second, the dynamics of the wave packet at the critical point is governed by the critical dynamical exponent $z$, characterized by the spectral fractal dimension and the dimensionality of the lattice [M. Hopjan and L. Vidmar, Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 060401 (2025)]. Thus, similarly to the 3D Anderson model, the critical dynamical exponent is sub-diffusive with the value of $z=3$.

</details>


### [237] [Quantum Localization in Geometric Complex Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15874)
*Jan Lewenstein-Sanpera,Tobias Grass,M. Àngeles Serrano,Bruno Juliá-Díaz*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究量子局域化现象在复杂网络拓扑下的表现，发现小世界网络倾向于产生扩展态，而非小世界网络倾向于产生局域态，表明复杂连接性可以替代随机能量无序的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子局域化通常源于如Anderson局域化中的在位随机势等外部因素，但一些具有复杂几何结构的系统可能在无外势的情况下复现类似结果。作者旨在探究单粒子态的局域化如何受底层网络拓扑影响。

Method: 采用几何软配置模型（geometric soft configuration model，即 S^1 模型），通过调节模型温度与异质性参数生成不同的结构模式，重点研究从小世界到非小世界区域的转变，并分析单粒子态的局域化行为。

Result: 研究发现小世界网络倾向于拥有扩展态，其波函数遍布整个系统尺度；而非小世界网络倾向于产生局域态。

Conclusion: 复杂网络的连接拓扑能够扮演随机能量无序所起的作用，即仅凭连接性即可导致量子局域化，无需外加随机势。

Abstract: Quantum localization, the phenomenon by which wave diffusion is halted, here understood as the superposition of propagating waves, can have many different origins, such as onsite random potentials in Anderson localization. However, some systems exhibit complex geometries and could potentially reproduce similar results without external potentials. In this work, we study how localization of single particle states is affected by the underlying topology of a simple but highly predictive complex network model: the geometric soft configuration model $\mathbb S^1$. By tuning the model's temperature and heterogeneity parameters, we are able to generate different structural patterns and focus on the transition from a small-world to a non-small-world regime. Our study suggests that small-world networks tend to have extended states, which spread the whole system's size, in contrast to non-small-world networks which tend to localize, showing how complex connectivity can play the role assigned to random energetic disorder.

</details>


### [238] [Orbital cancellation and reentrant localization in a quasiperiodic trimer ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16197)
*Xin Wang,Pei-Jie Chang,Quanfeng Lu,Muxi Zheng,Dong Ruan,Gui-Lu long*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在准周期调制增强的情况下，均匀轨道失谐可通过对角项抵消恢复空间扩展；三聚体梯子模型中，仅第三分子带保持强调制，出现两个重入高参与度窗口，且耦合梯子相比解耦梯子会收窄这两个窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究准周期调制通常会抑制空间扩展（局域化），而本文探索能否通过均匀轨道失谐这一对角调控手段抵消投影的现场势，从而在调制强度增大时恢复波函数的空间范围，并揭示剩余带间调制的作用。

Method: 在具有固定跃迁和轨道调制符号的三聚体梯子中，同步增大失谐与调制幅度，利用分子带图像观察避让交叉和轨道权重重分布；采用完整带参与度标度识别重入高参与度窗口，并以波包传播验证空间恢复；对剩余带间调制引入依赖分子能级间隔的自能描述，并用高斯求积改进闭行走行列式估计以交叉校验能量分辨衰减，最后在相同有限尺寸参与度判据下对比耦合与解耦梯子。

Result: 失谐与调制同步增大驱动连续避让交叉，重新分配轨道权重，使两个较低分子带中的投影现场势相互抵消而被抑制，第三个带仍保持强调制；完整带参与度标度呈现两个重入高参与度窗口，波包传播在代表性参数下证实空间恢复；剩余带间调制产生的自能量级取决于分子能级间隔；高斯求积提升了衰减估计的收敛性；耦合梯子恢复完整非对角调制后，两个采样窗口均变窄，其中第二个窗口的绝对收缩更大。

Conclusion: 均匀轨道失谐可通过分子带间对角项抵消，在准周期调制增强时抑制有效势并实现空间扩展的重入恢复；剩余带间调制强度由分子能级间隔控制，且完整非对角调制的存在会收窄高参与度窗口，第二个窗口收缩更显著。

Abstract: Uniform orbital detuning can restore spatial extent even as quasiperiodic modulation strengthens. In a trimer ladder with fixed hoppings and orbital modulation signs, increasing detuning and modulation amplitude together drives successive avoided crossings that redistribute orbital weights, suppressing the projected onsite potential in both lower molecular bands while leaving the third strongly modulated. Complete-band participation scaling supports two reentrant high-participation windows, with wave-packet propagation corroborating spatial recovery at representative controls over the studied sizes and times. These diagonal cancellations leave residual interband modulation, which generates a self-energy whose magnitude depends on the molecular level separation. Gaussian quadrature improves convergence of closed-walk determinant estimates used to cross-check energy-resolved attenuation. Comparing the coupled and decoupled ladders under the same finite-size participation criterion shows that restoring the full off-diagonal modulation narrows both sampled windows, with a larger absolute contraction of the second.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [IceCube data for astrophysical neutrinos exhibit q-statistical behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14129)
*Constantino Tsallis,Ugur Tirnakli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文分析IceCube天体物理中微子数据，发现其能谱可用q-高斯分布（q≈1.6）良好拟合，与宇宙线（q≈1.2）和LHC质子-质子碰撞（q≈1.1）相比q值更高，表明存在更长程的时空关联，并进一步支持非广延熵在黑洞及宇宙学现象中的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验IceCube最新天体物理中微子数据是否同样服从q-高斯分布，以强化非广延熵（如S_q类、S_δ类）在黑洞、暗能量等相关宇宙学系统中起关键作用的已有证据，并比较不同物理系统间的非广延指数差异。

Method: 对IceCube天体物理中微子的整个实测能量区间能谱，采用q-高斯分布进行拟合；并与宇宙线能谱、LHC质子-质子碰撞能谱的拟合结果（q值）进行对比分析，探讨不同系统所对应的非广延指数与时空关联范围。

Result: IceCube数据可用q-高斯分布在整个实测能量区间内满意地描述，其动量分布非广延指数q≈1.6，明显高于宇宙线的q≈1.2和LHC的q≈1.1，表明存在非常长程的时空关联，而其具体物理机制仍是待解决的开放问题。

Conclusion: 天体物理中微子、宇宙线及LHC碰撞的能谱均可由q-高斯分布统一描述，进一步支持非广延熵在这些物理系统中的核心作用；IceCube较高的q值意味着更长程的时空关联，但其背后的具体物理机制尚不明确，构成一个富有启发性的开放课题。

Abstract: Relevant IceCube recent data for astrophysical neutrinos are analyzed. Similarly to cosmic rays and proton-proton LHC collisions, the corresponding spectra are satisfactorily described, for the entire interval of measured energies, through q-Gaussians. This fact reinforces the already available evidence that nonadditive entropies (either of the $S_q$ class or of the $S_{delta}$ one, or even alternative ones) appear to play a crucial role in systems such as black holes and related cosmological phenomena (e.g., dark energy). The IceCube data yield sensibly high values for the momenta-distribution nonadditive index (q \simeq 1.6), in contrast with the cosmic rays values (q \simeq 1.2) and the LHC values (q \simeq 1.1). This fact neatly indicates very-long-ranged space-time correlations. The detailed associated physical mechanisms remain, at the present stage, as an instigating open issue.

</details>


### [240] [Competitive scaling of small-subsystem-optimization heuristics for LABS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14361)
*Ginger E. Lau,Adil A. Gangat*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究低自相关二元序列（LABS）问题，提出一种仅优化小规模解耦子系统的启发式方法，在 n=4 时实现 O(1.333^N) 的时间缩放，与当前经典与量子最优方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LABS 问题通常被表述为具有极粗糙能量景观的自旋晶格能量最小化问题，全局优化十分困难。现有启发式方法直接优化整个 N 自旋系统，对硬件算力要求高。作者试图验证仅对少量自旋的小子系统进行迭代优化，是否也能实现高效的全局优化，从而降低对专用优化硬件的规模要求。

Method: 提出"孤立子系统"启发式方法：每次只选取 n 个自旋组成的小子系统，将其与系统其余部分解耦并独立进行优化，迭代重复这一过程以实现 LABS 的全局优化。实验重点考察 n=4 的情形。

Result: 在 N=[27, 60] 区间上，n=4 的孤立子系统启发式方法实现了 O(1.333^N) 的时间-解缩放；相比之下，当前最优的经典方法缩放为 O(1.34^N)，量子方法为 O(1.21^N)。

Conclusion: 仅需少量逻辑自旋的孤立子系统迭代优化即可实现与全局优化启发式相竞争的性能缩放，表明只具备少量逻辑自旋的专用优化硬件可能具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: The low autocorrelation binary sequences (LABS) problem, often formulated as a spin-lattice energy-minimization problem, is a combinatorial optimization problem with a very rugged energy landscape. We demonstrate that iterative optimization of very small subsystems of n spins that are decoupled from the rest of the system achieves global optimization of LABS with a time-to-solution scaling that is competitive with heuristics that optimize the global system of N spins directly. Our isolated-subsystem heuristic with n = 4 achieves a time scaling of O(1.333N ) on the interval N = [27, 60], while the current state-of-the-art classical and quantum scalings are respectively O(1.34N ) and O(1.21N ). Our results suggest that specialized optimization hardware with only small numbers of logical spins may be of practical relevance.

</details>


### [241] [Extended-range percolation on lattices with cubic and non-compact neighborhoods in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15441)
*Zhe Zhang,Yuxuan Fan,Yang Yang,Yusong Tu,Zhipeng Xun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用高效单团簇生长算法的蒙特卡洛模拟，系统研究三维扩展范围逾渗模型，获得约100种结构的高精度逾渗阈值，揭示阈值与晶格拓扑（配位数、配位距离等）之间的普适标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展范围逾渗模型中，占据范围（邻域）的扩大如何影响逾渗阈值，以及阈值与晶格局部拓扑结构之间的定量关系尚不清晰。作者希望通过大规模精确模拟，建立阈值与配位数 z、各配位层位点数 z_i 及其距离 r_i 等拓扑量的普适标度律，并连接离散晶格与连续体逾渗。

Method: 采用基于高效单团簇生长算法的蒙特卡洛模拟，研究两类三维扩展范围逾渗模型（立方邻域与非紧致/复杂邻域），覆盖 SC、FCC、BCC 晶格，并分别处理键逾渗与点逾渗。引入三个拓扑指标 z（总配位数）、ξ=Σ z_i r_i²/i 和 ζ=Σ z_i r_i 进行对比分析。

Result: 键逾渗中，zp_c 渐近趋于 Bethe 晶格行为（zp_c~1），有限 z 修正满足 zp_c−1~a₁z^(−x)，x=(d−1)/d。点逾渗中，zp_c 随 z 非单调变化（先增后减），大 z 下线性拟合得 zp_c~2.5987(1)，并预测对齐立方体的连续体逾渗阈值 η_c=0.324838(12)（由 zp_c=2^d η_c）。对延伸至第五配位层的非紧致邻域，发现 ζ 能有效消除简并，阈值满足 p_c∝(ζ/K)^(−γ)，SC、FCC、BCC 晶格分别得 γ^ζ_SC=0.703(12)、γ^ζ_FCC=0.728(10)、γ^ζ_BCC=0.699(12)，且独立于底层晶格有 p_c∝ζ^(−γ)，γ^ζ=0.719(9)。

Conclusion: 三维扩展范围逾渗阈值可由局部拓扑量精确刻画：键逾渗服从 Bethe 型渐近行为，点逾渗通过配位数可以外推获得连续体逾渗阈值；引入的 ζ 指数能统一非紧致邻域下 SC、FCC、BCC 晶格的阈值标度，呈现晶格无关的幂律律。

Abstract: Using MC simulations based on an efficient single-cluster growth algorithm, we investigate two types of three-dimensional extended-range percolation models, namely cubic and complex (non-compact) neighborhoods. We obtain high-precision percolation thresholds $p_c$ for approximately 100 distinct structures and further explore the relationship between these thresholds and the topology of the lattices, which includes the total coordination number $z$, the number of sites $z_i$ and these sites' distance to the central site $r_i$ of the given neighborhood in the $i$th coordination zone. Both bond and site percolation are analyzed for cubic neighborhoods on the SC lattice. For bond percolation, the asymptotic value of $zp_c$ tends to Bethe-lattice behavior ($zp_c\sim1$), and finite-$z$ correction is found to be consistent with $zp_{c}-1\sim a_{1}z^{-x}$, where $x=(d-1)/d$. For site percolation, the value of $zp_c$ varies non-monotonically, exhibiting an initial increase followed by a decrease with the coordination number $z$. Linear fitting leads to $zp_c\sim2.5987(1)$ for large $z$, predicting a value of continuum percolation threshold of aligned cubes $η_c=0.324838(12)$ using $zp_c=2^{d}η_c$. We then examine site percolation for non-compact neighborhoods extending to the fifth coordination zone on SC, FCC, and BCC lattices. Through a comparison of the three indices of $z$, $ξ=\sum_iz_ir_i^2/i$, and $ζ=\sum_iz_ir_i$, we find that $ζ$ is effective in removing the degeneracy. The results obtained show power-law dependence $p_c\propto(ζ/K)^{-γ}$ with $γ_{\mathrm{SC}}^ζ=0.703(12)$, $γ_{\mathrm{FCC}}^ζ=0.728(10)$, and $γ_{\mathrm{BCC}}^ζ=0.699(12)$ for SC, FCC, and BCC lattices, and $p_c\proptoζ^{-γ}$ with $γ^ζ=0.719(9)$ independent of the underlying lattice. The number $K$ indicates lattice connectivity.

</details>


### [242] [Ergodicity breaking transition beyond qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15546)
*Tanay Pathak,Abhik Kumar Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在零维qudit玩具模型中研究局域希尔伯特空间维度对遍历性破缺相变的影响，通过杂化条件解析得到临界相互作用对局域维度的依赖关系，并用谱关联数值验证，发现临界行为不依赖于微观相互作用结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域希尔伯特空间维度是量子多体系统的基本参数，但其在控制遍历性破缺相变中的作用研究较少，作者希望通过系统的研究填补这一空白。

Method: 作者引入了零维的qudit（d维局域希尔伯特空间）玩具模型，这些模型展现出遍历性破缺相变；利用杂化条件（hybridization condition）解析推导临界相互作用值对局域希尔伯特空间维度的依赖关系；通过谱关联测度（spectral correlation measures）的数值研究验证理论预测；并进一步引入一种不同的高自旋（higher spin）版本模型进行数值检验。

Result: 数值谱关联测度与理论预测一致，证实了这些模型中遍历性破缺相变的存在；高自旋版本模型数值上得到了相同的临界行为，表明所观测到的临界点不依赖于所考虑的微观相互作用结构。

Conclusion: 局域希尔伯特空间维度在遍历性破缺相变中起关键控制作用，且临界行为具有普适性，与微观相互作用细节无关。

Abstract: The local Hilbert space dimension is a fundamental parameter in quantum many-body systems, but its role in controlling the ergodicity-breaking transition remains less explored. We systematically study this question by introducing toy models of qudits in zero dimensions that exhibit an ergodicity-breaking transition. Using the hybridization condition, we analytically obtain the dependence of the critical value of the interactions on the local Hilbert space dimension. Numerical studies of spectral correlation measures show agreement with the theoretical predictions, hence confirming the existence of the ergodicity-breaking transition in these models.
